yadṛcchālābhatuṣṭasya dvandvātītasya caiva hi  |
kurvato matprasādārthaṃ karma saṃsāranāśanam  || 2,11.84 ||
manmanā mannamaskāro madyājī matparāyaṇaḥ  |
māmupaiṣyati yogīśaṃ jñātvā māṃ parameśvaram  || 2,11.85 ||
madbuddhayo māṃ satataṃ bodhayantaḥ parasparam  |
kathayantaśca māṃ nityaṃ mama sāyujyamāpnuyuḥ  || 2,11.86 ||
evaṃ nityābhiyuktānāṃ māyeyaṃ karmasānvagam  |
nāśayāmi tamaḥ kṛtsnaṃ jñānadīpena bhāsvatā  || 2,11.87 ||
madbuddhayo māṃ satataṃ pūjayantīha ye janāḥ  |
teṣāṃ nityābhiyuktānāṃ yogakṣemaṃ vahāmyaham  || 2,11.88 ||
ye'nye ca kāmabhogārthaṃ yajante hyanyadevatāḥ  |
teṣāṃ tadantaṃ vijñeyaṃ devatānugataṃ phalam  || 2,11.89 ||
ye cānyadevatābhaktāḥ pūjayantīha devatāḥ  |
madbhāvanāsamāyuktā mucyante te'pi bhāvataḥ  || 2,11.90 ||
tasmādanīśvarānanyāṃstyaktvā devānaśeṣataḥ  |
māmeva saṃśrayedīśaṃ sa yāti paramaṃ padam  || 2,11.91 ||
tyaktvā putrādiṣu snehaṃ niḥ śoko niṣparigrahaḥ  |
yajeccāmaraṇālliṅge viraktaḥ parameśvaram  || 2,11.92 ||
ye'rcayanti sadā liṅgaṃ tyaktvā bhogānaśeṣataḥ  |
ekena janmanā teṣāṃ dadāmi paramaiśvaram  || 2,11.93 ||
parānandātmakaṃ liṅgaṃ kevalaṃ sannirañjanam  |
jñānātmakaṃ sarvagataṃ yogināṃ hṛdi saṃsthitam  || 2,11.94 ||
ye cānye niyatā bhaktā bhāvayitvā vidhānataḥ  |
yatra kvacana talliṅgamarcayanti maheśvaram  || 2,11.95 ||
jale vā vahnimadhye vāvyomni sūrye'thavānyataḥ  |
ratnādau bhāvayitveśamarcayelliṅgamaiśvaram  || 2,11.96 ||
sarvaṃ liṅgamayaṃ hyetatsarvaṃ liṅge pratiṣṭhitam  |
tasmālliṅge'rcayedīśaṃ yatra kvacana śāśvatam  || 2,11.97 ||
agnau kriyāvatāmapsu vyomni sūrye manīṣiṇām  |
kāṣṭhādiṣveva mūrkhāṇāṃ hṛdi liṅgantuyoginām  || 2,11.98 ||
yadyanutpannavijñāno viraktaḥ prītisaṃyutaḥ  |
yāvajjīvaṃ japedyuktaḥ praṇavaṃ brahmaṇo vapuḥ  || 2,11.99 ||
athavā śatarudrīyaṃ japedāmaraṇāddvijaḥ  |
ekākī yatacittātmā sa yāti paramaṃ padam  || 2,11.100 ||
vasedvāmaraṇādvipro vārāṇasyāṃ samāhitaḥ  |
so'pīśvaraprasādena yāti tatparamaṃ padam  || 2,11.101 ||
tatrotkramaṇakāle hi sarveṣāmeva dehinām  |
dadāti tatparaṃ jñānaṃ yena mucyeta bandhanāt || 2,11.102 ||
varṇāśramavidhiṃ kṛtsnaṃ kurvāṇo matparāyaṇaḥ  |
tenaiva janmanā jñānaṃ labdhvā yāti śivaṃ padam  || 2,11.103 ||
ye'pi tatra vasantīha nīcā vā pāpayonayaḥ  |
sarve taranti saṃsāramīśvarānugrahāddvijāḥ  || 2,11.104 ||
kintu vighnā bhaviṣyanti pāpopahatacetasām  |
dharmaṃ samāśrayettasmānmuktaye niyataṃ dvijāḥ  || 2,11.105 ||
etadrahasyaṃ vedānāṃ na deyaṃ yasya kasya cit |
dhārmikāyaiva dātavyaṃ bhaktāya brahmacāriṇe  || 2,11.106 ||
vyāsa uvāca
ityetaduktvā bhagavānātmayogamanuttamam  |
vyājahāra samāsīnaṃ nārāyaṇamanāmayam  || 2,11.107 ||
mayaitadbhāṣitaṃ jñānaṃ hitārthaṃ brahmavādinām  |
dātavyaṃ śāntacittebhyaḥ śiṣyebhyo bhavatā śivam  || 2,11.108 ||
uktvaivamatha yogīndrānabravīdbhagavānajaḥ  |
hitāya sarvabhaktānāṃ dvijātīnāṃ dvijottamāḥ  || 2,11.109 ||
bhavanto'pi hi majjñānaṃ śiṣyāṇāṃ vidhipūrvakam  |
upadekṣyanti bhaktānāṃ sarveṣāṃ vacanānmama  || 2,11.110 ||
ayaṃ nārāyaṇo yo'hamīśvaro nātra saṃśayaḥ  |
nāntaraṃ ye prapaśyanti teṣāṃ deyamidaṃ param  || 2,11.111 ||
mamaiṣā paramā mūrtirnārāyaṇasamāhvayā  |
sarvabhūtātmabhūtasthā śāntā cākṣarasaṃjñitā  || 2,11.112 ||
ye tvanyathā prapaśyanti loke bhedadṛśo janāḥ  |
na te māṃ saṃprapaśyanti jāyante ca punaḥ punaḥ  || 2,11.113 ||
ye tvimaṃ viṣṇumavyaktaṃ māṃ vā devaṃ maheśvaram  |
ekībhāvena paśyanti na teṣāṃ punarudbhavaḥ  || 2,11.114 ||
tasmādanādinidhanaṃ viṣṇumātmānamavyayam  |
māmeva saṃprapaśyadhvaṃ pūjayadhvaṃ tathaiva hi  || 2,11.115 ||
ye'nyathā māṃ prapaśyanti matvemaṃ devatāntaram  |
te yānti narakān ghorānnāhaṃ teṣuvyavasthitaḥ  || 2,11.116 ||
mūrkhaṃ vā paṇḍitaṃ vāpi brāhmaṇaṃ vā madāśrayam  |
mocayāmi śvapākaṃ vā na nārāyaṇanindakam  || 2,11.117 ||
tasmādeṣa mahāyogī madbhaktaiḥ puruṣottamaḥ  |
arcanīyo namaskāryo matprītijananāya hi  || 2,11.118 ||
evamuktvā samāliṅgya vāsudevaṃ pinākadhṛk |
antarhito'bhavatteṣāṃ sarveṣāmeva paśyatām  || 2,11.119 ||
nārāyaṇo'pi bhagavāṃstāpasaṃ veṣamuttamam  |
jagrāha yoginaḥ sarvāṃstyaktvā vai paramaṃ vapuḥ  || 2,11.120 ||
jñātaṃ bhavadbhiramalaṃ prasādātparameṣṭhinaḥ  |
sākṣādeva maheśasya jñānaṃ saṃsāranāśanam  || 2,11.121 ||
gacchadhvaṃ vijvarāḥ sarve vijñānaṃ parameṣṭhinaḥ  |
pravartayadhvaṃ śiṣyebhyo dhārmikebhyo munīśvarāḥ  || 2,11.122 ||
idaṃ bhaktāya śāntāya dhārmikāyāhitāgnaye  |
vijñānamaiśvaraṃ deyaṃ brāhmaṇāya viśeṣataḥ  || 2,11.123 ||
evamuktvā sa viśvātmā yogināṃ yogavittamaḥ  |
nārāyaṇo mahāyogī jagāmādarśanaṃ svayam  || 2,11.124 ||
te'pi devādideveśaṃ namaskṛtya maheśvaram  |
nārāyaṇaṃ ca bhūtādiṃ svāni sthānāni bhejire  || 2,11.125 ||
sanatkumāro bhagavān saṃvartāya mahāmuniḥ  |
dattavānaiśvaraṃ jñānaṃ so'pi satyavratāya tu  || 2,11.126 ||
sanandano'pi yogīndraḥ pulahāya maharṣaye  |
pradadau gautamāyātha pulaho'pi prajāpatiḥ  || 2,11.127 ||
aṅgirā vedaviduṣe bharadvājāya dattavān  |
jaigīṣavyāya kapilastathā pañcaśikhāya ca  || 2,11.128 ||
parāśaro'pi sanakātpitā me sarvatattvadṛk |
lebhetatparamaṃ jñānaṃ tasmādvālmīkirāptavān  || 2,11.129 ||
mamovāca purā devaḥ satīdehabhavāṅgajaḥ  |
vāmadevo mahāyogī rudraḥ kila pinākadhṛk || 2,11.130 ||
nārāyaṇo'pi bhagavān devakītanayo hariḥ  |
arjunāya svayaṃ sākṣātdattavānidamuttamam  || 2,11.131 ||
yadahaṃ labdhavān rudrādvāmadevādanuttamam  |
viśeṣādgiriśe bhaktistasmādārabhya me'bhavat || 2,11.132 ||
śaraṇyaṃ śaraṇaṃ rudraṃ prapanno'haṃ viśeṣataḥ  |
bhūteśaṃ giraśaṃ sthāṇuṃ devadevaṃ triśūlinam  || 2,11.133 ||
bhavanto'pi hi taṃ devaṃ śaṃbhuṃ govṛṣavāhanam  |
prapadyadhvaṃ sapatnīkāḥ saputrāḥ śaraṇaṃ śivam  || 2,11.134 ||
vartadhvaṃ tatprasādena karmayogena śaṅkaram  |
pūjayadhvaṃ mahādevaṃ gopatiṃ bhūtibhūṣaṇam  || 2,11.135 ||
evamukte'tha munayaḥ śaunakādyā maheśvaram  |
praṇemuḥ śāśvataṃ sthāṇuṃ vyāsaṃ satyavatīsutam  || 2,11.136 ||
abruvan hṛṣṭamanasaḥ kṛṣṇadvaipāyanaṃ prabhum  |
sākṣādeva hṛṣīkeśaṃ sarvalokamaheśvaram  || 2,11.137 ||
bhavatprasādādacalā śaraṇye govṛṣadhvaje  |
idānīṃ jāyate bhaktiryā devairapi durlabhā  || 2,11.138 ||
kathayasva muniśreṣṭha karmayogamanuttamam  |
yenāsau bhagavānīśaḥ samārādhyo mumukṣubhiḥ  || 2,11.139 ||
tvatsaṃnidhāveṣa sūtaḥ śṛṇotu bhagavadvacaḥ  |
tadvadākhilalokānāṃ rakṣaṇaṃ dharmasaṃgraham  || 2,11.140 ||
yaduktaṃ devadevena viṣṇunā kūrmarūpiṇā  |
pṛṣṭena munibhiḥ pūrvaṃ śakreṇāmṛtamanthane  || 2,11.141 ||
śrutvā satyavatīsūnuḥ karmayogaṃ sanātanam  |
munīnāṃ bhāṣitaṃ kṛṣṇaḥ provāca susamāhitaḥ  || 2,11.142 ||
ya imaṃ paṭhate nityaṃ saṃvādaṃ kṛttivāsasaḥ  |
sanatkumārapramukhaiḥ sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate  || 2,11.143 ||
śrāvayedvā dvijān śuddhān brahmacaryaparāyaṇān  |
yo vā vicārayedarthaṃ sa yāti paramāṃ gatim  || 2,11.144 ||
yaścaitacchṛṇuyānnityaṃ bhaktiyukto dṛḍhavrataḥ  |
sarvapāpavinirmukto brahmaloke mahīyate  || 2,11.145 ||
tasmātsarvaprayatnena paṭhitavyo manīṣibhiḥ  |
śrotavyaścātha mantavyo viśeṣādbrāhmaṇaiḥ sadā  || 2,11.146 ||

iti śrīkūrmapurāṇe ṣaṭsāhastryāṃ saṃhitāyāmuparivibhāge (īśvaragītāsu) ekādaśo'dhyāyaḥ


_____________________________________________________________


vyāsa uvāca
śṛṇudhvamṛṣayaḥ sarve vakṣyamāṇaṃ sanātanam  |
karmayogaṃ brāhmaṇānāmātyantikaphalapradam  || 2,12.1 ||
āmnāyasiddhamakhilaṃ brihmaṇānupradarśitam  |
ṛṣīṇāṃ śṛṇvatāṃ pūrvaṃ manurāha prajāpatiḥ  || 2,12.2 ||
sarvapāpaharaṃ puṇyamṛṣisaṅghairniṣevitam  |
samāhitadhiyo yūyaṃ śṛṇudhvaṃ gadato mama  || 2,12.3 ||
kṛtopanayano vedānadhīyīta dvijottamāḥ  |
garbhāṣṭame'ṣṭame vābde svasūtroktavidhānataḥ  || 2,12.4 ||
daṇḍī ca mekhalī sūtrī kṛṣṇājinadharo muniḥ  |
bhikṣāhāro guruhito vīkṣamāṇo gururmukham  || 2,12.5 ||
kārpāsamupavītārthaṃ nirmitaṃ brahmaṇā purā  |
brāhmaṇānāṃ trivitsūtraṃ kauśaṃ vā vastrameva vā  || 2,12.6 ||
sadopavītī caiva syātsadā baddhaśikho dvijaḥ  |
anyathā yatkṛtaṃ karma tadbhavatyayathākṛtam  || 2,12.7 ||
vasedavikṛtaṃ vāsaḥ kārpāsaṃ vā kaṣāyakam  |
tadeva paridhānīyaṃ śuklamacchidramuttamam  || 2,12.8 ||
uttaraṃ tu samākhyātaṃ vāsaḥ kṛṣṇājinaṃ śubham  |
abhāve gavyamajinaṃ rauravaṃ vā vidhīyate  || 2,12.9 ||
uddhṛtya dakṣiṇaṃ bāhuṃ savye bāhau samarpitam  |
upavītaṃ bhavennityaṃ nivītaṃ kaṇṭhasajjane  || 2,12.10 ||
savyaṃ bāhuṃ samuddhṛtya dakṣiṇe tu dhṛtaṃ dvijāḥ  |
prācīnāvītamityuktaṃ pitrye karmaṇi yojayet || 2,12.11 ||
agnyagāre gavāṃ goṣṭhe home japye tathaiva ca  |
svādhyāye bhojane nityaṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ ca sannidhau  || 2,12.12 ||
upāsane gurūṇāṃ ca saṃdhyayoḥ sādhusaṃgame  |
upavītī bhavennityaṃ vidhireṣa sanātanaḥ  || 2,12.13 ||
mauñjī trivṛtsamā ślakṣaṇā kāryā viprasya mekhalā  |
muñjābhāve kuśenāhurgranthinaikena vā tribhiḥ  || 2,12.14 ||
dhārayedbailvapālāśau daṇḍau keśāntakau dvijaḥ  |
yajñārhavṛkṣajaṃ vātha saumyamavraṇameva ca  || 2,12.15 ||
sāyaṃ prātardvijaḥ saṃdhyāmupāsīta samāhitaḥ  |
kāmāllobhādbhayānmohāttyaktena patito bhavet || 2,12.16 ||
agnikāryaṃ tataḥ kuryātsāyaṃ prātaḥ prasannadhīḥ  |
snātvā saṃtarpayeddevānṛṣīn pitṛgaṇāṃstathā  || 2,12.17 ||
devatābhyarcanaṃ kuryātpuṣpaiḥ patreṇa vāmbubhiḥ  |
abhivādanaśīlaḥ syānnityaṃ vṛddheṣu dharmataḥ  || 2,12.18 ||
asāvahaṃ bho nāmeti samyakpraṇatipūrvakam  |
āyurārogyasiddhyarthaṃ tandrādiparivarjitaḥ  || 2,12.19 ||
āyuṣṇān bhava saumyeti vācyo vipro'bhivādane  |
akāraścāsya nāmno'nte vācyaḥ pūrvākṣaraḥ plutaḥ  || 2,12.20 ||
na kuryādyo'bhivādasya dvijaḥ pratyabhivādanam  |
nābhivādyaḥ sa viduṣā yathā śūdrastathaiva saḥ  || 2,12.21 ||
vyatyastapāṇinā kāryamupasaṃgrahaṇaṃ guroḥ  |
savyena savyaḥ spraṣṭavyo dakṣiṇena tu dakṣiṇaḥ  || 2,12.22 ||
laukikaṃ vaidikaṃ cāpi tathādhyātmikameva vā  |
ādadīta yato jñānaṃ taṃ pūrvamabhivādayet || 2,12.23 ||
nodakaṃ dhārayedbhaikṣaṃ puṣpāṇi samidhastathā  |
evaṃvidhāni cānyāni na daivādyeṣu karmasu  || 2,12.24 ||
brāhmaṇaṃ kuśalaṃ pṛcchetkṣatrabandhumanāmayam  |
vaiśyaṃ kṣemaṃ samāgamya śūdramārogyameva tu  || 2,12.25 ||
upādhyāyaḥ pitā jyeṣṭho bhrātā caiva mahīpatiḥ  |
mātulaḥ śvaśurastrātā mātāmahapitāmahau  |
varṇajyeṣṭhaḥ pitṛvyaśca puṃso'tra guravaḥ smṛtāḥ  || 2,12.26 ||
mātā mātāmahī gurvo piturmātuśca sodarāḥ  |
śvaśrūḥ pitāmahījyeṣṭhā dhātrī ca guravaḥ striyaḥ  || 2,12.27 ||
ityukto guruvargo'yaṃ mātṛtaḥ pitṛto dvijāḥ  |
anuvartanameteṣāṃ manovākkāyakarmabhiḥ  || 2,12.28 ||
guruṃ dṛṣṭvā samuttiṣṭhedabhivādya kṛtāñjaliḥ  |
naitairupaviśetsārdhaṃ vivadennātmakāraṇāt || 2,12.29 ||
jīvitārthamapi dveṣādgurubhirnaiva bhāṣaṇam  |
udito'pi guṇairanyairgurudveṣī patatyadhaḥ  || 2,12.30 ||
gurūṇāmapi sarveṣāṃ pūjyāḥ pañca viśeṣataḥ  |
teṣāmādyāstrayaḥ śreṣṭhāsteṣāṃ mātā supūjitā  || 2,12.31 ||
yo bhāvayati yā sūte yena vidyopadiśyate  |
jyeṣṭho bhrātā ca bhartā ca pañcaite guravaḥ smṛtāḥ  || 2,12.32 ||
ātmanaḥ sarvayatnena prāṇatyāgena vā punaḥ  |
pūjanīyā viśeṣeṇa pañcaite bhūtimicchatā  || 2,12.33 ||
yāvatpitā ca mātā ca dvāvetau nirvikāriṇau  |
tāvatsarvaṃ parityajya putraḥ syāttatparāyaṇaḥ  || 2,12.34 ||
pitā mātā ca suprītau syātāṃ putraguṇairyadi  |
sa putraḥ sakalaṃ dharmamāpnuyāttena karmaṇā  || 2,12.35 ||
nāsti mātṛsamaṃ daivaṃ nāsti pitṛsamo guruḥ  |
tayoḥ pratyupakāro'pi na kathañcana vidyate  || 2,12.36 ||
tayornityaṃ priyaṃ kuryātkarmaṇā manasā girā  |
na tābhyāmananujñāto dharmamanyaṃ samācaret || 2,12.37 ||
varjayitvā muktiphalaṃ nityaṃ naimittikaṃ tathā  |
dharmasāraḥ samuddiṣṭaḥ pretyānantaphalapradaḥ  || 2,12.38 ||
samyagārādhya vaktāraṃ visṛṣṭastadanujñayā  |
śiṣyo vidyāphalaṃ bhuṅkte pretya cāpadyate divi  || 2,12.39 ||
yo bhrātaraṃ pitṛsamaṃ jyeṣṭhaṃ mūrkho'vamanyate  |
tena doṣeṇa sa pretya nirayaṃ ghoramṛcchati  || 2,12.40 ||
puṃsā vartmaniviṣṭena pūjyo bhartā tu sarvadā  |
yāti dātari loke'sminupakārāddhi gauravam  || 2,12.41 ||
yenarā bhartṛpiṇḍārthaṃ svān prāṇān saṃtyajanti hi  |
teṣāmathākṣayāṃllokān provāca bhagavānmanuḥ  || 2,12.42 ||
mātulāṃśca pitṛvyāṃśca śvaśurānṛtvijo gurūn  |
asāvahamiti brūyuḥ pratyutthāya yavīyasaḥ  || 2,12.43 ||
avācyo dīkṣito nāmnā yavīyānapi yo bhavet |
bhobhavatpūrvakaṃ tvenamabhibhāṣeta dharmavit || 2,12.44 ||
abhivādyāśca pūjyaśca śirasā vandya eva ca  |
brāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyādyaiśca śrīkāmaiḥ sādaraṃ sadā  || 2,12.45 ||
nābhivādyāstu vipreṇa kṣatriyādyāḥ kathañcana  |
jñānakarmaguṇopetā yadyapyete bahuśrutāḥ  || 2,12.46 ||
brāhmaṇaḥ sarvavarṇānāṃ svasti kuryāditi sthitiḥ  |
savarṇeṣu savarṇānāṃ kāryamevābhivādanam  || 2,12.47 ||
gururagnirdvijātīnāṃ varṇānāṃ brāhmaṇo guruḥ  |
patireko guruḥ strīṇāṃ sarvatrābhyāgato guruḥ  || 2,12.48 ||
vidyā karma vayo bandhurvittaṃ bhavati pañcamam  |
mānyasthānāni pañcāhuḥ pūrvaṃ pūrvaṃ gurūttarāt || 2,12.49 ||
pañcānāṃ triṣu varṇeṣu bhūyāṃsi balavanti ca  |
yatra syuḥ so'tra mānārhaḥ śūdro'pi daśamīṃ gataḥ  || 2,12.50 ||
panthā deyo brāhmaṇāya striyai rājñe hyacakṣuṣe  |
vṛddhāya bhārabugnāya rogiṇe durbalāya ca  || 2,12.51 ||
bhikṣāmāhṛtya śiṣṭānāṃ gṛhebhyaḥ prayato'nvaham  |
nivedya gurave'śnīyādvāgyatastadanujñayā  || 2,12.52 ||
bhavatpūrvaṃ caredbhaikṣyamupanīto dvijottamaḥ  |
bhavanmadhyaṃ tu rājanyo vaiśyastu bhavaduttaram  || 2,12.53 ||
mātaraṃ vā svasāraṃ vā māturvā bhaginīṃ nijām  |
bhikṣeta bhikṣāṃ prathamaṃ yā cainaṃ na vimānayet || 2,12.54 ||
sajātīyagṛheṣveva sārvavarṇikameva vā  |
bhaikṣyasya caraṇaṃ proktaṃ patitādiṣu varjitam  || 2,12.55 ||
vedayajñairahīnānāṃ praśastānāṃ svakarmasu  |
brahmacaryāharedbhaikṣaṃ gṛhebhyaḥ prayato'nvaham  || 2,12.56 ||
guroḥ kule na bhikṣeta na jñātikulabandhuṣu  |
alābhe tvanyagehānāṃ pūrvaṃ pūrvaṃ vivarjayet || 2,12.57 ||
sarvaṃ vā vicaredgrāmaṃ pūrvoktānāmasaṃbhave  |
niyamya prayato vācaṃ diśastvanavalokayan  || 2,12.58 ||
samāhṛtya tu tadbhaikṣaṃ yāvadarthamamāyayā  |
bhuñjīta prayato nityaṃ vāgyato'nanyamānasaḥ  || 2,12.59 ||
bhaikṣyeṇa vartayennityaṃ naikānnādī bhavedvratī  |
bhaikṣyeṇa vratino vṛttirupavāsasamā smṛtā  || 2,12.60 ||
pūjayedaśanaṃ nityamadyāccaitadakutsayan  |
dṛṣṭvā hṛṣyetprasīdecca pratinandecca sarvaśaḥ  || 2,12.61 ||
anārogyamanāyuṣyamasvargyaṃ cātibhojanam  |
apuṇyaṃ lokavidviṣṭaṃ tasmāttatparivarjayet || 2,12.62 ||
prāṅmukho'nnāni bhuñjīta sūryābhimukha eva vā  |
nādyādudaṅmukho nityaṃ vidhireṣa sanātanaḥ  || 2,12.63 ||
prakṣālya pāṇipādau ca bhuñjāno dvirupaspṛśet |
śucau deśe samāsīno bhuktvā ca dvirupaspṛśet || 2,12.64 ||

itī śrīkūrmapurāṇe ṣaṭsāhastryāṃ saṃhitāyāmuparivibhāge dvādaśo'dhyāyaḥ


_____________________________________________________________


vyāsa uvāca
bhuktvā pītvā ca suptvā ca snātvā rathyopasarpaṇe  |
oṣṭhāvalamokau spṛṣṭvā vāso viparidhāya ca  || 2,13.1 ||
retomūtrapurīṣāṇāmutsarge'yuktabhāṣaṇe  |
ṣṭhīvitvādhyayanārambhe kāsaśvāsāgame tathā  || 2,13.2 ||
catvaraṃ vā śmaśānaṃ vā samākramya dvijottamaḥ  |
saṃdhyayorubhayostadvadācānto'pyācametpunaḥ  || 2,13.3 ||
caṇḍālamlecchasaṃbhāṣe strīśūdrocchiṣṭabhāṣaṇe  |
ucchiṣṭaṃ puruṣaṃ spṛṣṭvā bhojyaṃ cāpi tathāvidham  |
ācāmedaśrupāte vā lohitasya tathaiva ca  || 2,13.4 ||
bhojane saṃdhyayoḥ snātvā pītvā mūtrapurīṣayoḥ  |
ācānto'pyācametsuptvā sakṛtsakṛdathānyataḥ  || 2,13.5 ||
agnergavāmathālambhe spṛṣṭvā prayatameva vā  |
strīṇāmathātmanaḥ sparśe nīvīṃ vā paridhāya ca  || 2,13.6 ||
upaspṛśejjalaṃ vārdraṃ tṛṇaṃ vā bhūmimeva vā  |
keśānāṃ cātmanaḥ sparśe vāsaso'kṣālitasya ca  || 2,13.7 ||
anuṣṇābhiraphenābiraduṣṭābhiśca dharmataḥ  |
śaucepsuḥ sarvadācāmedāsīnaḥ prāgudaṅmukhaḥ  || 2,13.8 ||
śiraḥ prāvṛtya kaṇṭhaṃ vā muktakacchasikho'pi vā  |
akṛtvā pādayoḥ śaucamācānto'pyaśucirbhavet || 2,13.9 ||
sopānatko jalastho vā noṣṇīṣī vācamedbudhaḥ  |
na caiva varṣadhārābhirna tiṣṭhannoddhṛtodakaiḥ  || 2,13.10 ||
naikahastārpitajalairvinā sūtreṇa vā punaḥ  |
na pādukāsanastho vā bahirjānurathāpi vā  || 2,13.11 ||
na jalpanna hasan prekṣan śayānaḥ prahva eva ca  |
nāvīkṣitābhiḥ phenādyairupetābhirathāpi vā  || 2,13.12 ||
śūdrāśucikaronmuktairna kṣārābhistathaiva ca  |
na caivāṅgulibhiḥ śabdaṃ na kurvannānyamānasaḥ  || 2,13.13 ||
na varṇarasaduṣṭābhirna caiva pradarodakaiḥ  |
na pāṇikṣubhitābhirvā na bahiṣkakṣa eva vā  || 2,13.14 ||
hṛdgābhiḥ pūyate vipraḥ kaṇṭhyābhiḥ kṣatriyaḥ śuciḥ  |
prāśitābhistathāvaiśyaḥ strīśūdrau sparśato'ntataḥ  || 2,13.15 ||
aṅguṣṭhamūlāntarato rekhāyāṃ brāhmamucyate  |
antarāṅguṣṭhadeśinyo pitṝṇāṃ tīrthamuttamam  || 2,13.16 ||
kaniṣṭhāmūlataḥ paścātprājāpatyaṃ pracakṣate  |
aṅgulyagre smṛtaṃ daivaṃ tadevārṣaṃ prakīrtitam  || 2,13.17 ||
mūle vā daivamārṣaṃ syādāgneyaṃ madhyataḥ smṛtaṃ  |
tadeva saumikaṃ tīrthametajjñātvā na muhyati  || 2,13.18 ||
brāhmeṇaiva tu tīrthena dvijo nityamupaspṛśet |
kāyena vātha daivena tu pitryeṇa vai dvijāḥ  || 2,13.19 ||
triḥ prāśnīyādapaḥ pūrvaṃ brāhmaṇaḥ prayatastataḥ  |
saṃmṛjyāṅguṣṭhamūlena mukhaṃ vai samupaspṛśet || 2,13.20 ||
aṅguṣṭhānāmikābhyāṃ tu spṛśennetradvayaṃ tataḥ  |
tarjanyaṅguṣṭhayogena spṛśennāsāpṛṭadvayam  || 2,13.21 ||
kaniṣṭhāṅguṣṭhayogena śravaṇe samupaspṛśet |
sarvāsāmatha yogena hṛdayaṃ tu talena vā  |
saṃspṛśedvā śirastadvadaṅguṣṭhenāthavā dvayam  || 2,13.22 ||
triḥ prāśnīyādyadambhastu suprītāstena devatāḥ  |
brahmā viṣṇurmaheśaśca bhavantītyanuśuśrumaḥ  || 2,13.23 ||
gaṅgā ca yamunā caiva prīyete parimārjanāt |
saṃspṛṣṭayorlocanayoḥ prīyete śaśibhāskarau  || 2,13.24 ||
nāsatyadastrau prīyete spṛṣṭe nāsāpuṭadvaye  |
karṇayoḥ spṛṣṭayostadvatprīyete cānilānalau  || 2,13.25 ||
saṃspṛṣṭe hṛdaye cāsya prīyante sarvadevatāḥ  |
mūrdhni saṃsparśanādekaḥ prītaḥ sa puruṣo bhavet || 2,13.26 ||
nocchiṣṭaṃ kurvate mukhyā vipruṣo'ṅgaṃ nayanti yāḥ  |
dantavaddantalagneṣu jihvāsparśe'śucirbhavet || 2,13.27 ||
spṛśānti bindavaḥ pādau ya ācāmayataḥ parān  |
bhūmigaiste samā jñeyā na tairaprayato bhavet || 2,13.28 ||
maduparke ca some ca tāmbūlasya ca bhakṣaṇe  |
phalamūle cekṣudaṇḍe na doṣaṃ prāha ve manuḥ  || 2,13.29 ||
pracaraṃścānnapāneṣu dravyahasto bhavennaraḥ  |
bhūmau nikṣipya taddravyamācamyābhyukṣayettu tat || 2,13.30 ||
taijasaṃ vai samādāya yadyucchiṣṭo bhaveddvijaḥ  |
bhūmau nikṣipya taddravyamācamyābhyukṣayettu tat || 2,13.31 ||
yadyamatraṃ samādāya bhaveduccheṣaṇānvitaḥ  |
anidhāyaiva taddravyamācāntaḥ śucitāmiyāt |
vastrādiṣu vikalpaḥ syāttatsaṃspṛṣṭvācamediha  || 2,13.32 ||
araṇye'nudake rātrau cauravyāghrākule pathi  |
kṛtvā mūtraṃ purīṣaṃ vā dravyahasto na duṣyati  || 2,13.33 ||
nidhāya dakṣiṇe karṇe brahmasūtramudaṅmukhaḥ  |
ahni kuryācchakṛnmūtraṃ rātrau ceddakṣiṇāmukhaḥ  || 2,13.34 ||
antardhāya mahīṃ kāṣṭhaiḥ patrairloṣṭhatṛṇena vā  |
prāvṛtya ca śiraḥ kuryādviṇmūtrasya visarjanam  || 2,13.35 ||
chāyākūpanadīgoṣṭhacaityāmbhaḥ pathi bhasmasu  |
agnau caiva śmaśāne ca viṇmūtre na samācaret || 2,13.36 ||
na gomaye na kṛṣṭe vā mahāvṛkṣe na śāḍvale  |
na tiṣṭhan vā na nirvāsā na ca parvatamastake  || 2,13.37 ||
na jīrṇadevāyatane na valmīke kadācana  |
na sasattveṣu garteṣu na gacchan vā samācaret || 2,13.38 ||
tuṣāṅgārakapāleṣu rājamārge tathaiva ca  |
na kṣetre na vile vāpi na tīrthe na catuṣpathe  || 2,13.39 ||
nodyānodasamīpe vā noṣare na parāśucau  |
na sopānatpāduko vā chatrī vā nāntarikṣake  || 2,13.40 ||
na caivābhimukhe strīṇāṃ gurubrāhmaṇayorgavām  |
na devadevālayayorapāmapi kadācana  || 2,13.41 ||
na jyotīṃṣi nirīkṣanvānasaṃdhyābhimukho'pivā  |
pratyādityaṃ pratyanalaṃ pratisomaṃ tathaiva ca  || 2,13.42 ||
āhṛtya mṛttikāṃ kūlāllepagandhāpakarṣaṇam  |
kuryādatandritaḥ śaucaṃ viśuddhairuddhṛtodakaiḥ  || 2,13.43 ||
nāharenmṛttikāṃ vipraḥ pāṃśulānna ca kardamāt |
na mārgānnoṣarāddeśācchaucaśiṣṭāṃ parasya ca  || 2,13.44 ||
na devāyatanātkūpādgrāmānna ca jalāttathā  |
upaspṛśettato nityaṃ pūrvoktena vidhānataḥ  || 2,13.45 ||

iti śrīkūrmapurāṇe ṣaṭsāhastryāṃ saṃhitāyāmuparivibhāge trayodaśo'dhyāyaḥ


_____________________________________________________________


vyāsa uvāca
evaṃ daṇḍādibhiryuktaḥ śaucācārasamanvitaḥ  |
āhūto'dhyayanaṃ kuryādvīkṣamāṇo gurormukham  || 2,14.1 ||
nityamudyatapāṇiḥ syātsādhvācāraḥ susaṃyataḥ  |
āsyatāmiti coktaḥ sannāsītābhimukhaṃ guroḥ  || 2,14.2 ||
pratiśravaṇasaṃbhāṣe śayāno na samācaret |
nāsīno na ca bhuñjāno na tiṣṭhanna parāṅmukhaḥ  || 2,14.3 ||
nīcaṃ śayyāsanaṃ cāsya sarvadā gurusannidhau  |
gurostu cakṣurviṣaye na yatheṣṭāsano bhavet || 2,14.4 ||
nodāharedasya nāma parokṣamapi kevalam  |
na caivāsyānukurvota gatibhāṣaṇaceṣṭitam  || 2,14.5 ||
guroryatra parīvādo nindā cāpi pravartate  |
karṇaiṃ tatra pidhātavyau gantavyaṃ vā tato'nyataḥ  || 2,14.6 ||
dūrastho nārcayedenaṃ na kruddho nāntike striyāḥ  |
na caivāsyottaraṃ brūyātsthito nāsīta sannidhau  || 2,14.7 ||
udakumbhaṃ kuśān puṣpaṃ samidho'syāharetsadā  |
mārjanaṃ lepanaṃ nityamaṅgānāṃ vai samācaret || 2,14.8 ||
nāsya nirmālyaśayanaṃ pādukopānahāvapi  |
ākramedāsanaṃ cāsya chāyādīn vā kadācana  || 2,14.9 ||
sādhayeddantakāṣṭhādīn labdhaṃ cāsmai nivedayet |
anāpṛcchya na gantavyaṃ bhavetpriyahite rataḥ  || 2,14.10 ||
na pādau sārayedasya saṃnidhāne kadācana  |
jṛmbhitaṃ hasitaṃ caiva kaṇṭhaprāvaraṇaṃ tathā  |
varjayetsannidhau nityamavasphocanameva ca  || 2,14.11 ||
yathākālamadhīyīta yāvanna vimanā guruḥ  |
āsītādho guroḥ kūrce phalake vā samāhitaḥ  || 2,14.12 ||
āsane śayane yāne naiva tiṣṭhetkadācana  |
dhāvantamanudhāveta gacchantamanugacchati  || 2,14.13 ||
go'śvoṣṭrayānaprāsādaprastareṣu kaṭeṣu ca  |
āsīta guruṇā sārdhaṃ śilāphalakanauṣu ca  || 2,14.14 ||
jitendriyaḥ syātsatataṃ vaśyātmākrodhanaḥ śuciḥ  |
prayuñjīta sadā vācaṃ madhurāṃ hitabhāṣiṇīm  || 2,14.15 ||
gandhamālyaṃ rasaṃ kalyāṃ śuktaṃ prāṇivihiṃsanam  |
abhyaṅgaṃ cāñcanopānacchatradhāraṇameva ca  || 2,14.16 ||
kāmaṃ lobhaṃ bhayaṃ nidrāṃ gītavāditranartanam  |
ātarjanaṃ parīvādaṃ strīprekṣālambhanaṃ tathā  |
paropaghātaṃ paiśunyaṃ prayatnena vivarjayet || 2,14.17 ||
udakumbhaṃ sumanaso gośakṛnmṛttikāṃ kuśān  |
āharedyāvadarthāni bhaikṣyaṃ cāharahaścaret || 2,14.18 ||
kṛtaṃ ca lavaṇaṃ sarvaṃ varjyaṃ paryuṣitaṃ ca yat |
anṛtyadarśo satataṃ bhavedgītādiniḥ spṛhaḥ  || 2,14.19 ||
nādityaṃ vai samīkṣeta na careddantadhāvanam  |
ekāntamaśucistrībhiḥ śūdrāntyairabhibhāṣaṇam  || 2,14.20 ||
gurūcchiṣṭaṃ bheṣajārthaṃ prayuñjīta na kāmataḥ  |
kalāpakarṣaṇasnānaṃ nācareddhi kadācana  || 2,14.21 ||
na kuryānmānasaṃ vipro gurostyāge kadācana  |
mohādvā yadi vā lobhāttyaktena patito bhavet || 2,14.22 ||
laukikaṃ vaidikaṃ cāpi tathādhyātmikameva ca  |
ādadīta yato jñānaṃ na taṃ druhyetkadācana  || 2,14.23 ||
gurorapyavaliptasya kāryākāryamajānataḥ  |
utpathapratipannasya manustyāgaṃ samabravīt || 2,14.24 ||
gurorgurau sannihite guruvadbhaktimācaret |
na cātisṛṣṭo guruṇā svān gurūnabivādayet || 2,14.25 ||
vidyāguruṣvetadeva nityā vṛttiḥ svayoniṣu  |
pratiṣedhatsu cādharmāddhitaṃ copadiśatsvapi  || 2,14.26 ||
śreyaḥsu guruvadvṛttiṃ nityameva samācaret |
guruputreṣu dāreṣu guroścaiva svabandhuṣu  || 2,14.27 ||
bālaḥ samānajanmā vā śiṣyo vā yajñakarmaṇi  |
adhyāpayan gurusuto guruvanmānamarhati  || 2,14.28 ||
utsādanaṃ vai gātrāṇāṃ snāpanocchiṣṭabhojane  |
na kuryādguruputrasya pādayoḥ śaucameva ca  || 2,14.29 ||
guruvatparipūjyāstu savarṇā guruyoṣitaḥ  |
asavarṇāstu saṃpūjyāḥ pratyutthānābhivādanaiḥ  || 2,14.30 ||
abhyañjanaṃ snāpanaṃ ca gātrotsādanameva ca  |
gurupatnyā na kāryāṇi keśānāṃ ca prasādhanam  || 2,14.31 ||
gurupatnī tu yuvatī nābhivādyeha pādayoḥ  |
kurvota vandanaṃ bhūmyāmasāvahamiti bruvan  || 2,14.32 ||
viproṣya pādagrahaṇamanvahaṃ cābhivādanam  |
gurudāreṣu kurvota satāṃ dharmamanusmaran  || 2,14.33 ||
mātṛṣvasā mātulānī śvaśrūścātha pitṛṣvasā  |
saṃpūjyā gurupatnīva samāstā gurubhāryayā  || 2,14.34 ||
bhrāturbhāryopasaṃgrāhyā savarṇāhanyahanyapi  |
viproṣya tūpasaṃgrāhyā jñātisaṃbandhiyoṣitaḥ  || 2,14.35 ||
piturbhaginyāṃ mātuśca jyāyasyāṃ ca svasaryapi  |
mātṛvadvṛttimātiṣṭhenmāttābhyo garīyasī  || 2,14.36 ||
evamācārasaṃpannamātmavantamadāmbhikam  |
vedamadhyāpayeddharmaṃ purāṇāṅgāni nityaśaḥ  || 2,14.37 ||
saṃvatsaroṣite śiṣye gururjñānamanirdiśan  |
harate duṣkṛtaṃ tasya śiṣyasya vasato guruḥ  || 2,14.38 ||
ācāryaputraḥ śuśrūṣurjñānado dhārmikaḥ śuciḥ  |
śakto'nnador'tho svaḥsādhuradhyāpyā daśa dharmataḥ  || 2,14.39 ||
kṛtajñaśca tathādrohī medhāvī śubhakṛnnaraḥ  |
āptaḥ priyo'tha vidhivatṣaḍadhyāpyā dvijātayaḥ  |
eteṣu brahmaṇo dānamanyatra tu yathoditān  || 2,14.40 ||
ācamya saṃyato nityamadhīyīta udaṅmukhaḥ  |
upasaṃgṛhya tatpādau vīkṣamāṇo gurormukham  |
adhīṣva bho iti brūyādvirāmo'stviti cāramet || 2,14.41 ||
prākkūlān paryupāsīnaḥ pavitraiścaiva pāvitaḥ  |
prāṇāyāmaistribhiḥ pūtastata oṅkāramarhati  || 2,14.42 ||
brāhmaṇaḥ praṇavaṃ kuryādante ca vidhivaddvijaḥ  |
kuryādadhyayanaṃ nityaṃ sa brahmāñjalipūrvataḥ  || 2,14.43 ||
sarveṣāmeva bhūtānāṃ vedaścakṣuḥ sanātanam  |
adhīyītāpyayaṃ nityaṃ brāhmaṇyāccyavate'nyathā  || 2,14.44 ||
yo'dhīyīta ṛco nityaṃ kṣīrāhutyā sa devatāḥ  |
prīṇāti tarpayantyenaṃ kāmaistṛptāḥ sadaiva hi  || 2,14.45 ||
yajūṃṣyadhīte niyataṃ dadhnā prīṇāti devatāḥ  |
sāmānyadhīte prīṇāti ghṛtāhutibhiranvaham  || 2,14.46 ||
atharvāṅgiraso nityaṃ madhvā prīṇāti devatāḥ  |
dharmāṅgāni purāṇāni māṃsaistarpayate surān  || 2,14.47 ||
apāṃ samīpe niyato naityakaṃ vidhimāśritaḥ  |
gāyatrīmapyadhīyīta gatvāraṇyaṃ samāhitaḥ  || 2,14.48 ||
sahasraparamāṃ devīṃ śatamadhyāṃ daśāvarām  |
gāyatrīṃ vai japennityaṃ japayajñaḥ prakīrtitaḥ  || 2,14.49 ||
gāyatrīṃ caiva vedāṃśca tulayātolayatprabhuḥ  |
ekataścaturo vedān gāyatrīṃ ca tathaikataḥ  || 2,14.50 ||
oṅkāramāditaḥ kṛtvā vyāhṛtīstadanantaram  |
tato'dhīyīta sāvitrīmekāgraḥ śraddhayānvitaḥ  || 2,14.51 ||
purākalpe samutpannā bhūrbhuvaḥsvaḥ sanātanāḥ  |
mahāvyāhṛtayastistraḥ sarvāśubhanibarhaṇāḥ  || 2,14.52 ||
pradhānaṃ puruṣaḥ kālo viṣṇurbrahmā maheśvaraḥ  |
sattvaṃ rajastamastistraḥ kramādvyāhṛtayaḥ smṛtāḥ  || 2,14.53 ||
oṅkārastatparaṃ brahma sāvitrī syāttadakṣaram  |
eṣa mantro mahāyogaḥ sārātsāra udāhṛtaḥ  || 2,14.54 ||
yo'dhīte'hanyahanyetāṃ gāyatrīṃ vedamātaram  |
vijñāyārthaṃ brahmacārī sa yāti paramāṃ gatim  || 2,14.55 ||
gāyatrī vedajananī gāyatrī lokapāvanī  |
na gāyatryāḥ paraṃ japyametadvijñāya mucyate  || 2,14.56 ||
śrāvaṇasya tu māsasya paurṇamāsyāṃ dvijottamāḥ  |
āṣāḍhyāṃ proṣṭhapadyāṃ vā vedopākaraṇaṃ smṛtam  || 2,14.57 ||
utsṛjya grāmanagaraṃ māsān vipror'ddhapañcamān  |
adhīyīta śucau deśe brahmacārī samāhitaḥ  || 2,14.58 ||
puṣye tu chandasāṃ kuryādbahirutsarjanaṃ dvijaḥ  |
māghaśuklasya vā prāpte pūrvāhne prathame'hani  || 2,14.59 ||
chandāṃsyūrdhvamathobhyasyecchuklapakṣeṣu vai dvijaḥ  |
vedāṅgāni purāṇāni kṛṣṇapakṣe ca mānavam  || 2,14.60 ||
imānnityamanadhyāyānadīyāno vivarjayet |
adhyāpanaṃ ca kurvāṇo hyabhyasyannapi yatnataḥ  || 2,14.61 ||
karṇaśrave'nile rātrau divā pāṃśusamūhane  |
vidyutstanitavarṣeṣu maholkānāṃ ca saṃplave  |
ākālikamanadhyāyameteṣvāha prajāpatiḥ  || 2,14.62 ||
etānabhyuditān vidyādyadā prāduṣkṛtāgniṣu  |
tadā vidyādanadhyāyamanṛtau cābhradarśane  || 2,14.63 ||
nirghāte bhūmicalane jyotiṣāṃ copasarjane  |
etānākālikān vidyādanadhyāyānṛtāvapi  || 2,14.64 ||
prāduṣkṛteṣvagniṣu tu vidyutstanitanisvane  |
sajyotiḥ syādanadhyāyaḥ śeṣarātrau yathā divā  || 2,14.65 ||
nityānadhyāya eva syādgrāmeṣu nagareṣu ca  |
dharmanaipuṇyakāmānāṃ pūtigandhe ca nityaśaḥ  || 2,14.66 ||
antaḥ śavagate grāme vṛṣalasya ca sannidhau  |
anadhyāyo rudyamāne samavāye janasya ca  || 2,14.67 ||
udake madhyarātre ca viṇmūtre ca visarjane  |
ucchiṣṭaḥ śrāddhabukcaiva manasāpi na cintayet || 2,14.68 ||
pratigṛhya dvijo vidvānekodiṣṭasya ketanam  |
tryahaṃ na kīrtayedbrahma rājño rāhośca sūtake  || 2,14.69 ||
yāvadeko'nudiṣṭasya sneho gandhaśca tiṣṭhati  |
viprasya viduṣo dehe tāvadbrahma na kīrtayet || 2,14.70 ||
śayānaḥ prauḍhapādaśca kṛtvā caivāvasakthikām  |
nādhīyītāmiṣaṃ jagdhvā sūtakānnādyameva ca  || 2,14.71 ||
nīhāre bāṇaśabde ca saṃdhyayorubhayorapi  |
amāvāsyāṃ caturdaśyāṃ paurṇamāsyaṣṭamīṣu ca  || 2,14.72 ||
upākarmaṇi cotsarge trirātraṃ kṣapaṇaṃ smṛtam  |
aṣṭakāsu tvahorātraṃ ṛtvantyāsu ca rātriṣu  || 2,14.73 ||
mārgaśīrṣe tathā pauṣe māghamāse tathaiva ca  |
tistro'ṣṭakāḥ samākhyātā kṛṣṇapakṣetu sūribhiḥ  || 2,14.74 ||
śleṣmātakasya chāyāyāṃ śālmalermadhukasya ca  |
kadācidapi nādhyeyaṃ kovidārakapitthayoḥ  || 2,14.75 ||
samānavidye ca mṛte tathā sabrahmacāriṇi  |
ācārye saṃsthite vāpi trirātraṃ kṣapaṇaṃ smṛtam  || 2,14.76 ||
chidrāṇyetāni viprāṇāṃye'nadhyāyaḥ prakīrtitāḥ  |
hiṃsanti rākṣasāsteṣu tasmādetān vivarjayet || 2,14.77 ||
naityake nāstyanadhyāyaḥ saṃdhyopāsana eva ca  |
upākarmaṇi karmānte homamantreṣu caiva hi  || 2,14.78 ||
ekāmṛcamathaikaṃ vā yajuḥ sāmāthavā punaḥ  |
aṣṭakādyāsvadhīyīta mārute cātivāyati  || 2,14.79 ||
anadhyāyastu nāṅgeṣu netihāsapurāṇayoḥ  |
na dharmaśāstreṣvanyeṣu parvaṇyetāni varjayet || 2,14.80 ||
eṣa dharmaḥ samāsena kīrtito brahmacāriṇām  |
brahmaṇābhihitaḥ pūrvamṛṣīṇāṃ bhāvitātmanām  || 2,14.81 ||
yo'nyatra kurute yatnamanadhītya śrutiṃ dvijaḥ  |
sa saṃmūḍho na saṃbhāṣyo vedabāhyo dvijātibhiḥ  || 2,14.82 ||
na vedapāṭhamātreṇa saṃtuṣṭo vai bhaveddvijaḥ  |
pāṭhamātrāvasannastu paṅke gauriva sīdati  || 2,14.83 ||
yo'dhītya vidhivadvedaṃ vedārthaṃ na vicārayet |
sasānvayaḥ śūdrakalpaḥ pātratāṃ na prapadyate  || 2,14.84 ||
yadi tvātyantikaṃ vāsaṃ kartumicchati vai gurau  |
yuktaḥ paricaredenamāśarīravimokṣaṇāt || 2,14.85 ||
gatvā vanaṃ vā vidhivajjuhuyājjātavedasam  |
adhīyīta sadā nityaṃ brahmaniṣṭhaḥ samāhitaḥ  || 2,14.86 ||
sāvitrīṃ śatarudrīyaṃ vedāntāṃśca viśeṣataḥ  |
abhyasetsatataṃ yukte bhasmasnānaparāyaṇaḥ  || 2,14.87 ||
etadvidhānaṃ paramaṃ purāṇaṃ
vedāgame samyagiheritaṃ vaḥ  |
purā maharṣipravarābhipṛṣṭaḥ
svāyaṃbhuvo yanmanurāha devaḥ  || 2,14.88 ||
evamīśvarasamarpitāntaro
yo'nutiṣṭhati vidhiṃ vidhānavit |
mohajālamapahāya so'mṛto
yāti tatpadamanāmayaṃ śivam  || 2,14.89 ||

iti śrīkūrmapurāṇe ṣaṭsāhastryāṃ saṃhitāyāmuparivibhāge caturdaśo'dhyāyaḥ


_____________________________________________________________


vyāsa uvāca
vedaṃ vedau tathā vedān vedān vā caturo dvijāḥ  |
adhītya cādhigamyārthaṃ tataḥ snāyāddvijottamaḥ  || 2,15.1 ||
gurave tu varaṃ dattvā snāyīta tadanujñayā  |
cīrṇavrato'tha yuktātmā saśaktaḥ snātumarhati  || 2,15.2 ||
vaiṇavīṃ dhārayedyaṣṭimantarvāsastathottaram  |
yajñopavītadvitayaṃ sodakaṃ ca kamaṇḍalum  || 2,15.3 ||
chatraṃ coṣṇīṣamamalaṃ pāduke cāpyupānahau  |
raukme ca kuṇḍale vedaṃ kṛttakeśanakhaḥ śuciḥ  || 2,15.4 ||
svādhyāye nityayuktaḥ syādbahirmālyaṃ na dhārayet |
anyatrakāñcanādvipronaraktāṃ bibhṛyātstrajam  || 2,15.5 ||
śuklāmbaradharo nityaṃ sugandhaḥ priyadarśanaḥ  |
na jīrṇamalavadvāsā bhavedvai vibhave sati  || 2,15.6 ||
na raktamulbaṇaṃ cānyadhṛtaṃ vāso na kuṇḍikām  |
nopānahau strajaṃ cātha pāduke ca prayojayet || 2,15.7 ||
upavītamalaṅkāraṃ darbhān kṛṣṇājināni ca  |
nāpasavyaṃ parīdadhyādvāso na vikṛtaṃ vaset || 2,15.8 ||
āharedvidhivaddārān sadṛśānātmanaḥ śubhān  |
rūpalakṣaṇasaṃyuktān yonidoṣavivarjitān  || 2,15.9 ||
amātṛgotraprabhavāmasamānarṣigotrajām  |
āharedbrāhmaṇo bhāryāṃ śīlaśaucasamanvitām  || 2,15.10 ||
ṛtukālābhigāmī syādyāvatputro'bhijāyate  |
varjayetpratiṣiddhāni prayatnena dināni tu  || 2,15.11 ||
ṣaṣṭhyaṣṭamīṃ pañcadaśīṃ dvādaśīṃ ca caturdaśīm  |
brahmacārī bhavennityaṃ tadvajjanmatrayāhani  || 2,15.12 ||
ādadhītāvasathyāgniṃ juhuyājjātavedasam  |
vratāni snātako nityaṃ pāvanāni ca pālayet || 2,15.13 ||
vedoditaṃ svakaṃ karma nityaṃ kuryādatandritaḥ  |
akurvāṇaḥ patatyāśu narakānatibhīṣaṇān  || 2,15.14 ||
abyasetprayato vedaṃ mahāyajñānna hāpayet |
kuryādgṛhyāṇi karmāṇi saṃdhyopāsanameva ca  || 2,15.15 ||
sakhyaṃ samādhaikaiḥ kuryādupeyādīśvaraṃ sadā  |
daivatānyapi gaccheta kuryādbhāryābhipoṣaṇam  || 2,15.16 ||
na dharmaṃ khyāpayedvidvānna pāpaṃ gūhayedapi  |
kurvotātmahitaṃ nityaṃ sarvabhūtānikampakaḥ  || 2,15.17 ||
vayasaḥ karmaṇor'thasya śrutasyābhijanasya ca  |
veṣavāgbuddhisārūpyamācaran vicaretsadā  || 2,15.18 ||
śrutismṛtyuditaḥ samyaksādhubhiryaśca sevitaḥ  |
tamācāraṃ niṣeveta nehetānyatra karhicit || 2,15.19 ||
yenāsya pitaro yātā yena yātāḥ pitāmahāḥ  |
tena yāyātsatāṃ mārgaṃ tena gacchanna riṣyati  || 2,15.20 ||
nityaṃ svādhyāyaśīlaḥ syānnityaṃ yajñopavītavān  |
satyavādī jitakrodho brahmabhūyāya kalpate  || 2,15.21 ||
saṃdhyāsnānaparo nityaṃ brahmayajñuparāyaṇaḥ  |
anasūyī mṛdurdānto gṛhasthaḥ pretya vardhate  || 2,15.22 ||
vītarāgabhayakrodho lobhamohavivarjitaḥ  |
sāvitrījāpyanirataḥ śrāddhakṛnmucyate gṛhī  || 2,15.23 ||
mātāpitrorhite yukto gobrāhmaṇahite rataḥ  |
dānto yajvā devabhakto brahmaloke mahīyate  || 2,15.24 ||
trivargasevī satataṃ devatānāṃ ca pūjanam  |
kuryādaharaharnityaṃ namasyetprayataḥ surān  || 2,15.25 ||
vibhāgaśīlaḥ satataṃ kṣamāyukto dayālukaḥ  |
gṛhasthastu samākhyāto na gṛheṇa gṛhī bhavet || 2,15.26 ||
kṣamā dayā ca vijñānaṃ satyaṃ caiva damaḥ śamaḥ  |
adhyātmanirataṃ jñānametadbrāhmaṇalakṣaṇam  || 2,15.27 ||
etasmānna pramādyeta viśeṣeṇa dvijottamaḥ  |
yathāśaktiṃ caran karma ninditāni vivarjayet || 2,15.28 ||
vidhūya mohakalilaṃ labdhvā yogamanuttamam  |
gṛhastho mucyate bandhātnātra kāryā vicāraṇā  || 2,15.29 ||
vigarhātikramākṣepahiṃsābandhavadhātmanām  |
anyamanyusamutthānāṃ doṣāṇāṃ marṣaṇaṃ kṣamā  || 2,15.30 ||
svaduḥ kheṣviva kāruṇyaṃ paraduḥ kheṣu sauhṛdāt |
dayeti munayaḥ prāhuḥ sākṣāddharmasya sādhanam  || 2,15.31 ||
caturdaśānāṃ vidyānāṃ dhāraṇaṃ hi yatārthataḥ  |
vijñānamiti tadvidyādyena dharmo vivardhate  || 2,15.32 ||
adhītya vidhivadvidyāmarthaṃ caivopalabhya tu  |
dharmakāryānnivṛttaścenna tadvijñānamiṣyate  || 2,15.33 ||
satyena lokāñjayati satyaṃ tatparamaṃ padam  |
yathābhūtapravādtu satyamāhurmanīṣiṇaḥ  || 2,15.34 ||
damaḥ śarīroparamaḥ śamaḥ prajñāprisādajaḥ  |
adhyātmamakṣaraṃ vidyādyatra gatvā na śocati  || 2,15.35 ||
yayā sa devo bhagavān vidyayā vedyate paraḥ  |
sākṣāddevo mahādevastajjñānamiti kīrtitam  || 2,15.36 ||
tanniṣṭhastatparo vidvānnityamakrodhanaḥ śuciḥ  |
mahāyajñaparo vipro labhate tadanuttamam  || 2,15.37 ||
dharmasyāyatanaṃ yatnāccharīraṃ paripālayet |
na hi dehaṃ vinā rudraḥ puruṣairvidyate paraḥ  || 2,15.38 ||
nityadharmārthakāmeṣu yujyeta niyato dvijaḥ  |
na dharmavarjitaṃ kāmamarthaṃ vā manasā smaret || 2,15.39 ||
sīdannapi hi dharmeṇa na tvadharmaṃ samācaret |
dharmo hi bhagavān devo gatiḥ sarveṣu jantuṣu  || 2,15.40 ||
bhūtānāṃ priyakārī syātna paradrohakarmadhīḥ  |
na vedadevatānindāṃ kuryāttaiśca na saṃvaset || 2,15.41 ||
yastvimaṃ niyataṃ vipro dharmādhyāyaṃ paṭhecchuciḥ  |
adhyāpayetśrāvayedvā brahmaloke mahīyate  || 2,15.42 ||

iti śrīkūrmapurāṇe ṣaṭsāhastryāṃ saṃhitāyāmuparivibhāge pañcadaśo'dhyāyaḥ


_____________________________________________________________

vyāsa uvāca
na hiṃsyātsarvabhūtāninānṛtaṃ vāvadetkvacit |
nāhitaṃ nāpriyaṃ vākyaṃ na stenaḥ syādkadācana  || 2,16.1 ||
tṛṇaṃ vā yadi vā śākaṃ mṛdaṃ vā jalameva vā  |
parasyāpaharañjanturnarakaṃ pratipadyate  || 2,16.2 ||
na rājñaḥ pratigṛhṇīyānna śūdrapatitādapi  |
na cānyasmādaśaktaśca ninditān varjayedbudhaḥ  || 2,16.3 ||
nityaṃ yācanako na syātpunastaṃ naiva yācayet |
prāṇānapaharatyevaṃ yācakastasya durmatiḥ  || 2,16.4 ||
na devadravyahārī syādviśeṣeṇa dvijottamaḥ  |
brahmasvaṃ vā nāpaharedāpadyapi kadācana  || 2,16.5 ||
na viṣaṃ viṣamityāhurbrahmasvaṃ viṣamucyate  |
devasvaṃ cāpi yatnena sadā pariharettataḥ  || 2,16.6 ||
puṣpe śākrodake kāṣṭhe tathā mūle phale tṛṇe  |
adattādānamasteyaṃ manuḥ prāha prajāpatiḥ  || 2,16.7 ||
grahītavyāni puṣpāṇi devārcanavidhau dvijāḥ  |
naikasmādeva niyatamananujñāya kevalam  || 2,16.8 ||
tṛṇaṃ kāṣṭhaṃ phalaṃ puṣpaṃ prakāśaṃ vai haredbudhaḥ  |
dharmārthaṃ kevalaṃ viprā hyanyathā patito bhavet || 2,16.9 ||
tilamudgayavādīnāṃ muṣṭirgrāhyā pathi sthitaiḥ  |
kṣudhārtairnānyathā viprā dharmavidbhiriti sthitiḥ  || 2,16.10 ||
na dharmasyāpadeśena pāpaṃ kṛtvā vrataṃ caret |
vratena pāpaṃ pracchādya kurvan strīśūdradambhanam  || 2,16.11 ||
pretyeha cedṛśo vipro garhyate brahmavādibhiḥ  |
chadmanācaritaṃ yacca vrataṃ rakṣāṃsi gacchati  || 2,16.12 ||
aliṅgī liṅgiveṣeṇa yo vṛttimupajīvati  |
sa liṅgināṃ haredenastiryagyonau ca jāyate  || 2,16.13 ||
baiḍālavratinaḥ pāpā loke dharmavināśakāḥ  |
sadyaḥ patanti pāpeṣu karmaṇastasya tatphalam  || 2,16.14 ||
pāṣaṇḍino vikarmasthān vāmācārāṃstathaiva ca  |
pañcarātrān pāśupatān vāṅmātreṇāpi nārcayet || 2,16.15 ||
vedanindāratānmartyān devanindāratāṃstathā  |
dvijanindāratāṃścaiva manasāpi na cintayet || 2,16.16 ||
yājanaṃ yonisaṃbandhaṃ sahavāsaṃ ca bhāṣaṇam  |
kurvāṇaḥ patate jantustasmādyatnena varjayet || 2,16.17 ||
devadrohādgurudrohaḥ koṭikoṭiguṇādhikaḥ  |
jñānāpavādo nāstikyaṃ tasmātkoṭiguṇādhikam  || 2,16.18 ||
gobhiśca daivatairvipraiḥ kṛṣyā rājopasevayā  |
kulānyakulatāṃ yānti yāni hīnāni dharmataḥ  || 2,16.19 ||
kuvivāhaiḥ kriyālopairvedānadhyayanena ca  |
kulānyakulatāṃ yānti brāhmaṇātikrameṇa ca  || 2,16.20 ||
anṛtātpāradāryācca tathābhakṣyasya bhakṣaṇāt |
aśrautadharmācaraṇātkṣipraṃ naśyati vai kulam  || 2,16.21 ||
aśrotriyeṣu vai dānādvṛṣaleṣu tathaiva ca  |
vihitācārahīneṣu kṣipraṃ naśyati vai kulam  || 2,16.22 ||
nādhārmikairvṛte grāme na vyādhibahule bhṛśam  |
na śūdrarājye nivasenna pāṣaṇḍajanairvṛte  || 2,16.23 ||
himavadvindhyayormadhye pūrvapaścimayoḥ śubham  |
muktvā samudrayordeśaṃ nānyatra nivaseddvijaḥ  || 2,16.24 ||
kṛṣṇo vā yatra carati mṛgo nityaṃ svabhāvataḥ  |
puṇyāśca viśrutā nadyastatra vā nivaseddvijaḥ  || 2,16.25 ||
ardhakrośānnadīkūlaṃ varjayitvā dvijottamaḥ  |
nānyatra nivasetpuṇyaṃ nāntyajagrāmasannidhau  || 2,16.26 ||
na saṃvasecca patitairna caṇḍālairna pukkasaiḥ  |
na mūrkhairnāvaliptaiśca nāntyairnāntyāvasāyibhiḥ  || 2,16.27 ||
ekaśayyāsanaṃ paṅktirbhāṇḍapakvānnamiśraṇam  |
yājanādhyāpane yonistathaiva sahabhojanam  || 2,16.28 ||
sahādhyāyastu daśamaḥ sahayājanameva ca  |
ekādaśa samuddiṣṭā doṣāḥ sāṅkaryasaṃjñitāḥ  || 2,16.29 ||
samīpe vā vyavasthānātpāpaṃ saṃkramate nṛṇām  |
tasmātsarvaprayatnena sāṅkaryaṃ parivarjayet || 2,16.30 ||
ekapaṅktyupaviṣṭā ye na spṛśanti parasparam  |
bhasmanā kṛtamaryādā na teṣāṃ saṃkaro bhavet || 2,16.31 ||
agninā bhasmanā caiva salilenāvasekataḥ  |
dvāreṇa stambhamārgeṇa ṣaḍbhiḥ paṅktirvibhidyate  || 2,16.32 ||
na kuryācchuṣkavairāṇi vivādaṃ ca na paiśunam  |
parakṣetre gāṃ dhayantīṃ na cācakṣīta kasyacit |
na saṃvadetsūtake ca na kañcinmarmaṇi spṛśet || 2,16.33 ||
na sūryapariveṣaṃ vā nendracāpaṃ śavāgnikam  |
parasmai kathayedvidvān śaśinaṃ vā kadācana  || 2,16.34 ||
na kuryādbahubhiḥ sārdhaṃ virodhaṃ bandhubhistathā  |
ātmanaḥ pratikūlāni pareṣāṃ na samācaret || 2,16.35 ||
tithiṃ pakṣasya na brūyātna nakṣatrāṇi nirdiśet |
nodakyāmabhibhāṣeta nāśuciṃ vā dvijottamaḥ  || 2,16.36 ||
na devaguruviprāṇāṃ dīyamānaṃ tu vārayet |
na cātmānaṃ praśaṃsedvā paranindāṃ ca varjayet |
vedanindāṃ devanindāṃ prayatnena vivarjayet || 2,16.37 ||
yastu devānṛṣīn viprānvedān vā nindati dvijaḥ  |
na tasya niṣkṛtirdṛṣṭā śāstreṣviha munīśvarāḥ  || 2,16.38 ||
nindayedvai guruṃ devaṃ vedaṃ vā sopabṛṃhaṇam  |
kalpakoṭiśataṃ sāgraṃ raurave pacyate naraḥ  || 2,16.39 ||
tūṣṇīmāsīta nindāyāṃ na brūyātkiñciduttaram  |
karṇau pidhāya gantavyaṃ na caitānavalokayet || 2,16.40 ||
varjayedvai rahasyāni pareṣāṃ gūhayedbudhaḥ  |
vivādaṃ svajanaiḥ sārdhaṃ na kuryādvai kadācana  || 2,16.41 ||
na pāpaṃ pāpināṃ brūyādapāpaṃ vā dvijāttamāḥ  |
satenatulyadoṣaḥ syānmithyā dvirdeṣavān bhavet || 2,16.42 ||
yāni mithyābhiśastānāṃ patantyaśrūṇi rodanāt |
tāniputrān paśūnghninti teṣāṃ mithyābhiśaṃsinām  || 2,16.43 ||
brihmahatyāsurāpāne steyagurvaṅganāgame  |
dṛṣṭaṃ viśodhanaṃ vṛddhairnāsti mithyābhiśaṃsane  || 2,16.44 ||
nekṣetodyantamādityaṃ śaśinaṃ cānimittataḥ  |
nāstaṃ yāntaṃ na vāristhaṃ nopasṛṣṭaṃ na maghyagam  |
tirohitaṃ vāsasā vā nādarśāntaragāminam  || 2,16.45 ||
na nagnāṃ striyamīkṣeta puruṣaṃ vā kadācana  |
na ca mūtraṃ purīṣaṃ vā na ca saṃspṛṣṭamaithunam  |
nāśuciḥ sūryasomādīn grahānālokayedbudhaḥ  || 2,16.46 ||
patitavyaṅgacaṇḍālānucchiṣṭānnāvalokayet |
nābhibhāṣeta ca paramucchiṣṭo vāvaguṇṭhitaḥ  || 2,16.47 ||
na paśyetpretasaṃsparśaṃ na kruddhasya gurormukham  |
na tailodakayośchāyāṃ na patnīṃ bhojane sati  |
nāmuktabandhanāṅgāṃ vā nonmattaṃ mattameva vā  || 2,16.48 ||
nāśnīyātbhāryayā sārdhaṃnaināmīkṣeta cāśnatīm  |
kṣuvantīṃ jṛmbhamāṇāṃ vā nāsanasthāṃ yathāsukham  || 2,16.49 ||
nodake cātmano rūpaṃ na kūlaṃ śvabhrameva vā  |
na laṅghayecca mūtraṃ vā nādhitiṣṭhetkadācana  || 2,16.50 ||
na śūdrāya matiṃ dadyātkṛśaraṃ pāyasaṃ dadhi  |
nocchiṣṭaṃ vā madhu ghṛtaṃ na ca kṛṣṇājinaṃ haviḥ  || 2,16.51 ||
na caivāsmai vrataṃ dadyānna ca dharmaṃ vadedbudhaḥ  |
na ca krodhavaśaṃ gaccheddveṣaṃ rāgaṃ ca varjayet || 2,16.52 ||
lobhaṃ dambhaṃ tathā yatnādasūyāṃ jñānakutsanam  |
īrṣyāṃ madaṃ tathā śokaṃ mohaṃ ca parivarjayet || 2,16.53 ||
na kuryātkasyacitpīḍāṃ sutaṃ śiṣyaṃ ca tāḍayet |
na hīnānupaseveta na ca tīkṣṇamatīn kvacit || 2,16.54 ||
nātmānaṃ cāvamanyeta dainyaṃ yatnena varjayet |
na viśiṣṭānasatkuryātnātmānaṃ vā śapedbudhaḥ  || 2,16.55 ||
na nakhairvilikhedbhūmiṃ gāṃ ca saṃveśayenna hi  |
na nadīṣu nadīṃ brūyātparvateṣu ca parvatān  || 2,16.56 ||
āvāse bhojane vāpi na tyajethasayāyinam  |
nāvagāhedapo nagno vahniṃ nātivrajetpadā  || 2,16.57 ||
śiro'bhyaṅgāvaśiṣṭena tailenāṅgaṃ na lepayet |
na sarpaśastraiḥ krīḍeta svāni khāni na saṃspṛśet |
romāṇi ca rahasyāni nāśiṣṭena saha vrajet || 2,16.58 ||
na pāṇipādavāṅnetracāpalyaṃ samupāśrayet |
na śiśnodaracāpalyaṃ na ca śravaṇayoḥ kvacit || 2,16.59 ||
na cāṅganakhavādaṃ vai kuryānnāñjalinā pibet |
nābhihanyājjalaṃ padbhyāṃ pāṇinā vā kadācana  || 2,16.60 ||
na śātayediṣṭakābhiḥ phalāni na phalena ca  |
na mlecchabhāṣāṃ śikṣeta nākarṣecca padāsanam  || 2,16.61 ||
na bhedanamavasphoṭaṃ chedanaṃ vā vilekhanam  |
kuryādvimardanaṃ dhīmānnākasmādeva niṣphalam  || 2,16.62 ||
notsaṅgebhakṣayedbhakṣyaṃ vṛthā ceṣṭāṃ ca nācaret |
na nṛtyedathavā gāyenna vāditrāṇi vādayet || 2,16.63 ||
na saṃhatābhyāṃ pāṇibhyāṃ kaṇḍūyedātmanaḥ śiraḥ  |
na laukikaiḥ stavairdevāṃstoṣayedbāhyajairapi  || 2,16.64 ||
nākṣaiḥ krīḍenna dhāveta nāpsu viṇmūtramācaret |
nocchiṣṭaḥ saṃviśennityaṃ na nagnaḥ snānamācaret || 2,16.65 ||
na gacchenna paṭhedvāpi na caiva svaśiraḥ spṛśet |
na dantairnakharomāṇi chindyātsuptaṃ na bodhayet || 2,16.66 ||
na bālātapamāsevetpretadhūmaṃ vivarjayet |
naikaḥ supyācchūnyagṛhe svayaṃ nopānahau haret || 2,16.67 ||
nākāraṇādvā niṣṭhīvenna bāhubhyāṃ nadīṃ taret |
na pādakṣālanaṃ kuryātpādenaiva kadācana  || 2,16.68 ||
nāgnau pratāpayetpādau na kāṃsye dhāvayedbudhaḥ  |
nābhiprāsarayeddevaṃ brāhmaṇān gāmathāpi vā  |
vāyvagniguruviprān vā sūryaṃ vā śaśinaṃ prati  || 2,16.69 ||
aśuddhaḥ śayanaṃ yānaṃ svādhyāyaṃ snānavāhanam  |
bahirniṣkramaṇaṃ caiva na kurvota kathañcana  || 2,16.70 ||
svapnamadhyayanaṃ snānamudvartaṃ bhojanaṃ gatim  |
ubhayoḥ saṃdhyayornityaṃ madhyāhne caiva varjayet || 2,16.71 ||
na spṛśetpāṇinocchiṣṭo viprogobrāhmaṇānalān  |
na cāsanaṃ padā vāpi na devapratimāṃ spṛśet || 2,16.72 ||
nāśuddho'gniṃ paricarenna devān kīrtayedṛṣīn  |
nāvagāhedagādhāmbu dhārayennānimittataḥ  || 2,16.73 ||
na vāmahastenoddhatya pibedvaktreṇa vā jalam  |
nottaredanupaspṛśya nāpsu retaḥ samutsṛjet || 2,16.74 ||
amedhyaliptamanyadvā lohitaṃ vā viṣāṇi vā  |
vyatikramenna stravantīṃ nāpsu maithunamācaret |
caityaṃ vṛkṣaṃ na vai chindyānnāpsu ṣṭhīvanamācaret || 2,16.75 ||
nāsthibhasmakapālāni na keśānna ca kaṇṭakān  |
tuṣāṅgārakarīṣaṃ vā nādhitiṣṭhetkadācana  || 2,16.76 ||
na cāgniṃ laṅghayeddhīmānnopadadhyādadhaḥ kvacit |
na cainaṃ pādataḥ kuryānmukhena na dhamedbudhaḥ  || 2,16.77 ||
na kūpamavaroheta nāvekṣetāśuciḥ kvacit |
agnau na ca kṣipedagniṃ nādbhiḥ praśamayettathā  || 2,16.78 ||
suhṛnmaraṇamārtiṃ vā na svayaṃ śrāvayetparān  |
apaṇyaṃ kūṭapaṇyaṃ vā vikraye na prayojayet || 2,16.79 ||
na vahniṃ mukhaniśvāsairjvālayennāśucirbudhaḥ  |
puṇyasthānodakasthāne sīmāntaṃ vā kṛṣenna tu  || 2,16.80 ||
na bhindyātpūrvasamayamabhyupetaṃ kadācana  |
parasparaṃ paśūn vyālān pakṣiṇo nāvabodhayet || 2,16.81 ||
parabādhaṃ na kurvota jalavātātapādibhiḥ  |
kārayitvā svakarmāṇi kārūn paścānna vañcayet |
sāyaṃprātargṛhadvārān bhikṣārthaṃ nāvaghaṭṭayet || 2,16.82 ||
bahirmālyaṃ bahirgandhaṃ bhāryayā saha bhojanam  |
vigṛhya vādaṃ kudvārapraveśaṃ ca vivarjayet || 2,16.83 ||
na khādanbrāhmaṇastiṣṭhenna jalpedvā hasan budhaḥ  |
svamagniṃ naiva hastena spṛśennāpsu ciraṃ vaset || 2,16.84 ||
na pakṣakeṇopadhamenna śūrpeṇa na pāṇinā  |
mukhe naiva dhamedagniṃ mukhādagnirajāyata  || 2,16.85 ||
parastriyaṃ na bhāṣeta nāyājyaṃ yājayeddvijaḥ  |
naikaścaretsabhāṃ vipraḥ samavāyaṃ ca varjayet || 2,16.86 ||
na devāyatanaṃ gacchetkadācidvāpradakṣiṇam  |
na vījayedvā vastreṇa na devāyatane svapet || 2,16.87 ||
naiko'dhvānaṃ prapadyeta nādhārmikajanaiḥ saha  |
na vyādhidūṣitairvāpi na śūdraiḥ patitena vā  || 2,16.88 ||
nopānadvarjito vātha jalādirahitastathā  |
na rātrau nāriṇā sārdhaṃ na vinā ca kamaṇḍalum  |
nāgnigobrāhmaṇādīnāmantareṇa vrajetkvacit || 2,16.89 ||
na vatsatantrīṃ vitatāmatikrāmetkvaciddvijaḥ  |
na nindedyoginaḥ siddhān vratino vāyatīṃstathā  || 2,16.90 ||
devatāyatanaṃ prājño devānāṃ caiva satriṇām  |
nākrāmetkāmataśchāyāṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ ca gorapi  || 2,16.91 ||
svāṃ tu nākramayecchāyāṃ patitādyairna rogibhiḥ  |
nāṅgārabhasmakeśādiṣvadhitiṣṭhetkadācana  || 2,16.92 ||
varjayenmārjanīreṇuṃ snānavastraghacodakam  |
na bhakṣayedabhakṣyāṇi nāpeyaṃ ca pibeddvijaḥ  || 2,16.93 ||

iti śrīkūrmapurāṇe ṣaṭsāhastryāṃ saṃhitāyāmuparivibhāge ṣoḍaśo'dhyāyaḥ


_____________________________________________________________


vyāsa uvāca
nādyācchūdrasya vipro'nnaṃ mohādvā yadi vānyataḥ  |
sa śūdrayoniṃ vrajati yastu bhuṅkte hyanāpadi  || 2,17.1 ||
ṣaṇmāsān yo dvijo bhuṅkte śūdrasyānnaṃ vigarhitam  |
jīvanneva bhavecchūdro mṛtaḥ śvā cābhijāyate  || 2,17.2 ||
brāhmaṇakṣatriyaviśāṃ śūdrasya ca munīśvarāḥ  |
yasyānnenodarasthena mṛtastadyonimāpnuyāt || 2,17.3 ||
rājānnaṃ nartakānnaṃ ca takṣṇo'nnaṃ carmakāriṇaḥ  |
gaṇānnaṃ gaṇikānnaṃ ca ṣaṇḍhānnaṃ caiva varjayet || 2,17.4 ||
cakropajīvirajakataskaradhvajināṃ tathā  |
gāndharvalohakārānnaṃ sūtakānnaṃ ca varjayet || 2,17.5 ||
kulālacitrakarmānnaṃ vārdhuṣeḥ patitasya ca  |
paunarbhavacchatrikayorabhiśastasya caiva hi  || 2,17.6 ||
suvarṇakāraśailūṣavyādhabaddhāturasya ca  |
cikitsakasya caivānnaṃ puṃścalyā daṇḍikasya ca  || 2,17.7 ||
stenanāstikayorannaṃ devatānindakasya ca  |
somavikrayiṇaścānnaṃ śvapākasya viśeṣataḥ  || 2,17.8 ||
bhāryājitasya caivānnaṃ yasya copapatirgṛhe  |
utsṛṣṭasya kadaryasya tathaivocchiṣṭabhojinaḥ  || 2,17.9 ||
apāṅktyānnaṃ ca saṅghānnaṃ śastrājīvasya caiva hi  |
klībasaṃnyāsinoścānnaṃ mattonmattasya caiva hi  |
bhītasya ruditasyānnamavakruṣṭaṃ parikṣutam  || 2,17.10 ||
brahmadviṣaḥ pāparuceḥ śrāddhānnaṃ sūtakasya ca  |
vṛthāpākasya caivānnaṃ śāvānnaṃ śvaśurasya ca  || 2,17.11 ||
aprajānāṃ tu nārīṇāṃ bhṛtakasya tathaiva ca  |
kārukānnaṃ viśeṣeṇa śastravikrayiṇastathā  || 2,17.12 ||
śauṇḍānnaṃ ghāṭikānnaṃ ca bhiṣajāmannameva ca  |
viddhaprajananasyānnaṃ parivittyannameva ca  || 2,17.13 ||
punarbhuvo viśeṣeṇa tathaiva didhiṣūpateḥ  |
avajñātaṃ cāvadhūtaṃ saroṣaṃ vismayānvitam  |
gurorapi na bhoktavyamannaṃ saṃskāravarjitam  || 2,17.14 ||
duṣkṛtaṃ hi manuṣyasya sarvamanne vyavasthitam  |
yo yasyānnaṃ samaśnāti sa tasyāśnāni kilbiṣam  || 2,17.15 ||
ārdhikaḥ kulamitraśca svagopālaśca nāpitaḥ  |
ete śūdreṣu bhojyānnā yaścātmānaṃ nivedayet || 2,17.16 ||
kuśīlavaḥ kumbhakāraḥ kṣetrakarmaka eva ca  |
ete śūdreṣu bhojyānnā dattvā svalpaṃ paṇaṃ budhaiḥ  || 2,17.17 ||
pāyasaṃ snehapakvaṃ yadgorasaṃ caiva saktavaḥ  |
piṇyākaṃ caiva tailaṃ ca śūdrādgrāhyaṃ dvijātibhiḥ  || 2,17.18 ||
vṛntākaṃ nālikāśākaṃ kusumbhāśmantakaṃ tathā  |
palāṇḍuṃ laśunaṃ śuktaṃ niryāsaṃ caiva varjayet || 2,17.19 ||
chatrākaṃ viḍvarāhaṃ ca śelaṃ peyūṣameva ca  |
vilayaṃ sumukhaṃ caiva kavakāni ca varjayet || 2,17.20 ||
gṛñjanaṃ kiṃśukaṃ caiva kakubhāṇḍaṃ tathaiva ca  |
udumbaramalābuṃ ca jagdhvā patati vai dvijaḥ  || 2,17.21 ||
vṛthā kṛśarasaṃyāvaṃ pāyasāpūpameva ca  |
anupākṛtamāṃsaṃ ca devānnāni havīṃṣi ca  || 2,17.22 ||
yavāgūṃ mātuliṅgaṃ ca matsyānapyanupākṛtān  |
nīpaṃ kapitthaṃ plakṣaṃ ca prayatnena vivarjayet || 2,17.23 ||
piṇyākaṃ coddhṛtasnehaṃ devadhānya tathaiva ca  |
rātrau ca tilasaṃbaddhaṃ prayatnena dadhi tyajet || 2,17.24 ||
nāśnīyātpayasā takraṃ na bījānyupajīvayet |
kriyāduṣṭaṃ bhāvaduṣṭamasatsaṃsargi varjayet || 2,17.25 ||
keśakīṭāvapannaṃ ca sahṛllekhaṃ ca nityaśaḥ  |
śvāghrātaṃ ca punaḥ siddhaṃ caṇḍālāvekṣitaṃ tathā  || 2,17.26 ||
udakyayā ca patitairgavā cāghrātameva ca  |
anarcitaṃ puryuṃ ṣitaṃ paryāyānnaṃ ca nityaśaḥ  || 2,17.27 ||
kākakukkuṭasaṃspṛṣṭaṃ kṛmibhiścaiva saṃyutam  |
manuṣyairapyavaghrātaṃ kuṣṭhinā spṛṣṭameva ca  || 2,17.28 ||
na rajasvalayā dattaṃ na puṃścālyā saroṣayā  |
malabadvāsasā vāpi paravāso'tha varjayet || 2,17.29 ||
vivatsāyāśca goḥ kṣīramauṣṭraṃ vānirdaśaṃ tathā  |
āvikaṃ sandhinīkṣīramapeyaṃ manurabravīt || 2,17.30 ||
balākaṃ haṃsadātyūhaṃ kalaviṅkaṃ śukaṃ tathā  |
kuraraṃ ca cakoraṃ ca jālapādaṃ ca kokilam  || 2,17.31 ||
vāyasaṃ khañjarīṭaṃ ca śyenaṃ gṛdhraṃ tathaiva ca  |
ulūkaṃ cakravākaṃ ca bhāsaṃ pārāvatānapi  |
kapotaṃ ṭiṭṭibhaṃ caiva grāmakukkuṭameva ca  || 2,17.32 ||
siṃhavyāghraṃ ca mārjāraṃ śvānaṃ śūkarameva ca  |
śṛgālaṃ markaṭaṃ caiva gardabhaṃ ca na bhakṣayet || 2,17.33 ||
na bhakṣayetsarvamṛgān pakṣiṇo'nyān vanecarān  |
jalecarān sthalacarān prāṇinaśceti dhāraṇā  || 2,17.34 ||
godhā kūrmaḥ śaśaḥ śvāvicchalyakaśceti sattamāḥ  |
bhakṣyāḥ pañcanakhā nityaṃ manurāha prijāpatiḥ  || 2,17.35 ||
matsyān saśalkān bhuñjīyānmāṃsaṃ rauravamevaca  |
nivedya devatābhyastu brāhmaṇebhyastu nānyathā  || 2,17.36 ||
mayūraṃ tittiraṃ caiva kapotaṃ ca kapiñjalam  |
vādhrīṇasaṃ bakaṃ bhakṣyaṃ mīnahaṃsaparājitāḥ  || 2,17.37 ||
śapharaṃ siṃhatuṇḍaṃ ca tathā pāṭhīnarohitau  |
matsyāścaite samuddiṣṭā bhakṣaṇāya dvijottamāḥ  || 2,17.38 ||
prokṣitaṃ bhakṣayedeṣāṃ māṃsaṃ ca dvijakāmyayā  |
yathāvidhi niyuktaṃ ca prāṇānāmapi cātyaye  || 2,17.39 ||
bhakṣayennaiva māṃsāni śeṣabhojī na lipyate  |
auṣadhārthamaśaktau vā niyogādyajñakāraṇāt || 2,17.40 ||
āmantritastu yaḥ śrāddhe daive vā māṃsamutsṛjet |
yāvanti paśuromāṇi tāvato narakān vrajet || 2,17.41 ||
adeyaṃ cāpyapeyaṃ ca tathaivāspṛśyameva ca  |
dvijātīnāmanālokyaṃ nityaṃ madyamiti sthitiḥ  || 2,17.42 ||
tasmātsarvaprakāreṇa madyaṃ nityaṃ vivarjayet |
pītvā patati karmabhyastvasaṃbhāṣyo bhaveddvijaḥ  || 2,17.43 ||
bhakṣayitvā hyabhakṣyāṇi pītvāpeyānyapi dvijaḥ  |
nādhikārī bhavettāvadyāvadtanna jahātyadhaḥ  || 2,17.44 ||
tasmātpariharennityamabhakṣyāṇi prayatnataḥ  |
apeyāni ca vipro vai tathā cedyāti rauravam  || 2,17.45 ||

iti śrīkūrmapurāṇe ṣaṭsāhastryāṃ saṃhitāyāmuparivibhāge saptadaśo'dhyāyaḥ


_____________________________________________________________

ṛṣaya ūcuḥ
ahanyahani kartavyaṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ mahāmune  |
tadācakṣvākhilaṃ karma yena mucyeta bandhanāt || 2,18.1 ||
vyāsa uvāca
vakṣye samāhitā yūyaṃ śṛṇudhvaṃ gadato mama  |
ahanyahani kartavyaṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ kramādvidhim  || 2,18.2 ||
brāhme muhūrte tūtthāya dharmamarthaṃ ca cintayet |
kāyakleśaṃ tadudbhūtaṃ dhyāyīta manaseśvaram  || 2,18.3 ||
uṣaḥ kāle'tha saṃprāpte kṛtvā cāvaśyakaṃ budhaḥ  |
snāyānnadīṣu suddhāsu śaucaṃ kṛtvā yathāvidhi  || 2,18.4 ||
prātaḥ snānena pūyante ye'pi pāpakṛto janāḥ  |
tasmātsarvaprayatnena prātaḥ snānaṃ samācaret || 2,18.5 ||
prātaḥ snānaṃ praśaṃsanti dṛṣṭādṛṣṭakaraṃ śubham  |
ṛṣīṇāmṛṣitā nityaṃ prātaḥ snānānna saṃśayaḥ  || 2,18.6 ||
mukhe suptasya satataṃ lālā yāḥ saṃstravanti hi  |
tato naivācaretkarma akṛtvā snānamāditaḥ  || 2,18.7 ||
alakṣmīḥ kālakarṇo ca duḥ svapnaṃ durvicintitam  |
prātaḥ snānena pāpāni pūyante nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 2,18.8 ||
na ca snānaṃ vinā puṃsāṃ pāvanaṃ karma susmṛtam  |
home japye viśeṣeṇa tasmātsnānaṃ samācaret || 2,18.9 ||
aśaktāvaśiraskaṃ vā snānamasya vidhīyate  |
ārdreṇa vāsasā vātha mārjanaṃ kāpilaṃ smṛtam  || 2,18.10 ||
asāmarthye samutpanne snānamevaṃ samācaret |
brāhmādīni yathāśaktau snānānyāhurmanīṣiṇaḥ  || 2,18.11 ||
brāhmamāgneyamuddiṣṭaṃ vāyavyaṃ divyameva ca  |
vāruṇaṃ yaugikaṃ tadvatṣoḍhā snānaṃ prakīrtitam  || 2,18.12 ||
brāhmaṃ tu mārjanaṃ mantraiḥ kuśaiḥ sodakabindubhiḥ  |
āgneyaṃ bhasmanā pādamastakāddehadhūlanam  || 2,18.13 ||
gavāṃ hi rajasā proktaṃ vāyavyaṃ snānamuttamam  |
yattu sātapavarṣeṇa snānaṃ taddivyamucyate  || 2,18.14 ||
vāruṇaṃ cāvagāhastu mānasaṃ tvātmavedanam  |
yaugikaṃ snānamākhyātaṃ yogo viṣṇuvicintanam  || 2,18.15 ||
ātmatīrthamiti khyātaṃ sevitaṃ brahmavādibhiḥ  |
manaḥ śucikaraṃ puṃsāṃ nityaṃ tatsnānamācaret || 2,18.16 ||
śaktaścedvāruṇaṃ vidvān prājāpatyaṃ tathaiva ca  |
prakṣālya dantakāṣṭhaṃ vai bhakṣayitvā vidhānataḥ  || 2,18.17 ||
ācamya prayato nityaṃ snānaṃ prātaḥ samācaret |
madhyāṅgulisamasthaulyaṃ dvādaśāṅgulasaṃmitam  || 2,18.18 ||
satvacaṃ dantakāṣṭhaṃ syāttadagreṇa tu dhāvayet |
kṣīravṛkṣasamudbhūtaṃ mālatīsaṃbhavaṃ śubham  |
apāmārgaṃ ca bilvaṃ ca karavīraṃ viśeṣataḥ  || 2,18.19 ||
varjayitvā ninditāni gṛhītvaikaṃ yathoditam  |
parihṛtya dinaṃ pāpaṃ bhakṣayedvai vidhānavit || 2,18.20 ||
notpāṭayeddantakāṣṭaṃnāṅgulyā dhāvayetkvacit |
prakṣālya bhaṅktvā tajjahyācchucaudeśe samāhitaḥ  || 2,18.21 ||
snātvā saṃtarpayeddevānṛṣīn pitṛgaṇāṃstathā  |
ācamya mantravannityaṃ punarācamya vāgyataḥ  || 2,18.22 ||
saṃmārjya mantrairātmānaṃ kuśaiḥ sodakabindubhiḥ  |
āpo hiṣṭhā vyāhṛtibhiḥ sāvitryā vāruṇaiḥ śubhaiḥ  || 2,18.23 ||
oṅkāravyāhṛtiyutāṃ gāyatrīṃ vedamātaram  |
japtvā jalāñjaliṃ dadyādbhāskaraṃ prati tanmanāḥ  || 2,18.24 ||
prākkūleṣu samāsīno darbheṣu susamāhitaḥ  |
prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ kṛtvā dhyāyetsaṃdhyāmiti śrutiḥ  || 2,18.25 ||
yā saṃdhyā sā jagatsūtirmāyātītā hi niṣkalā  |
aiśvarī tu parāśaktistattvatrayasamudbhavā  || 2,18.26 ||
dhyātvār'kamaṇḍalagatāṃ sāvitrīṃ vai japan budhaḥ  |
prāṅmukhaḥ satataṃ vipraḥ saṃdhyopāsanamācaret || 2,18.27 ||
saṃdhyāhīno'śucirnityamanarhaḥ sarvakarmasu  |
yadanyatkurute kiñcinna tasya phalamāpnuyāt || 2,18.28 ||
ananyacetasaḥ śāntā brāhmaṇā vedapāragāḥ  |
upāsya vidhivatsaṃdhyāṃ prāptāḥ pūrvaṃ parāṃ gatim  || 2,18.29 ||
yo'nyatra kurute yatnaṃ dharmakārye dvijottamaḥ  |
vihāya saṃdhyāpraṇatiṃ sa yāti narakāyutam  || 2,18.30 ||
tasmātsarvaprayatnena saṃdhyopāsanamācaret |
upāsito bhavettena devo yogatanuḥ paraḥ  || 2,18.31 ||
sahasraparamāṃ nityaṃ śatamadhyāṃ daśāvarām  |
sāvitrariṃ vai japedvidvān prāṅmukhaḥ prayataḥ sthitaḥ  || 2,18.32 ||
athopatiṣṭhedādityamudayantaṃ samāhitaḥ  |
mantraistu vividhaiḥ saurerṛgyajuḥ sāmasaṃbhavaiḥ  || 2,18.33 ||
upasthāya mahāyogaṃ devadevaṃ divākaram  |
kurvota praṇatiṃ bhūmau mūrdhnā tenaiva mantrataḥ  || 2,18.34 ||
oṃ khakholkāya śāntāya kāraṇatrayahetave  |
nivedayāmi cātmānaṃ namaste jñānarūpiṇe  |
namaste ghṛṇine tubhyaṃ sūryāya brahmarūpiṇe  || 2,18.35 ||
tvameva brahma paramamāpo jyotī raso'mṛtam  |
bhūrbhuvaḥ svastvamoṅkāraḥ sarve rudrāḥ sanātanāḥ  |
puruṣaḥ sanmaho'tastvāṃ praṇamāmi kapardinam  || 2,18.36 ||
tvameva viśvaṃ bahudhā sadasatsūyate ca yat |
namo rudrāya sūryāya tvāmahaṃ śaraṇaṃ gataḥ  || 2,18.37 ||
pracetase namastubhyaṃ namo mīḍhuṣṭamāya te  |
namo namaste rudrāya tvāmahaṃ śaraṇaṃ gataḥ  || 2,18.38 ||
hiraṇyabāhave tubhyaṃ hiraṇyapataye namaḥ  |
ambikāpataye tubhyamumāyāḥ pataye namaḥ  || 2,18.39 ||
namo'stu nīlagrīvāya namastubhyaṃ pinākine  |
vilohitāya bhargāya sahasrākṣāya te namaḥ  || 2,18.40 ||
namo haṃsāya te nityamādityāya namo'stu te  |
namaste vajrahastāya tryambakāya namo'stu te  || 2,18.41 ||
prapadye tvāṃ virūpākṣaṃ mahāntaṃ parameśvaram  |
hiraṇmayaṃ gṛhe guptamātmānaṃ sarvadehinām  || 2,18.42 ||
namasyāmi paraṃ jyotirbrahmāṇaṃ tvāṃ parāṃ gatim  |
viśvaṃ paśupatiṃ bhīmaṃ naranārīśarīriṇam  || 2,18.43 ||
namaḥ sūryāya rudrāya bhāsvate parameṣṭhine  |
ugrāya sarvabhaktāya tvāṃ prapadye sadaiva hi  || 2,18.44 ||
etadvai sūryahṛdayaṃ japtvā stavamanuttamam  |
prātaḥ kāle'tha madhyāhne namaskuryāddivākaram  || 2,18.45 ||
idaṃ putrāya śiṣyāya dhārmikāya dvijātaye  |
pradeyaṃ sūryahṛdayaṃ brahmaṇā tu pradarśitam  || 2,18.46 ||
sarvapāpapraśamanaṃ vedasārasamudbhavam  |
brāhmaṇānāṃ hitaṃ puṇyamṛṣisaṅghairniṣevitam  || 2,18.47 ||
athāgamya gṛhaṃ vipraḥ samācamya yathāvidhi  |
prajvālya vihniṃ vidhivajjuhuyājjātavedasam  || 2,18.48 ||
ṛtvikputro'tha patnī vā śiṣyo vāpi sahodaraḥ  |
prāpyānujñāṃ viśeṣeṇa juhuyurvā yatāvidhi  || 2,18.49 ||
pavitrapāṇiḥ pūtātmā śuklāmbaradharottaraḥ  |
ananyamānaso vahniṃ juhuyātsaṃyatendriyaḥ  || 2,18.50 ||
vinā darbheṇa yatkarma vinā sūtreṇa vā punaḥ  |
rākṣasaṃ tadbhavetsarvaṃ nāmutreha phalapradam  || 2,18.51 ||
daivatāni namaskuryāddeyasārānnivedayet |
dadyātpuṣpādikaṃ teṣāṃ vṛddhāṃścaivābhivādayet || 2,18.52 ||
guruṃ caivāpyupāsīta hitaṃ cāsya samācaret |
vedābhyāsaṃ tataḥ kuryātprayatnācchaktito dvijaḥ  || 2,18.53 ||
japedadhyāpayecchiṣyān dhārayecca vicārayet |
avekṣeta ca śāstrāṇi dharmādīni dvijottamaḥ  |
vaidikāṃścaiva nigamān vedāṅgāni veśiṣataḥ  || 2,18.54 ||
upeyādīśvaraṃ cātha yogakṣemaprasiddhaye  |
sādhayedvividhānarthān kuṭumbārthe tato dvijaḥ  || 2,18.55 ||
tato madhyāhnasamaye snānārthaṃ mṛdamāharet |
puṣpākṣatān kuśatilān gomayaṃ śuddhameva ca  || 2,18.56 ||
nadīṣu devakhāteṣu taḍāgeṣu saraḥsu ca  |
snānaṃ samācarennityaṃ gartaprastravaṇeṣu ca  || 2,18.57 ||
parakīyanipāneṣu na snāyādvai kadācana  |
pañcapiṇḍān samuddhṛtya snāyādvāsaṃbhave punaḥ  || 2,18.58 ||
mṛdaikayā śiraḥ kṣālyaṃ dvābhyāṃ nābhestathopari  |
adhaśca tisṛbhiḥ kāyaṃ pādau ṣaḍbhistathaiva ca  || 2,18.59 ||
mṛttikā ca samuddiṣṭā tvārdrāmalakamātrikā  |
gomayasya pramāṇaṃ tattenāṅgaṃ lepayettataḥ  || 2,18.60 ||
lepayitvā tu tīrasthastalliṅgaireva mantrataḥ  |
prakṣālyācamya vidhivattataḥ snāyātsamāhitaḥ  || 2,18.61 ||
abhimantrya jalaṃ mantraistalliṅgairvāruṇaiḥ śubhaiḥ  |
bhāvapūtastadavyaktaṃ dhyāyan vai viṣṇumavyayam  || 2,18.62 ||
āpo nārāyaṇodbhūtāstā evāsyāyanaṃ punaḥ  |
tasmānnārāyaṇaṃ devaṃ snānakāle smaredbudhaḥ  || 2,18.63 ||
procya soṃkāramādityaṃ trirnimajjejjalāśaye  |
ācāntaḥ punarācāmenmantreṇānena mantravit || 2,18.64 ||
antaścarasi bhūteṣu guhāyāṃ viśvato mukhaḥ  |
tvaṃ yajñastvaṃ vaṣaṭkāra āpo jyotī raso'mṛtam  || 2,18.65 ||
drupadāṃ vā trirabhyasyedvyāhṛtipraṇavānvitām  |
sāvitrīṃ vā japedvidvān tathā caivāghamarṣaṇam  || 2,18.66 ||
tataḥ saṃmārjanaṃ kuryādāpo hi ṣṭhā mayobhuvaḥ  |
idamāpaḥ pravahata vyāhṛtibhistathaiva ca  || 2,18.67 ||
tato'bhimantrya tattīrthamāpo hiṣṭhādimantrakaiḥ  |
antarjalagato magno japettriraghamarṣaṇam  || 2,18.68 ||
tripadāṃ vātha sāvitrīṃ tadviṣṇoḥ paramaṃ padam  |
āvartayedvā praṇavaṃ devaṃ vā saṃsmareddharim  || 2,18.69 ||
drupadādiva yo mantro yajurvede pratiṣṭhitaḥ  |
antarjale trirāvartya sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate  || 2,18.70 ||
apaḥ pāṇau samādāya japtvā vai mārjane kṛte  |
vinyasya mūrdhni tattoyaṃ mucyate sarvapātakaiḥ  || 2,18.71 ||
yathāśvamedhaḥ kraturāṭsarvapāpāpanodanaḥ  |
tathāghamarṣaṇaṃ sūktaṃ sarvapāpāpanodanam  || 2,18.72 ||
athopatiṣṭhedādityaṃ mūrdhni puṣpānvitāñjalim  |
prakṣipyālokayeddevamudvayaṃ tamasaspari  || 2,18.73 ||
udutyaṃ citramityete taccakṣuriti mantrataḥ  |
haṃsaḥ śuciṣadetena sāvitryā ca viśeṣataḥ  || 2,18.74 ||
anyaiśca vaidikairmantraiḥ sauraiḥ pāpapraṇāśanaiḥ  |
sāvitrīṃ vai japetpaścājjapayajñaḥ sa vai smṛtaḥ  || 2,18.75 ||
vividhāni pavitrāṇi guhyavidyāstathaiva ca  |
śatarudrīyamatharvaśiraḥ saurāṃśca śaktitaḥ  || 2,18.76 ||
prākkūleṣu samāsīnaḥ kuśeṣu prāṅmukhaḥ śuciḥ  |
tiṣṭhaṃścedīkṣamāṇor'kaṃ japyaṃ kuryātsamāhitaḥ  || 2,18.77 ||
sphāṭikendrākṣarudrākṣaiḥ putrajīvasamudbhavaḥ  |
kartavyā tvakṣamālā syāduttarāduttamā smṛtā  || 2,18.78 ||
japakāle na bhāṣeta nānyāni prekṣayedbudhaḥ  |
na kampayecchirogrīvāṃ dantānnaiva prakāśayet || 2,18.79 ||
guhyakā rākṣasā siddhā haranti prasabhaṃ yataḥ  |
ekānte suśubhe deśe tasmājjapyaṃ samācaret || 2,18.80 ||
caṇḍālāśaucapatitān dṛṣṭvācamya punarjapet |
taireva bhāṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā snātvā caiva japetpunaḥ  || 2,18.81 ||
ācamya prayato nityaṃ japedaśucidarśane  |
saurānmantrān śaktito vai pāvamānīstu kāmataḥ  || 2,18.82 ||
yadi syātklinnavāsā vai vārimadhyagato japet |
anyathā tu śucau bhūmyāṃ darbheṣu susamāhitaḥ  || 2,18.83 ||
pradakṣiṇaṃ samāvṛtya namaskṛtvā tataḥ kṣitau  |
ācamya ca yathāśāstraṃ śaktyā svādhyāyamācaret || 2,18.84 ||
tataḥ saṃtarpayeddevānṛṣīn pitṛgaṇāṃstathā  |
adāvoṅkāramuccārya namo'nte tarpayāmi vaḥ  || 2,18.85 ||
devān brahmaḥṛṣīṃścaiva tarpayedakṣatodakaiḥ  |
tilodakaiḥ pitṝn bhaktyā svasūtroktavidhānataḥ  || 2,18.86 ||
anvārabdhena savyena pāṇinā dakṣiṇena tu  |
devarṣostarpayeddhīmānudakāñjalibhiḥ pitan  || 2,18.87 ||
yajñopavītī devānāṃ nivītī ṛṣītarpaṇe  |
prācīnāvītī pitrye tu svena tīrthena bhāvataḥ  || 2,18.88 ||
niṣpīḍya snānavastraṃ tu samācamya ca vāgyataḥ  |
svairmantrairarcayeddevān puṣpaiḥ patrairathāmbubhiḥ  || 2,18.89 ||
brahmāṇaṃ śaṅkaraṃ sūryaṃ tathaiva madhusūdanam  |
anyāṃścābhimatān devān bhaktyā cākrodhano'tvaraḥ  || 2,18.90 ||
pradadyādvātha puṣpāṇi sūktena pauruṣeṇa tu  |
āpo vā devatāḥ sarvāstena samyaksamarcitāḥ  || 2,18.91 ||
dhyātvā praṇavapūrvaṃ vai daivatāni samāhitaḥ  |
namaskāreṇa puṣpāṇi vinyasedvai pṛthakpṛthak || 2,18.92 ||
na viṣṇvārādhanātpuṇyaṃ vidyate karma vaidikam  |
tasmādanādimadhyāntaṃ nityamārādhayeddharim  || 2,18.93 ||
tadviṣṇoriti mantreṇa sūktena puruṣeṇa tu  |
naitābhyāṃ sadṛśo mantro sūktena puruṣeṇa tu  |
naitābhyāṃ sadṛśo mantro vedeṣūktaścaturṣvapi  || 2,18.94 ||
nivedayeta svātmānaṃ viṣṇāvamalatejasi  |
tadātmā tanmanāḥ śāntastadviṣṇoriti mantrataḥ  || 2,18.95 ||
athavā devamīśānaṃ bhagavantaṃ sanātanam  |
ārādhayenmahādevaṃ bhāvapūto maheśvaram  || 2,18.96 ||
mantreṇa rudrāgāyatryā praṇavenātha vā punaḥ  |
īśānenātha vā rudraistryambakena samāhitaḥ  || 2,18.97 ||
puṣpaiḥ patrairathādbhirvā candanādyairmaheśvaram  |
uktvā namaḥ śivāyeti mantreṇānena yojayet || 2,18.98 ||
namaskuryānmahādevaṃ ṛtaṃ satyamitiśvaram  |
nivedayīta svātmānaṃ yo brahmāṇamitīśvaram  || 2,18.99 ||
pradakṣiṇaṃ dvijaḥ kuryātpañca brahmāṇi vai japan  |
dhyāyīta devamīśānaṃ vyomamadhyagataṃ śivam  || 2,18.100 ||
athāvalokayedarkaṃ haṃsaḥ suciṣadityṛcā  |
kuryātpañca mahāyajñān gṛhaṃ gatvā samāhitaḥ  || 2,18.101 ||
devayajñaṃ pitṛyajñaṃ bhūtayajñaṃ tathaiva ca  |
mānuṣyaṃ brahmayajñaṃ ca pañca yajñān pracakṣate  || 2,18.102 ||
yadi syāttarpaṇādarvākbrahmayajñaḥ kṛto na hi  |
kṛtvā manuṣyayajñaṃ vai tataḥ svādhyāyamācaret || 2,18.103 ||
agneḥ paścimato deśe bhūtayajñānta eva vā  |
kuśapuñje samāsīnaḥ kuśapāṇiḥ samāhitaḥ  || 2,18.104 ||
śālāgnau laukike vāgnau jale bhūbhyāmathāpivā  |
vaiśvadevaṃ tataḥ kuryāddevayajñaḥ sa vai smṛtaḥ  || 2,18.105 ||
yadi syāllaukike pakvaṃ tato'nnaṃ tatra hūyate  |
śālāgnau tatra devānnaṃ vidhireṣa sanātanaḥ  || 2,18.106 ||
devebhyastu hutādannāccheṣādbhūtabaliṃ haret |
bhūtayajñaḥ sa vai jñeyo bhūtidaḥ sarvadehinām  || 2,18.107 ||
śvabhyaśca śvapacebhyaśca patitādibhya eva ca  |
dadyādbhūmau baliṃ tvannaṃ pakṣibhyo'tha dvijottamaḥ  || 2,18.108 ||
sāyaṃ cānnasya siddhasya patnyamantraṃ baliṃ haret |
bhūtayajñastvayaṃ nityaṃ sāyaṃ prātarvidhīyate  || 2,18.109 ||
ekaṃ tu bhojayedvipraṃ pitṝnuddiśya sattamam  |
nityaśrāddhaṃ taduddiṣṭaṃ pitṛyajño gatipradaḥ  || 2,18.110 ||
uddhṛtya vā yathāśakti kiñcidannaṃ samāhitaḥ  |
vedatattvārthaviduṣe dvijāyaivopapādayet || 2,18.111 ||
pūjayedatithiṃ nityaṃ namasyedarcayeddvijam  |
manovākkarmabhiḥ śāntamāgataṃ svagṛha tataḥ  || 2,18.112 ||
hantakāramathāgraṃ vā bhikṣāṃ vā śaktito dvijaḥ  |
dadyādatithaye nityaṃ budhyeta parameśvaram  || 2,18.113 ||
bhikṣāmāhurgrāsamātramagraṃ tasyāścaturguṇam  |
puṣkalaṃ hantakāraṃ tu taccaturguṇamucyate  || 2,18.114 ||
godohamātraṃ kālaṃ vai pratīkṣyo hyatithiḥ svayam  |
abhyāgatān yathāśakti pūjayedatithiṃ yathā  || 2,18.115 ||
bhikṣāṃ vai bhikṣave dadyādvidhivadbrahmacāriṇe  |
dadyādannaṃ yathāśakti tvarthibhyo lobhavarjitaḥ  || 2,18.116 ||
sarveṣāmapyalābhe tu annaṃ gobhyo nivedayet |
bhuñjīta bandhubhiḥ sārdhaṃ vāgyato'nnamakutsayan  || 2,18.117 ||
akṛtvā tu dvijaḥ pañca mahāyajñān dvijottamāḥ  |
bhṛñjīta cetsa mūḍhātmā tiryagyoniṃ sagacchati  || 2,18.118 ||
vedābhyāso'nvahaṃ śaktyā mahāyajñakriyā kṣamā  |
nāśayatyāśu pāpāni devānāmarcanaṃ tathā  || 2,18.119 ||
yo mohādathavālasyādakṛtvā devatārcanam  |
bhuṅkte sa yāti narakān śūkareṣvabhijāyate  || 2,18.120 ||
tasmātsarvaprayatnena kṛtvā karmāṇi vai dvijāḥ  |
bhuñjīta svajanaiḥ sārdhaṃ sayāti paramāṃ gatim  || 2,18.121 ||

iti śrīkūrmapurāṇe ṣaṭsāhastryāṃ saṃhitāyāmuparivibhāge aṣṭādaśo'dhyāyaḥ


_____________________________________________________________


vyāsa uvāca
prāṅmukho'nnāni bhuñjīta sūryābhimukha eva vā  |
āsīnastvāsane śuddhe bhūmyāṃ pādau nidhāya tu  || 2,19.1 ||
āyuṣyaṃ prāṅmukho bhuṅkte yaśasyaṃ dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ  |
śriyaṃ pratyaṅmukho bhuṅkte ṛtaṃ bhuṅkte udaṅmukhāḥ  || 2,19.2 ||
pañcārdre bhojanaṃ kuryādbhūmau pātraṃ nidhāya tu  |
upavāsena tattulyaṃ manurāha prajāpatiḥ  || 2,19.3 ||
upalipte śucau deśe pādau prakṣālya vai karau  |
ācamyārdrānano'krodhaḥ pañcārdre bhojanaṃ caret || 2,19.4 ||
mahāvyahṛtibhistvannaṃ paridhāyodakena tu  |
amṛtopastaraṇamasītyāpośānakriyāṃ caret || 2,19.5 ||
svāhāpraṇavasaṃyuktāṃ prāṇāyādyāhutiṃ tataḥ  |
apānāya tato hutvā vyānāya tadanantaram  || 2,19.6 ||
udānāya tataḥ kuryātsamānāyeti pañcamīm  |
vijñāya tattvameteṣāṃ juhuyādātmani dvijaḥ  || 2,19.7 ||
śeṣamannaṃ yathākāmaṃ bhuñjītavyaṃ janairyutam  |
dhyātvā tanmanasā devamātmānaṃ vai prajāpatim  || 2,19.8 ||
amṛtāpidhānamasītyupariṣṭādapaḥ pibet |
ācāntaḥ punarācāmedāyaṃ gauriti mantrataḥ  || 2,19.9 ||
drupadāṃ vā trirāvartya sarvapāpapraṇāśanīm  |
prāṇānāṃ granthirasītyālabhedhṛdayaṃ tataḥ  || 2,19.10 ||
ācamyāṅguṣṭhamātreti pādāṅguṣṭhe'tha dakṣiṇe  |
niḥ stravayedhastajalamūrdhvahastaḥ samāhitaḥ  || 2,19.11 ||
hutānumantraṇaṃ kuryātśraddhāyāmiti mantrataḥ  |
athākṣareṇa svātmānaṃ yojayedbrahmaṇeti hi  || 2,19.12 ||
sarveṣāmeva yāgānāmātmayāgaḥ paraḥ smṛtaḥ  |
yo'nena vidhinā kuryātsa yāti brahmaṇaḥ kṣayam  || 2,19.13 ||
yajñopavītī bhuñjīta straggandhālaṅkṛtaḥ śuciḥ  |
sāyaṃprāparnāntarā vai saṃdhyāyāṃ tu viśeṣataḥ  || 2,19.14 ||
nādyātsūryagrahātpūrvamahni sāyaṃ śaśigrahāt |
grahakāle ca nāśnīyātsnātvāśnīyāttu muktayoḥ  || 2,19.15 ||
mukte śaśini bhuñjīta yadi na syānmahāniśā  |
amuktayorastaṅgatayoradyāddṛṣṭvā pare'hani  || 2,19.16 ||
nāśnīyātprekṣamāṇānāmapradāyaiva durmatiḥ  |
na yajñaśiṣṭādandvā na kruddho nānyamānasaḥ  || 2,19.17 ||
ātmārthaṃ bhojanaṃ yasya ratyarthaṃ yasya maithunam  |
vṛtyarthaṃ yasya cādhītaṃ niṣphalaṃ tasya jīvitam  || 2,19.18 ||
yadbhuṅkte veṣṭitaśirā yacca bhuṅkte udaṅmukhaḥ  |
sopānatkaśca yadbhuṅkte sarvaṃ vidyāttadāsuram  || 2,19.19 ||
nārdharātre na madhyāhne nājīrṇe nārdravastradhṛk |
na ca bhinnāsanagato na śayānaḥ sthito'pi vā  || 2,19.20 ||
na bhinnabhājane caiva na bhūmyāṃ na ca pāṇiṣu  |
nocchiṣṭo ghṛtamādadyānna mūrdhānaṃ spṛśedapi  || 2,19.21 ||
na brahma kīrtayan vāpi na niḥ śeṣaṃ na bhāryayā  |
nāndhakāre na cākāśe na ca devālayādiṣu  || 2,19.22 ||
naikavastrastu bhuñjīta na yānaśayanasthitaḥ  |
na pādukānirgato'tha na hasan vilapannapi  || 2,19.23 ||
bhuktvaivaṃ sukhamāsthāya tadannaṃ pariṇāmayet |
itihāsapurāṇābhyāṃ vedārthānupabṛṃhayet || 2,19.24 ||
tataḥ saṃdhyāmupāsīta pūrvoktavidhinā dvijaḥ  |
āsīnastu japeddevīṃ gāyatrīṃ paścimāṃ prati  || 2,19.25 ||
na tiṣṭhati tu yaḥ purvāṃ nāste saṃdhyāṃ tu paścimām  |
sa śūdreṇa samo loke sarvadharmavivarjitaḥ  || 2,19.26 ||
hutvāgniṃ vidhivanmantrairbhuktvā yajñāvaśiṣṭakam  |
sabhṛtyabāndhavajanaḥ svapecchuṣkapado niśi  || 2,19.27 ||
nottarābhimukhaḥ svapyātpaścimābhimukho na ca  |
na cākāśe na nagno vā nāśucirnāsane kvacit || 2,19.28 ||
na śīrṇāyāṃ tu khaṭvāyāṃ śūnyāgāre na caiva hi  |
nānuvaṃśaṃ na pālāśe śayane vā kadācana  || 2,19.29 ||
ityetadakhilenoktamahanyahani vai mayā  |
brāhmaṇānāṃ kṛtyajātamapavargaphalapradam  || 2,19.30 ||
nāstikyādathavālasyātbrāhmaṇo na karoti yaḥ  |
sa yāti narakān ghorān kākayonau ca jāyate  || 2,19.31 ||
nānyo vimuktaye panthā muktvāśramavidhiṃ svakam  |
tasmātkarmāṇi kurvota tuṣṭaye parameṣṭhinaḥ  || 2,19.32 ||

iti śrīkūrmapurāṇe ṣaṭsāhastryāṃ saṃhitāyāmuparivibhāge ekonaviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ


_____________________________________________________________


vyāsa uvāca
atha śrāddhamamāvāsyāṃ prāpya kāryaṃ dvijottamaiḥ  |
piṇḍānvāhāryakaṃ bhaktyā bhuktimuktiphalapradam  || 2,20.1 ||
piṇḍānvāhāryakaṃ śrāddhaṃ kṣīṇe rājani śasyate  |
aparāhne dvijātīnāṃ praśastenāmiṣeṇa ca  || 2,20.2 ||
pratipatprabhṛti hyanyāstithayaḥ kṛṣṇapakṣake  |
caturdaśīṃ varjayitvā praśastā hyuttarottarāḥ  || 2,20.3 ||
amāvāsyāṣṭakāstistraḥ pauṣamāsādiṣu triṣu  |
tistraścānvaṣṭakāḥ puṇyā māghī pañcadaśī tathā  || 2,20.4 ||
trayodaśī maghāyuktā varṣāsu tu viśeṣataḥ  |
śasyāpākaśrāddhakālā nityāḥ proktā dine dine  || 2,20.5 ||
naimittikaṃ tu kartavyaṃ grahaṇe candrasūryayoḥ  |
bāndhavānāṃ ca maraṇe nārakī syādato'nyathā  || 2,20.6 ||
kāmyāni caiva śrāddhāni śasyānte grahaṇādiṣu  |
ayane viṣuve caiva vyatīpāte'pyanantakam  || 2,20.7 ||
saṃkrāntyamakṣayaṃ śrāddhaṃ tathā janmadineṣvapi  |
nakṣatreṣu ca sarveṣu kāryaṃ kāmyaṃ viśeṣataḥ  || 2,20.8 ||
svargaṃ ca labhate kṛtvā kṛttikāsu dvijottamaḥ  |
apatyamatha rohiṇyāṃ saumye tu brahmavarcasam  || 2,20.9 ||
raudrāṇāṃ karmaṇāṃ siddhimārdrāyāṃ śauryameva ca  |
punarvasau tathā bhūmiṃ śriyaṃ puṣye tathaiva ca  || 2,20.10 ||
sarvān kāmāṃstathā sārpe pitrye saubhāgyameva ca  |
aryamṇe tu dhanaṃ vindyātphālgunyāṃ pāpanāśanam  || 2,20.11 ||
jñātiśraiṣṭhyaṃ tathā haste citrāyāṃ ca bahūn sutān  |
vāṇijyasiddhiṃ svātau tu viśākhāsu suvarṇakam  || 2,20.12 ||
maitre bahūni mitrāṇi rājyaṃ śākre tathaiva ca  |
mūle kṛṣiṃ labhedyānasiddhimāpye samudrataḥ  || 2,20.13 ||
sarvān kāmān vaiśvadeve śraiṣṭhyaṃ tu śravaṇe punaḥ  |
śraviṣṭhāyāṃ tathā kāmān vāruṇe ca paraṃ balam  || 2,20.14 ||
ajaikapāde kupyaṃ syādahirbudhne gṛhaṃ śubham  |
revatyāṃ bahavo gāvo hyaśvinyāṃ turagāṃstathā  |
yāmye'tha jīvanaṃ tatsyādyadi śrāddhaṃ prayacchati  || 2,20.15 ||
ādityavāre tvārogyaṃ candre saubhāgyameva ca  |
kauje sarvatra vijayaṃ sarvān kāmān budhasya tu  || 2,20.16 ||
vidyāmabhīṣṭā jīve tu dhanaṃ vai bhārgave punaḥ  |
śamaiśvare labhedāyuḥ pratipatsu sutān śubhān  || 2,20.17 ||
kanyakāṃ vai dvitīyāyāṃ tṛtīyāyāṃ tu vandinaḥ  |
paśūnkṣudrāṃścaturthyāṃ tu pañcamyāṃśobhanān sutān  || 2,20.18 ||
ṣaṣṭyāṃ dyūtaṃ kṛṣiṃ cāpi saptamyāṃ labhate naraḥ  |
aṣṭamyāmapi vāṇijyaṃ labhate śrāddhadaḥ sadā  || 2,20.19 ||
syānnavamyāmekakhuraṃ daśamyāṃ dvikhuraṃ bahu  |
ekādaśyāṃ tathā rūpyaṃ brahmavarcasvinaḥ sutān  || 2,20.20 ||
dvādaśyāṃ jātarūpaṃ ca rajataṃ kupyameva ca  |
jñātiśraiṣṭhyaṃ trayodaśyāṃ caturdaśyāṃ tu kruprajāḥ  |
pañcadaśyāṃ sarvakāmānāpnoti śrāddhadaḥ sadā  || 2,20.21 ||
tasmācchrāddhaṃ na kartavyaṃ caturdaśyāṃ dvijātibhiḥ  |
śastreṇa tu hatānāṃ vai tatra śrāddhaṃ prakalpayet || 2,20.22 ||
dravyabrāhmaṇasaṃpattau na kālaniyamaḥ kṛtaḥ  |
tasmādbhogāpavargārthaṃ śrāddhaṃ kuryurdvijātayaḥ  || 2,20.23 ||
karmārambheṣu sarveṣu kuryādābhyudayaṃ punaḥ  |
putrajanmādiṣu śrāddhaṃ pārvaṇaṃ parvaṇi smṛtam  || 2,20.24 ||
ahanyahani nityaṃ syātkāmyaṃ naimittikaṃ punaḥ  |
ekoddiṣṭādi vijñeyaṃ vṛddhiśrāddhaṃ tu pārvaṇam  || 2,20.25 ||
etatpañcavidhaṃ śrāddhaṃ manunā parikīrtitam  |
yātrāyāṃ ṣaṣṭhamākhyātaṃ tatprayatnena pālayet || 2,20.26 ||
śuddhaye saptamaṃ śrāddhaṃ brahmaṇā paribhāṣitam  |
daivikaṃ cāṣṭamaṃ śrāddhaṃ yatkṛtvā mucyate bhayāt || 2,20.27 ||
saṃdhyārātryorna kartavyaṃ rāhoranyatra darśanāt |
deśānāṃ ca viśeṣeṇa bhavetpuṇyamanantakam  || 2,20.28 ||
gaṅgāyāmakṣayaṃ śrāddhaṃ prayāge'marakaṇṭake  |
gāyanti pitaro gāthāṃ kīrtayanti manīṣiṇaḥ  || 2,20.29 ||
eṣṭavyā bahavaḥ putrāḥ śīlavanto guṇānvitāḥ  |
teṣāṃ tu samavetānāṃ yadyeko'pi gāyāṃ vrajet || 2,20.30 ||
gayāṃ prāpyānuṣaṅgeṇa yadi śrāddhaṃ samācaret |
tāritāḥ pitarastena sa yāti paramāṃ gatim  || 2,20.31 ||
varāhaparvate caiva gaṅgāyāṃ vai viśeṣataḥ  |
vārāṇasyāṃ viśeṣeṇa yatra devaḥ svayaṃ haraḥ  || 2,20.32 ||
gaṅgādvāre prabhāse ca bilvake nīlaparvate  |
kurukṣetre ca kubjāmre bhṛgutuṅge mahālaye  || 2,20.33 ||
kedāre phalgutīrthe ca naimiṣāraṇya eva ca  |
sarasvatyāṃ viśeṣeṇa puṣkareṣu viśeṣataḥ  || 2,20.34 ||
narmadāyāṃ kuśāvarte śrīśaile bhadrakarṇake  |
vetravatyāṃ vipāśāyāṃ godāvaryāṃ viśeṣataḥ  || 2,20.35 ||
evamādiṣu cānyeṣu tīrtheṣu pulineṣu ca  |
nadīnāṃ caiva tīreṣu tuṣyanti pitaraḥ sadā  || 2,20.36 ||
vrīhibhiśca yavairmāṣairadbhirmūlaphalena vā  |
śyāmākaiśca yavaiḥ śākairnovāraiśca priyaṅgubhiḥ  |
gaudhūmaiśca tilairmudgairmāsaṃ prīṇayate pitṝn  || 2,20.37 ||
āmrān pāne ratānikṣūnmṛdvīkāṃśca sadāḍimān  |
vidāryāśca bharaṇḍāśca śrāddhakāle prādapayet || 2,20.38 ||
lājānmadhuyutān dadyātsaktūn śarkarayā saha  |
dadyācchrāddhe prayatnena śṛṅgāṭakakaśerukān  || 2,20.39 ||
dvau māsau matsyamāṃsena trīnmāsān hāriṇenatu  |
aurabhreṇātha caturaḥ śākuneneha pañca tu  || 2,20.40 ||
ṣaṇmāsāṃśchāgamāṃsena pārṣatenātha sapta vai  |
aṣṭāveṇasya māṃsena rauraveṇa navaiva tu  || 2,20.41 ||
daśamāsāṃstu tṛpyanti varāhamahiṣāmiṣaiḥ  |
śaśakūrmaryormāṃsena māsānekādaśaiva tu  || 2,20.42 ||
saṃvatsaraṃ tu gavyena payasā pāyasena tu  |
vārdhroṇasasya māṃsena tṛptirdvādaśavārṣikī  || 2,20.43 ||
kālaśākaṃ mahāśalkaṃ khaṅgalohāmiṣaṃ madhu  |
ānantyāyaiva kalpante munyannāni ca sarvaśaḥ  || 2,20.44 ||
krītvā labdhvā svayaṃ vātha mṛtānāhṛtya vā dvijaḥ  |
dadyācchrāddhe prayatnena tadasyākṣayamucyate  || 2,20.45 ||
pippalīṃ kramukaṃ caiva tathā caiva masūrakam  |
kūṣmāṇḍālābuvārtākān bhūstṛṇaṃ surasaṃ tathā  || 2,20.46 ||
kusumbhapiṇḍamūlaṃ vai tandulīyakameva ca  |
rājamāṣāṃstathā kṣīraṃ māhiṣaṃ ca vivarjayet || 2,20.47 ||
kodravān kovidārāṃścapālakyānmaricāṃstathā  |
varjayetsarvayatnena śrāddhakāle dvijottamaḥ  || 2,20.48 ||

iti śrīkūrmapurāṇe ṣaṭsāhastryāṃ saṃhitāyāmuparivibhāge viśo'dhyāyaḥ


_____________________________________________________________


vyāsa uvāca
snātvā yathoktaṃ saṃtarpya pitṝṃścandrakṣaye dvijaḥ  |
piṇḍānvāhāryakaṃ śrāddhaṃ kuryātsaumyamanāḥ śuciḥ  || 2,21.1 ||
pūrvameva parīkṣeta brāhmaṇaṃ vedapāragam  |
tīrthaṃ tadhavyakavyānāṃ pradāne cātithiḥ smṛtaḥ  || 2,21.2 ||
ye somapā virajaso dharmajñāḥ śāntacetasaḥ  |
vratino niyamasthāśca ṛtukālābhigāminaḥ  || 2,21.3 ||
pañcāgnirapyadhīyāno yajurvedavideva ca  |
bahvṛcaśca trisauparṇastrimadhurvātha yo bhavet || 2,21.4 ||
triṇāciketacchandogo jyeṣṭhasāmaga eva ca  |
atharvaśiraso'dhyetā rudrādhyāyī viśeṣataḥ  || 2,21.5 ||
agnihotraparo vidvānnyāyavicca ṣaḍaṅgavit |
mantrabrāhmaṇaviccaiva yaśca syāddharmapāṭhakaḥ  || 2,21.6 ||
ṛṣivratī ṛṣīkaśca tathā dvādaśavārṣikaḥ  |
brahmadeyānusaṃtāno garbhaśuddhaḥ sahasradaḥ  || 2,21.7 ||
cāndrāyaṇavratacaraḥ satyavādī purāṇavit |
gurudevāgnipūjāsu prasakto jñānatatparaḥ  || 2,21.8 ||
vimuktaḥ sarvato dhīro brahmabhūto dvijottamaḥ  |
mahādevārcanarato vaiṣṇavaḥ paṅktipāvanaḥ  || 2,21.9 ||
ahiṃsānirato nityamapratigrahaṇastathā  |
satriṇo dānaniratā vijñeyāḥ paṅktipāvanāḥ  || 2,21.10 ||
yuvānaḥ śrotriyāḥ svasthā mahāyajñaparāyaṇāḥ  |
sāvitrījāpaniratā brāhmaṇāḥ paṅktipāvanāḥ  || 2,21.11 ||
kulīnāḥ śrutavantaśca śīlavantastapasvinaḥ  |
agnicitsnātakā viprā vijñeyāḥ paṅktipāvanāḥ  || 2,21.12 ||
mātāpitrorhite yuktaḥ prātaḥ snāyī tathā dvijaḥ  |
adhyātmavinmunirdānto vijñeyaḥ paṅktipāvanaḥ  || 2,21.13 ||
jñānaniṣṭho mahāyogī vedāntārthavicintakaḥ  |
śraddhāluḥ śrāddhanirato brāhmaṇaḥ paṅktipāvanaḥ  || 2,21.14 ||
vedavidyārataḥ snāto brahmacaryaparaḥ sadā  |
atharvaṇo mumukṣuśca brāhmaṇaḥ paṅktipāvanaḥ  || 2,21.15 ||
asamānapravarako hyasagotrastathaiva ca  |
asaṃbandhī ca vijñeyo brāhmaṇaḥ paṅktipāvanaḥ  || 2,21.16 ||
bhojayedyoginaṃ pūrvaṃ tattvajñānarataṃ yatim  |
alābhe naiṣṭhikaṃ dāntamupakurvāṇakaṃ tathā  || 2,21.17 ||
tadalābhe gṛhasthaṃ tu mumukṣuṃ saṅgavarjitam  |
sarvālābhe sādhakaṃ vā gṛhasthamapi bhojayet || 2,21.18 ||
prakṛterguṇatattvajño yasyāśnāti yatirhaviḥ  |
phalaṃ vedavidāṃ tasya sahasrādatiricyate  || 2,21.19 ||
tasmādyatnena yogīndramīśvarajñānatatparam  |
bhojayedhavyakavyeṣu alābhāditarān dvijān  || 2,21.20 ||
eṣa vai prathamaḥ kalpaḥ pridāne havyakavyayoḥ  |
anukalpastvayaṃ jñeyaḥ sadā sadbhiranuṣṭhitaḥ  || 2,21.21 ||
mātāmahaṃ mātulaṃ ca svastrīyaṃ śvaśuraṃ gurum  |
dauhitraṃ viṭpatiṃ bandhumṛtvigyājyau ca bhojayet || 2,21.22 ||
na śrāddhe bhojayenmitraṃ dhanaiḥ kāryo'sya saṃgrahaḥ  |
paiśācī dakṣiṇā sā hi naivāmutra phalapradā  || 2,21.23 ||
kāma śrāddhe'rcayenmitraṃ nābhirūpamapi tvarim  |
dviṣatā hi havirbhuktaṃ bhavati pretya niṣphalam  || 2,21.24 ||
brāhmaṇo hyanadhīyānastṛṇāgniriva śāmyati  |
tasmai havyaṃ na dātavyaṃ na hi bhasmani hūyate  || 2,21.25 ||
yatheriṇe bījamuptvā na vaptā labhate phalam  |
tathānṛce havirdattvā na dātā labhate phalam  || 2,21.26 ||
yāvato grasate piṇḍān havyakavyeṣvamantravit |
tāvato grasate pretya dīptān sthūlāṃstvayoguḍān  || 2,21.27 ||
api vidyākulairyuktā hīnavṛttā narādhamāḥ  |
yatraite bhuñjate havyaṃ tadbhavedāsura dvijāḥ  || 2,21.28 ||
yasya vedaśca vedī ca vicchidyete tripūruṣam  |
sa vai durbrāhmaṇo nārhaḥ śrāddhādiṣu kadācana  || 2,21.29 ||
śūdrapreṣyo bhṛto rājño vṛṣalo grāmayājakaḥ  |
badhabandhopajīvī ca ṣaḍete brahmabandhavaḥ  || 2,21.30 ||
dattānuyogān vṛtyarthaṃ patitānmanurabravīt |
vedavikrāyiṇo hyete śrāddhādiṣu vigarhitāḥ  || 2,21.31 ||
śrutivikrayiṇo ye tu parapūrvāsamudbhavāḥ  |
asamānān yājayanti patitāste prakīrtitāḥ  || 2,21.32 ||
asaṃskṛtādhyāpakā ye bhṛtyā vādhyāpayanti ye  |
adhīyate tathā vedān patitāste prakīrtitāḥ  || 2,21.33 ||
vṛddhaśrāvakanirgranthāḥ pañcarātravido janāḥ  |
kāpālikāḥ pāśupatāḥ pāṣaṇḍā ye ca tadvidhāḥ  || 2,21.34 ||
yasyāśnanti havīṃṣyete durātmānastu tāmasāḥ  |
na tasya tadbhavecchrāddhaṃ pretya ceha phalapradam  || 2,21.35 ||
anāśramo yo dvijaḥ syādāśramī vā nirarthakaḥ  |
mithyāśramī ca te viprā vijñeyāḥ paṅktidūṣakāḥ  || 2,21.36 ||
duścarmā kunakhī kuṣṭhī śvitrī ca śyāvadantakaḥ  |
viddhaprajananaścaiva stenaḥ klībo'tha nāstikaḥ  || 2,21.37 ||
madyapo vṛṣalīsakto vīrahā didhiṣūpatiḥ  |
āgāradāhī kuṇḍāśī somavikrayiṇo dvijāḥ  || 2,21.38 ||
parivettā tathā hiṃstraḥ parivittirnirākṛtiḥ  |
paunarbhavaḥ kusīdī ca tathā nakṣatradarśakaḥ  || 2,21.39 ||
gītavāditranirato vyādhitaḥ kāṇa eva ca  |
hīnāṅgaścātiriktāṅgo hyavakīrṇistathaiva ca  || 2,21.40 ||
kanyādūṣī kuṇḍagolau abhiśasto'tha devalaḥ  |
mitradhrukpiśunaścaiva nityaṃ bhāryānuvartakaḥ  || 2,21.41 ||
mātāpitrorgurostyāgī dāratyāgī tathaiva ca  |
gotrabhidbhraṣṭaśaucaśca kāṇḍaspṛṣṭastathaiva ca  || 2,21.42 ||
anapatyaḥ kūṭasākṣī yācako raṅgajīvakaḥ  |
samudrayāyī kṛtahā tathā samayabhedakaḥ  || 2,21.43 ||
devanindāparaścaiva vedanindāratastathā  |
dvijanindārataścaite varjyāḥ śrāddhādikarmasu  || 2,21.44 ||
kṛtaghnaḥ piśunaḥ krūro nāstiko vedanindakaḥ  |
mitradhrukkuhakaścaiva viśeṣātpaṅktidūṣakāḥ  || 2,21.45 ||
sarve punarabhojyānnāstvadānārhāśca karmasu  |
brahmabhāvanirastāśca varjanīyāḥ prayatnataḥ  || 2,21.46 ||
śūdrānnarasapuṣṭāṅgaḥ saṃdhyopāsanavarjitaḥ  |
mahāyajñavihīnaśca brāhmaṇaḥ paṅktidūṣakaḥ  || 2,21.47 ||
adhītanāśanaścaiva snānahomavivarjitaḥ  |
tāmaso rājasaścaiva brāhmaṇaḥ paṅktidūṣakaḥ  || 2,21.48 ||
bahunātra kimuktena vihitān ye na kurvate  |
ninditānācarantyete varjanīyāḥ prayatnataḥ  || 2,21.49 ||

iti śrīkūrmapurāṇe ṣaṭsāhastryāṃ saṃhitāyāmuparivibhāge ekaviśo'dhyāya
