īṇṛEE ṇīCḥṭZūḻṣṣīṅE ZEīCḥEṇ̱

vyāsa uvāca
gomayenodakairbhūmiṃ śodhayitvā samāhitaḥ  |
saṃnipātya dvijān sarvān sādhubhiḥ saṃnimantrayet || 2,22.1 ||
śvo bhaviṣyati me śrāddhaṃ pūrvedyurabhipūjya ca  |
asaṃbhave paredyurvā yathoktairlakṣaṇairyutān  || 2,22.2 ||
tasya te pitaraḥ śrutvā śrāddhakālamupasthitam  |
anyonyaṃ manasā dhyātvā saṃpatanti manojavāḥ  || 2,22.3 ||
brāhmaṇaiste sahāśnanti pitaro hyantarikṣagāḥ  |
vāyubhūtāstu tiṣṭhanti bhuktvā yānti parāṃ gatim  || 2,22.4 ||
āmantritāśca te viprāḥ śrāddhakāla upasthite  |
vaseyurniyatāḥ sarve brahmacaryaparāyaṇāḥ  || 2,22.5 ||
akrodhano'tvaro'mattaḥ satyavādī samāhitaḥ  |
bhāraṃ maithunamadhvānaṃ śrāddhakṛdvarjayejjapam  || 2,22.6 ||
āmantrito brāhmaṇo vā yo'nyasmai kurute kṣaṇam  |
sa yāti narakaṃ ghoraṃ sūkaratvāṃ prāyāti ca  || 2,22.7 ||
āmantrayitvā yo mohādanyaṃ cāmantrayeddvijam  |
sa tasmādadhikaḥ pāpī viṣṭhākīṭo'bhijāyate  || 2,22.8 ||
śrāddhe nimantrito vipro maithunaṃ yo'dhigacchati  |
brahmahatyāmavāpnoti tiryagyonau ca jāyate  || 2,22.9 ||
nimantritastu yo vipro hyadhvānaṃ yāti durmatiḥ  |
bhavanti pitarastasya taṃ māsaṃ pāṃśubhojanāḥ  || 2,22.10 ||
nimantritastu yaḥ śrāddhe prakuryātkalahaṃ dvijaḥ  |
bhavanti tasya tanmāsaṃ pitaro malabhojanāḥ  || 2,22.11 ||
tasmānnimantritaḥ śrāddhe niyatātmā bhaveddvijaḥ  |
akrodhanaḥ śaucaparaḥ kartā caiva jitendriyaḥ  || 2,22.12 ||
śvobhūte dakṣiṇāṃ gatvā diśaṃ darbhān samāhitaḥ  |
samūlānāharedvāri dakṣiṇāgrān sunirmalān  || 2,22.13 ||
dakṣiṇāpravaṇaṃ snigdhaṃ vibhaktaṃ śubhalakṣaṇam  |
śuciṃ deśaṃ viviktaṃ ca gomayenopalepayet || 2,22.14 ||
nadītīreṣu tīrtheṣu svabhūmau caiva sānuṣu  |
vivikteṣu ca tuṣyanti dattena pitaraḥ sadā  || 2,22.15 ||
pārakye bhūmibhāge tu pitṝṇāṃ naiva nirvapet |
svāmibhistadvihanyeta mohādyatkriyate naraiḥ  || 2,22.16 ||
aṭavyaḥ parvatāḥ puṇyāstīrthānyāyatanāni ca  |
sarvāṇyasvāmikānyāhurna hi teṣu parigrahaḥ  || 2,22.17 ||
tilān pravikirettatra sarvato bandhayedajān  |
asuropahataṃ sarvaṃ tilaiḥ śuddhyatyajena vā  || 2,22.18 ||
tato'nnaṃ bahusaṃskāraṃ naikavyañjanamacyutam  |
coṣyapeyasamṛddhaṃ ca yathāśaktyā prakalpayet || 2,22.19 ||
tato nivṛtte madhyāhne luptalomanakhān dvijān  |
abhigamya yathāmārgaṃ prayaccheddantadhāvanam  || 2,22.20 ||
tailamabhyañjanaṃ snānaṃ snānīyaṃ ca pṛthagvidham  |
pātrairaudumbarairdadyādvaiśvadaivatyapūrvakam  || 2,22.21 ||
tataḥ snātvā nivṛttebhyaḥ pratyutthāyakṛtāñjaliḥ  |
pādyamācamanīyaṃ ca saṃprayacchedyathākramam  || 2,22.22 ||
ye cātra viśvedevānāṃ viprāḥ pūrvaṃ nimantritāḥ  |
prāṅmukhānyāsanānyeṣāṃ tridarbhopahitāni ca  || 2,22.23 ||
dakṣiṇāmukhayuktāni pitṝṇāmāsanāni ca  |
dakṣiṇāgraikadarbhāṇi prokṣitāni tilodakaiḥ  || 2,22.24 ||
teṣūpaveśayedetānāsanaṃ spṛśya sa dvijam  |
āsadhvamiti saṃjalpanāsanāste pṛthakpṛthak || 2,22.25 ||
dvau daive prāṅmukhau pitrye trayaścodaṅmukhāstathā  |
ekaikaṃ vā bhavettatra devamātāmaheṣvapi  || 2,22.26 ||
satkriyāṃ deśakālau ca śaucaṃ brāhmaṇasaṃpadam  |
pañcaitān vistaro hanti tasmānneheta vistaram  || 2,22.27 ||
api vā bhojayedekaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ vedapāragam  |
śrutaśīlādisaṃpannamalakṣaṇavivarjitam  || 2,22.28 ||
uddhṛtya pātre cānnaṃ tatsarvasmātprakṛtātpunaḥ  |
devatāyatane cāsmai nivedyānyatpravartayet || 2,22.29 ||
prāsyedagnau tadannaṃ tu dadyādvā brahmacāriṇe  |
tasmādekamapi śreṣṭhaṃ vidvāṃsaṃ bhojayeddvijam  || 2,22.30 ||
bhikṣuko brahmacārī vā bhojanārthamupasthitaḥ  |
upaviṣṭeṣu yaḥ śrāddhe kāmaṃ tamapi bhojayet || 2,22.31 ||
atithiryasya nāśnāti na tacchrāddhaṃ praśasyate  |
tasmātprayatnācchrāddheṣu pūjyā hyatithayo dvijaiḥ  || 2,22.32 ||
ātithyarahite śrāddhe bhuñjate ye dvijātayaḥ  |
kākayoniṃ vrajantyete dātā caiva na saṃśayaḥ  || 2,22.33 ||
hīnāṅgaḥ patitaḥ kuṣṭhī vraṇī pukkasanāstikau  |
kukkuṭāḥ śūkarāḥ śvāno varjyāḥ śrāddheṣu dūrataḥ  || 2,22.34 ||
bībhatsumaśuciṃ nagnaṃ mattaṃ dhūrtaṃ rajasvalām  |
nīlakāṣāyavasanaṃ pāṣaṇḍāṃśca vivarjayet || 2,22.35 ||
yattatra kriyate karma paitṛkaṃ brāhmaṇān prati  |
tatsarvameva kartavyaṃ vaiśvadaivatyapūrvakam  || 2,22.36 ||
yathopaviṣṭān sarvāṃstānalaṅkuryādvibhūṣaṇaḥ  |
stragdāmabhiḥ śiroveṣṭairdhūpavāso'nulepanaiḥ  || 2,22.37 ||
tatastvāvāhayeddevān brāhmaṇānāmanujñayā  |
udaṅmukho yathānyāyaṃ viśve devāsa ityṛcā  || 2,22.38 ||
dve pavitre gṛhītvātha bhājane kṣālite punaḥ  |
śaṃ no devyā jalaṃ kṣiptvā yavo'sīti yavāṃstathā  || 2,22.39 ||
yā divyā iti mantraṇa haste tvarghaṃ vinikṣipet |
pradadyādgandhamālyāni dhūpādīni ca śaktitaḥ  || 2,22.40 ||
apasavyaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā pitṝṇāṃ dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ  |
āvāhanaṃ tataḥ kuryāduśantastvetyṛcā budhaḥ  || 2,22.41 ||
āvāhya tadanujñāto japedāyantu nastataḥ  |
śaṃ no devyodakaṃ pātre tilo'sīti tilāṃstathā  || 2,22.42 ||
kṣiptvā cārghaṃ yathāpūrvaṃ dattvā hasteṣu vai punaḥ  |
saṃstravāṃśca tataḥ sarvān pātre kuryātsamāhitaḥ  |
pitṛbhyaḥ sthānametena nyubjaṃ pātraṃ nidhāpayet || 2,22.43 ||
agnau kariṣyetyādāya pṛcchatyannaṃ ghṛtaplutam  |
kuruṣvetyabhyanujñāto juhuyādupavītavān  || 2,22.44 ||
yajñopavītinā homaḥ kartavyaḥ kuśapāṇinā  |
prācīnāvītinā pitryaṃ vaiśvadevaṃ tu homavat || 2,22.45 ||
dakṣiṇaṃ pātayejjānuṃ devān paricaran pumān  |
pitṛṇāṃ paricaryāsu pātayeditaraṃ tathā  || 2,22.46 ||
somāya vai pitṛmate svadhā nama iti bruvan  |
agnaye kavyavāhanāya svadheti juhuyāttataḥ  || 2,22.47 ||
agnyabhāve tu viprasya pāṇāvevopapādayet |
mahādevāntike vātha goṣṭhe vā susamāhitaḥ  || 2,22.48 ||
tatastairabhyanujñāto gatvā vai dakṣiṇāṃ diśam  |
gomayenopatipyorvoṃ sthānaṃ kṛtvā tu saikatam  || 2,22.49 ||
maṇḍalaṃ caturastraṃ vā dakṣiṇāvanataṃ śubham  |
trirullikhettasya madhyaṃ darbheṇaikena caiva hi  || 2,22.50 ||
tataḥ saṃstīrya tatsthāne darbhān vaidakṣiṇāgrakān  |
trīn piṇḍānnirvapettatra haviḥ śeṣātsamāhitaḥ  || 2,22.51 ||
nyupya piṇḍāṃstu taṃ hastaṃ nimṛjyāllepabhāginām  |
teṣu darbheṣvathācamya trirāyamya śanairasūn  |
tadannaṃ tu namaskuryātpitṝneva ca mantravit || 2,22.52 ||
udakaṃ ninayeccheṣaṃ śanaiḥ piṇḍāntike punaḥ  |
avajighrecca tān piṇḍān yathānyuptān samāhitaḥ  || 2,22.53 ||
atha piṇḍāvaśiṣṭānnaṃ vidhinā bhojayeddvijān  |
māṃsānyapūpān vividhān dadyātkṛsarapāyasam  || 2,22.54 ||
sūpaśākaphalānīkṣūn payo dadhi ghṛtaṃ madhu  |
annaṃ caiva yathākāmaṃ vividhaṃ bhakṣyapeyakam  || 2,22.55 ||
yadyadiṣṭaṃ dvijendrāṇāṃ tatsarvaṃ vinivedayet |
dhānyāṃstilāṃśca vividhān śarkarā vividhāstathā  || 2,22.56 ||
uṣṇamannaṃ dvijātibhyo dātavyaṃ śreya icchatā  |
anyatra phalamūlebhyaḥ pānakebhyastathaiva ca  || 2,22.57 ||
nāśrūṇi pātayejjātu na kupyennānṛtaṃ vadet |
na pādena spṛśedannaṃ na caitadavadhūnayet || 2,22.58 ||
krodhena caiva yatdattaṃ yadbhuktaṃ tvarayā punaḥ  |
yātudhānā vilumpanti jalpatā copapāditam  || 2,22.59 ||
svinnagātro na tiṣṭheta sannidhau tu dvijanmanām  |
na cātra śyenakākādīn pakṣiṇaḥ pratiṣedhayet |
tadrūpāḥ pitarastatra samāyānti bubhukṣavaḥ  || 2,22.60 ||
na dadyāttatra hastena pratyakṣalavaṇaṃ tathā  |
na cāyasena pātreṇa na caivāśraddhayā punaḥ  || 2,22.61 ||
kāñcanena tu pātreṇa rājataudumbareṇa vā  |
dattamakṣayatāṃ yāti khaḍgena ca viśeṣataḥ  || 2,22.62 ||
pātre tu mṛṇmaye yo vai śrāddhe bhojayate pitan  |
sa yāti narakaṃ ghoraṃ bhoktā caiva purodhasaḥ  || 2,22.63 ||
na paṅktyāṃ viṣamaṃ dadyānna yācenna ca dāpayet |
yācitā dāpitā dātā narakān yānti dāruṇān  || 2,22.64 ||
bhuñjīran vāgyatāḥ śiṣṭā na brūyuḥ prākṛtān guṇān  |
tāvaddhi pitaro'śnanti yāvannoktā havirguṇāḥ  || 2,22.65 ||
nāgrāsanopaviṣṭastu bhuñjota prathamaṃ dvijaḥ  |
bahūnāṃ paśyatāṃ so'jñaḥ paṅktyā harati kilbiṣam  || 2,22.66 ||
na kiñcidvarjayecchrāddhe niyuktastu dvijottamaḥ  |
na māṃsaṃ pratiṣedheta na cānyasyānnamīkṣayet || 2,22.67 ||
yo nāśnāti dvijo māṃsaṃ niyuktaḥ pitṛkarmaṇi  |
sa pretya paśutāṃ yāti saṃbhavānekaviṃśatim  || 2,22.68 ||
svādhyāyaṃ śrāvayedeṣāṃ dharmaśāstrāṇi caiva hi  |
itihāsapurāṇāni śrāddhakalpāṃśca śobhanān  || 2,22.69 ||
tato'nnamutsṛjedbhukte agrato vikiran bhuvi  |
pṛṣṭvā tṛptāḥ stha ityevaṃ tṛptānācāmayettataḥ  || 2,22.70 ||
ācāntānanujānīyādabhito ramyatāmiti  |
svadhāstviti ca taṃ brūyurbrāhmaṇāstadanantaram  || 2,22.71 ||
tato bhuktavatāṃ teṣāmannaśeṣaṃ nivedayet |
yathā brūyustathā kuryādanujñātastu vai dvijaiḥ  || 2,22.72 ||
pitrye svadita ityeva vākyaṃ goṣṭheṣu sūnṛtam  |
saṃpannamityabhyudaye daive rocata ityapi  || 2,22.73 ||
visṛjya brāhmaṇāṃstān vai daivapūrvaṃ tu vāgyataḥ  |
dakṣiṇāṃ diśamākāṅkṣanyācetemān varān pitṝn  || 2,22.74 ||
dātāro no'bhivardhantāṃ vedāḥ saṃtatireva ca  |
śraddhā ca no mā vyagamadbahudeyaṃ ca nosttviti  || 2,22.75 ||
piṇḍāṃstu go'javiprebhyo dadyādagnau jale'pi vā  |
madhyamaṃ tu tataḥ piṇḍamadyātpatnī sutārthinī  || 2,22.76 ||
prakṣālya hastāvācamya jñātīn śeṣeṇa toṣayet |
jñātiṣvapi catuṣṭeṣu svān bhṛtyān bhojayottataḥ  |
paścātsvayaṃ ca patnībhiḥ śeṣamannaṃ samācaret || 2,22.77 ||
nodvāsayettaducchiṣṭaṃ yāvannāstaṅgato raviḥ  |
brahmacārī bhavetāṃ tu dampatī rajanīṃ tu tām  || 2,22.78 ||
dattvā śrāddhaṃ tathā bhuktvā sevate yastu maithunam  |
mahārauravamāsādya kīṭayoniṃ vrajetpunaḥ  || 2,22.79 ||
śucirakrodhanaḥ śāntaḥ satyavādī samāhitaḥ  |
svādhyāyaṃ ca tathādhvānaṃ kartā bhoktā ca varjayet || 2,22.80 ||
śrāddhaṃ bhuktvā paraśrāddhaṃ bhuñjate ye dvijātayaḥ  |
mahāpātikibhistulyā yānti te narakān bahūn  || 2,22.81 ||
eṣa vo vihitaḥ samyakśrāddhakalpaḥ sanātanaḥ  |
āmena vartayennityamudāsīno'tha tattvavit || 2,22.82 ||
anagniradhvago vāpi tathaiva vyasanānvitaḥ  |
āmaśrāddhaṃ dvijaḥ kuryādvidhijñaḥ śraddhayānvitaḥ  |
tenāgnau karaṇaṃ kuryātpiṇḍāṃstenaiva nirvapet || 2,22.83 ||
yo'nena vidhinā śrāddhaṃ kuryātsaṃyatamānasaḥ  |
vyapetakalpaṣo nityaṃ yogināṃ vartate padam  || 2,22.84 ||
tasmātsarvaprayatnena śrāddhaṃ kuryāddvijottamaḥ  |
ārādhito bhavedīśastena samyaksanātanaḥ  || 2,22.85 ||
api mūlairphalairvāpi prakuryānnirdhano dvijaḥ  |
tilodakaistarpayedvā pitṝn snātvā samāhitaḥ  || 2,22.86 ||
na jīvatpitṛko dadyāddhomāntaṃ cābhidhīyate  |
yeṣāṃ vāpi pitā dadyātteṣāṃ caike pracakṣate  || 2,22.87 ||
pitā pitāmahaścaiva tathaiva prapitāmahaḥ  |
yo yasya mriyate tasmai deyaṃ nānyasya tena tu  || 2,22.88 ||
bhojayedvāpi jīvantaṃ yathākāmaṃ tu bhaktitaḥ  |
na jīvantamatikramya dadāti śrūyate śrutiḥ  || 2,22.89 ||
dvyāmuṣyāyaṇiko dadyādbījikṣetrikayoḥ samam  |
ṛkyādardhaṃ samādadyānniyogotpādito yadi  || 2,22.90 ||
aniyuktaḥ suto yaśca śulkato jāyate tviha  |
pradadyādbījine piṇḍaṃ kṣetriṇe tu tato'nyathā  || 2,22.91 ||
dvau piṇḍau nirvapettābhyāṃ kṣetriṇe bījine tathā  |
kīrtayedatha caikasmin bījinaṃ kṣetriṇaṃ tataḥ  || 2,22.92 ||
mṛtāhani tu kartavyamekodiṣṭaṃ vidhānataḥ  |
aśauce sve parikṣīṇe kāmyaṃ vai kāmataḥ punaḥ  || 2,22.93 ||
pūrvāhne caiva kartavyaṃ śrāddhamabhyudayārthinā  |
devavatsarvameva syādyavaiḥ kāryā tilakriyā  || 2,22.94 ||
darbhāśca ṛjavaḥ kāryā yugmān vai bhojayeddvijān  |
nāndīmukhāstu pitaraḥ prīyantāmiti vācayet || 2,22.95 ||
mātṛśrāddhaṃ tu pūrvaṃ syātpitṝṇāṃ syādanantaram  |
tato mātāmahānāṃ tu vṛddhau śrāddhatrayaṃ smṛtam  || 2,22.96 ||
davapūrvaṃ pradadyādvai na kuryādapradakṣiṇam  |
prāṅmukho nirvapetpiṇḍānupavītī samāhitaḥ  || 2,22.97 ||
pūrvaṃ tu mātaraḥ pūjyā bhaktyā vai sagaṇeśvarāḥ  |
sthaṇḍileṣu vicitreṣu pratimāsu dvijātiṣu  || 2,22.98 ||
puṣperdhūpaiśca naivedyairgandhādyairbhūṣaṇairapi  |
pūjayitvā mātṛgaṇaṃ kūryācchrāddhatrayaṃ budhaḥ  || 2,22.99 ||
akṛtvā mātṛyāgaṃ tu yaḥ śrāddhaṃ pariveṣayet |
tasya krodhasamāviṣṭā hiṃsāmicchanti mātaraḥ  || 2,22.100 ||

iti śrīkūrmapurāṇe ṣaṭsāhastryāṃ saṃhitāyāmuparivibhāge dvāviśo'dhyāyaḥ

īṇṛEE ṇīCḥṭZūḻṣṣīṅE ZEīCḥEṇ̱

vyāsa uvāca
daśāhaṃ prāhurāśaucaṃ sapiṇḍeṣu vipaścitaḥ  |
mṛteṣu vātha jāteṣu brāhmaṇānāṃ dvijottamāḥ  || 2,23.1 ||
nityāni caiva karmāṇi kāmyāni ca viśeṣataḥ  |
nakuryādvihitaṃ kiñcitsvādhyāyaṃ manasāpica  || 2,23.2 ||
śucīnakrodhanān bhūmyān śālāgnau bhāvayeddvijān  |
śuṣkānnena phalairvāpi vaitānaṃ juhuyāttathā  || 2,23.3 ||
na spṛśeyurimānanye na ca tebhyaḥ samāharet |
caturthe pañcame vāhni saṃsparśaḥ kathito budhaiḥ  || 2,23.4 ||
sūtake tu sapiṇḍānāṃ saṃsparśo na praduṣyati  |
sūtakaṃ sūtikāṃ caiva varjayitvā nṛṇāṃ punaḥ  || 2,23.5 ||
adhīyānastathā yajvā vedavicca pitā bhavet |
saṃspṛśyāḥ sarva evaite snānānmātā daśāhataḥ  || 2,23.6 ||
daśāhaṃ nirguṇe proktamaśaucaṃ cātinirguṇe  |
ekadvitriguṇairyuktaṃ catustryekadinaiḥ śuciḥ  || 2,23.7 ||
daśāhāttu paraṃ samyagadhīyīta juhoti ca  |
caturthe tasya saṃsparśaṃ manurāha prajāpatiḥ  || 2,23.8 ||
kriyāhīnasya mūrkhasya mahārogiṇa eva ca  |
yatheṣṭācaraṇasyāhurmaraṇāntamaśauvakam  || 2,23.9 ||
trirātraṃ daśarātraṃ vā brāhmaṇānāmaśaucakam  |
prāksaṃskārāttrirātraṃ syāttasmādūrdhvaṃ daśāhakam  || 2,23.10 ||
ūnadvivārṣike prete mātāpitrostadiṣyate  |
trirātreṇa śucistvanyo yadi hyatyantanirguṇaḥ  || 2,23.11 ||
adantajātamaraṇe pitrorekāhamiṣyate  |
jātadante trirātraṃ syādyadi syātāṃ tu nirguṇau  || 2,23.12 ||
ādantajananātsadya ācaulādekarātrakam  |
trirātramaupanayanātsapiṇḍānāmudāhṛtam  || 2,23.13 ||
jātamātrasya bālasya yadi syānmaraṇaṃ pituḥ  |
mātuśca sūtakaṃ tatsyātpitā syātspṛśya eva ca  || 2,23.14 ||
sadyaḥ śaucaṃ sapiṇḍānāṃ kartavyaṃ sodarasya ca  |
ūrdhvaṃ daśāhādekāhaṃ sodaro yadi nirguṇaḥ  || 2,23.15 ||
athordhvaṃ dantajananātsapiṇḍānāmaśaucakam  |
ekarātraṃ nirguṇānāṃ cailādūrdhvaṃ trirātrakam  || 2,23.16 ||
adantajātamaraṇaṃ saṃbhavedyadi sattamāḥ  |
ekarātraṃ sapiṇḍānāṃ yadi te'tyantanirguṇāḥ  || 2,23.17 ||
vratādeśātsapiṇḍānāmarvāksnānaṃ vidhīyate  |
sarveṣāmeva guṇināmūrdhvaṃ tu viṣamaṃ punaḥ  || 2,23.18 ||
arvākṣaṇmāsataḥ strīṇāṃ yadi syādgarbhasaṃstravaḥ  |
tadā māsasamaistāsāmaśaucaṃ divasaiḥ smṛtam  || 2,23.19 ||
tata ūrdhvaṃ tu patane strīṇāṃ dvādaśarātrikam  |
sadyaḥ śaucaṃ sapiṇḍānāṃ garbhastrāvācca vā tataḥ  || 2,23.20 ||
garbhacyutāvahorātraṃ sapiṇḍe'tyantanirguṇe  |
yatheṣṭācaraṇe jñātau trirātramiti niścayaḥ  || 2,23.21 ||
yadi syātsūtake sūtirmaraṇe vā mṛtirbhavet |
śeṣeṇaiva bhavecchuddhirahaḥ śeṣe trirātrakam  || 2,23.22 ||
maraṇotpattiyoge tu maraṇācchuddhiriṣyate  |
aghavṛddhimadāśaucamūrghvaṃ cettena śudhyati  || 2,23.23 ||
atha cetpañcamīrātrimatītya parato bhavet |
aghavṛddhimadāśaucaṃ tadā pūrveṇa śudhyati  || 2,23.24 ||
deśāntaragataṃ śrutvā sūtakaṃ śāvameva tu  |
tāvadaprayato martyo yāvaccheṣaḥ samāpyate  || 2,23.25 ||
atīte sūtake proktaṃ sapiṇḍānāṃ trirātrakam  |
tathaiva maraṇe snānamūrdhvaṃ saṃvatsarādyadi  || 2,23.26 ||
vedāntaviccādhīyāno yo'gnimān vṛttikarṣitaḥ  |
sadyaḥ śaucaṃ bhavettasya sarvāvasthāsu sarvadā  || 2,23.27 ||
strīṇāmasaṃskṛtānāṃ tu pradānātpūrvataḥ sadā  |
sapiṇḍānāṃ trirātraṃ syātsaṃskāre bhartureva hi  || 2,23.28 ||
ahastvadattakanyānāmaśaucaṃ maraṇe smṛtam  |
ūnadvivarṣānmaraṇe sadyaḥ śaucamudāhṛtam  || 2,23.29 ||
ādantātsodare sadya ācaulādekarātrakam  |
āpradānāttrirātraṃ syāddaśarātramataḥ param  || 2,23.30 ||
mātāmahānāṃ maraṇe trirātraṃ syādaśaucakam  |
ekodakānāṃ maraṇe sūtake caitadeva hi  || 2,23.31 ||
pakṣiṇī yonisambandhe bāndhaveṣu tathaiva ca  |
ekarātraṃ samuddiṣṭaṃ gurau sabrahmacāriṇi  || 2,23.32 ||
prete rājani sajyotiryasya syādviṣaye sthitiḥ  |
gṛhe mṛtāsu dattāsu kanyakāsu tryahaṃ pituḥ  || 2,23.33 ||
parapūrvāsu bhāryāsu putreṣu kṛtakeṣu ca  |
trirātraṃ syāttathācārye svabhāryāsvanyagāsu ca  || 2,23.34 ||
ācāryaputre patnyāṃ ca ahorātramudāhṛtam  |
ekāhaṃ syādupādhyāye svagrāme śrotriye'pi ca  || 2,23.35 ||
trirātramasapiṇḍeṣu svagṛhe saṃsthiteṣu ca  |
ekāhaṃ cāsvavarye syādekarātraṃ tadiṣyate  || 2,23.36 ||
trirātraṃ śvaśrūmaraṇe śvaśure vai tadeva hi  |
sadyaḥ śaucaṃ samuddiṣṭaṃ sagotre saṃsthite sati  || 2,23.37 ||
śuddhyedvipro daśāhena dvādaśāhena bhūmipaḥ  |
vaiśyaḥ pañcadaśāhena śūdro māsena śudyati  || 2,23.38 ||
kṣatraviṭśūdradāyādā ye syurviprasya bāndhavāḥ  |
teṣāmaśauce viprasya daśāhācchuddhiriṣyate  || 2,23.39 ||
rājanyavaiśyāvapyevaṃ hīnavarṇāsu yoniṣu  |
svameva śaucaṃ kuryātāṃ viśuddhyarthamasaṃśayam  || 2,23.40 ||
sarve tūttaravarṇānāmaśaucaṃ kuryurādṛtāḥ  |
tadvarṇavidhidṛṣṭena svaṃ tu śaucaṃ svayoniṣu  || 2,23.41 ||
ṣaḍrātraṃ vā trirātraṃ syādekarātraṃ krameṇa hi  |
vaiśyakṣatriyaviprāṇāṃ śūdreṣvāśaucameva tu  || 2,23.42 ||
ardhamāso'tha ṣaḍrātraṃ trirātraṃ dvijapuṅgavāḥ  |
śūdrakṣatriyaviprāṇāṃ vaiśyeṣvāśaucamiṣyate  || 2,23.43 ||
ṣaḍrātraṃ vai daśāhaṃ ca viprāṇāṃ vaiśyaśūdrayoḥ  |
aśaucaṃ kṣatriye proktaṃ krameṇa dvijapuṅgavāḥ  || 2,23.44 ||
śūdraviṭkṣatriyāṇāṃ tu brāhmaṇe saṃsthite sati  |
daśarātreṇa śuddhiḥ syādityāha kamalodbhavaḥ  || 2,23.45 ||
asapiṇḍaṃ dvijaṃ pretaṃ vipro nirhṛtya bandhuvat |
aśitvā ca sahoṣitvā daśarātreṇa śudhyati  || 2,23.46 ||
yadyannamatti teṣāṃ tu trirātreṇa tataḥ śuciḥ  |
anadannannamahnaiva na ca tasmin gṛhe vaset || 2,23.47 ||
sodakeṣvetadeva syānmāturāpteṣu bandhuṣu  |
daśāhena śavasparśe sapiṇḍaścaiva śudhyati  || 2,23.48 ||
yadi nirharati pretaṃ prolabhākrāntamānasaḥ  |
daśāhena dvijaḥ śudhyeddvādaśāhena bhūmipaḥ  || 2,23.49 ||
ardhamāsena vaiśyastu śūdro māsena śudhyati  |
ṣaḍrātreṇāthavā sarve trirātreṇāthavā punaḥ  || 2,23.50 ||
anāthaṃ caiva nirhṛtya brāhmaṇaṃ dhanavarjitam  |
snātvā saṃprāśya tu ghṛtaṃ śudhyanti brāhmaṇādayaḥ  || 2,23.51 ||
avaraścedvaraṃ varṇamavaraṃ vā varo yadi  |
aśauce saṃspṛśetsnehāttadāśaucena śudhyati  || 2,23.52 ||
pretībhūtaṃ dvijaṃ vipro yo'nugacchata kāmataḥ  |
snātvā sacailaṃ spṛṣṭvāgniṃ ghṛtaṃ prāśya viśudhyati  || 2,23.53 ||
ekāhātkṣatriye śuddhirvaiśye syācca dvyahena tu  |
śūdre dinatrayaṃ proktaṃ prāṇāyāmaśataṃ punaḥ  || 2,23.54 ||
anasthisaṃcite śūdre rauti cedbrāhmaṇaḥ svakaiḥ  |
trirātraṃ syāttathāśaucamekāhaṃ tvanyathā smṛtam  || 2,23.55 ||
asthisaṃcayanādarvāgekāhaṃ kṣatravaiśyayoḥ  |
anyathā caiva sajyotirbrāhmaṇe snānameva tu  || 2,23.56 ||
anasthisaṃcitvipre brāhmaṇo rauti cettadā  |
snānenaiva bhavecchuddhiḥ sacailena na saṃśayaḥ  || 2,23.57 ||
yastaiḥ sahāśanaṃ kuryācchayanādīni caiva hi  |
bāndhavo vāparo vāpi sa daśāhena śudhyati  || 2,23.58 ||
yasteṣāmannamaśnāti sakṛdevāpi kāmataḥ  |
tadāśauce nivṛtte'sau snānaṃ kṛtvā viśudhyati  || 2,23.59 ||
yāvattadannamaśnāti durbhikṣopahato naraḥ  |
tāvantyahānyaśaucaṃ syātprāyaścittaṃ tataścaret || 2,23.60 ||
dāhādyaśaucaṃ kartavyaṃ dvijānāmagnihotriṇām  |
sapiṇḍānāṃ tu maraṇe maraṇāditareṣu ca  || 2,23.61 ||
sapiṇḍatā ca puruṣe saptame vinivartate  |
samānodakabhāvastu janmanāmnoravedane  || 2,23.62 ||
pitā pitāmahaścaiva tathaiva prapitāmahaḥ  |
lepabhājastrayaścātmā sāpiṇḍyaṃ sāptapauruṣaṇ || 2,23.63 ||
aprattānāṃ tathā strīṇāṃ sāpiṇḍyaṃ sāptapauruṣam  |
ūḍhānāṃ bhartusāpiṇḍyaṃ prāha devaḥ pitāmahaḥ  || 2,23.64 ||
ye caikajātā bahavo bhinnayonaya eva ca  |
bhinnavarṇāstu sāpiṇḍyaṃ bhavetteṣāṃ tripūruṣam  || 2,23.65 ||
kāravaḥ śilpino vaidyā dāsīdāsāstathaiva ca  |
dātāro niyamī caiva brahmavidbrahmacāriṇau  || 2,23.66 ||
satriṇo vratinastāvatsadyaḥ śaucā udāhṛtāḥ  |
rājā caivābhiṣiktaśca prāṇasatriṇa eva ca  || 2,23.67 ||
yajñe vivāhakāle ca devayāge tathaiva ca  |
sadyaḥ śaucaṃ samākhyātaṃ durbhikṣe cāpyupadrave  || 2,23.68 ||
ḍimbāhavahatānāṃ ca vidyutā pārthivairdvijaiḥ  |
sadyaḥ śaucaṃ samākhyātaṃ sarpādimaraṇe tathā  || 2,23.69 ||
agnau maruprapatane vīrādhvanyapyanāśake  |
brāhmaṇārthe ca saṃnyaste sadyaḥ śaucaṃ vidhīyate  || 2,23.70 ||
naiṣṭhikānāṃ vanasthānāṃ yatīnāṃ brahmacāriṇām  |
nāśaucaṃ kīrtyate sadbhiḥ patite ca tathā mṛte  || 2,23.71 ||
patitānāṃ na dāhaḥ syānnāntyeṣṭirnāsthisaṃcayaḥ  |
na cāśrupātapiṇḍau vā kāryaṃ śrāddhādi kaṅkvacit || 2,23.72 ||
vyāpādayettathātmānaṃ svayaṃ yo'gniviṣādibhiḥ  |
vihitaṃ tasya nāśaucaṃ nāgnirnāpyudakādikam  || 2,23.73 ||
atha kaścitpramādena mriyate'gniviṣādibhiḥ  |
tasyāśaucaṃ vidhātavyaṃ kāryaṃ caivodakādikam  || 2,23.74 ||
jāte kumāre tadahaḥ kāmaṃ kuryātpratigraham  |
hiraṇyadhānyagovāsastilānnaguḍasarpiṣām  || 2,23.75 ||
phalāni puṣpaṃ śākaṃ ca lavaṇaṃ kāṣṭhameva ca  |
toyaṃ dadhi ghṛtaṃ tailamauṣadhaṃ kṣīrameva ca  |
āśaucināṃ gṛhādgrāhyaṃ śuṣkānnaṃ caiva nityaśaḥ  || 2,23.76 ||
āhitāgniryathānyāyaṃ dagdhavyastribhiragnibhiḥ  |
anāhitāgnirgṛhyeṇa laukikenetaro janaḥ  || 2,23.77 ||
dehābhāvātpalāśaistu kṛtvā pratikṛtiṃ punaḥ  |
dāhaḥ kāryo yathānyāyaṃ sapiṇḍaiḥ śraddhayānvitaiḥ  || 2,23.78 ||
sakṛtprasiñcantyudakaṃ nāmagotreṇa vāgyatāḥ  |
daśāhaṃ bāndhavaiḥ sārdhaṃ sarve caivārdravāsasaḥ  || 2,23.79 ||
piṇḍaṃ pratidinaṃ dadyuḥ sāyaṃ prātaryathāvidhi  |
pretāya ca gṛhadvāri caturthe bhojayeddvijān  || 2,23.80 ||
dvitīye'hani kartavyaṃ kṣurakarma sabāndhavaiḥ  |
caturthe bāndhavaiḥ sarvairasthanāṃ saṃcayanaṃ bhavet |
pūrvaṃ tu bhojayedviprānayugmān śraddhayā śucīn  || 2,23.81 ||
pañcame navame caiva tathaivaikādaśe'hani  |
ayugmān bhojayedviprānnavaśrāddhaṃ tu tadviduḥ  || 2,23.82 ||
ekādaśe'hni kurvota pretamuddiśya bhāvataḥ  |
dvādaśe vātha kartavyamanindye tvathavāhani  |
ekaṃ pavitramekor'ghaḥ piṇḍapātraṃ tathaiva ca  || 2,23.83 ||
evaṃ mṛtāhni kartavyaṃ pratimāsaṃ tu vatsaram  |
sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ proktaṃ pūrṇe saṃvatsare punaḥ  || 2,23.84 ||
kuryāccatvāri pātrāṇi pretādīnāṃ dvijottamāḥ  |
pretārthaṃ pitṛpātreṣu pātramāsecayettataḥ  || 2,23.85 ||
ye samānā iti dvābhyāṃ piṇḍānapyevameva hi  |
sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ śrāddhaṃ devapūrvaṃ vidhīyate  || 2,23.86 ||
pitṝnāvāhayettatra punaḥ pretaṃ ca nirdiśet |
ye sapiṇḍīkṛtāḥ pretāna teṣāṃ syātpṛthakkriyāḥ  |
yastu kuryātpṛthakpiṇḍaṃ pitṛhā so'bhijāyate  || 2,23.87 ||
mṛte pitari vai putraḥ piṇḍamabdaṃ samācaret |
dadyāccānnaṃ sodakumbhaṃ pratyahaṃ pretadharmataḥ  || 2,23.88 ||
pārvaṇena vidhānena saṃvatsarikamiṣyate  |
pratisaṃvatsaraṃ kāryaṃ vidhireṣa sanātanaḥ  || 2,23.89 ||
mātāpitroḥ sutaiḥ kāryaṃ piṇḍadānādikaṃ ca yat |
patnī kuryātsutābhāve patnya bhāve sahodahaḥ  || 2,23.90 ||
anenaiva vidhāne jīvan vā śrāddhamācaret |
kṛtvā dānādikaṃ sarvaṃ śraddhāyuktaḥ samāhitaḥ  || 2,23.91 ||
eṣa vaḥ kathitaḥ samyaggṛhasthānāṃ kriyāvidhiḥ  |
strīṇāṃ tu bhartṛśuśrūṣā dharmo nānya iheṣyate  || 2,23.92 ||
svadharmaparamo nityamīśvirārpitamānasaḥ  |
prāpnoti tatparaṃ sthānaṃ yaduktaṃ vedavādibhiḥ  || 2,23.93 ||

iti śrīkūrmapurāṇe ṣaṭsāhastryāṃ saṃhitāyāmuparivibhāge trayoviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ

_____________________________________________________________


vyāsa uvāca
agnihotraṃ tu juhuyādādyante'harniśoḥ sadā  |
darśena caiva pakṣānte paurṇamāsena caiva hi  || 2,24.1 ||
śasyānte navaśasyeṣṭyā tathartvante dvijo'dhvaraiḥ  |
paśunā tvayanasyānte samānte saumikairmakhaiḥ  || 2,24.2 ||
nāniṣṭvā navaśasyeṣṭyā paśunā vāgnimān dvijaḥ  |
navānnamadyānmāṃsaṃ vā dīrghamāyurjijīviṣuḥ  || 2,24.3 ||
navenānnena cāniṣṭvā paśuhavyena cāganyaḥ  |
prāṇānevāttumicchanti navānnāmiṣagṛddhinaḥ  || 2,24.4 ||
sāvitrān śāntihomāṃśca kuryātparvasu nityaśaḥ  |
pitṝṃścaivāṣṭakāsvarcannityamanvaṣṭakāsu ca  || 2,24.5 ||
eṣa dharmaḥ paro nityamapadharmo'nya ucyate  |
trayāṇāmiha varṇānāṃ gṛhasthāśramavāsinām  || 2,24.6 ||
nāstikyādathavālasyādyo'gnīnnādhātumicchati  |
yajeta vā na yajñena sa yāti narakān bahūn  || 2,24.7 ||
tāmistramandhatāmistraṃ mahārauravarauravau  |
kumbhīpākaṃ vaitaraṇīmasipatravanaṃ tathā  || 2,24.8 ||
anyāṃśca narakān ghorān saṃprāpyānte sudurmatiḥ  |
antyajānāṃ kule viprāḥ śūdrayonau ca jāyate  || 2,24.9 ||
tasmātsarvaprayatnena brāhmaṇo hi viśeṣataḥ  |
ādhāyāgniṃ viśuddhātmā yajeta parameśvaram  || 2,24.10 ||
agnihotrātparo dharmo dvijānāṃ neha vidyate  |
tasmādārādhayennityamagnihotreṇa śāśvatam  || 2,24.11 ||
yaścādhāyāgnimālasyānna yaṣṭuṃ devamicchati  |
so'sau mūḍho na saṃbhāṣyaḥ kiṃ punarnāstiko janaḥ  || 2,24.12 ||
yasya traivārṣikaṃ bhaktaṃ paryāptaṃ bhṛtyavṛttaye  |
adhikaṃ cāpi vidyeta sa somaṃ pātumarhati  || 2,24.13 ||
eṣa vai sarvayajñānāṃ somaḥ prathama iṣyate  |
somenārādhayeddevaṃ somalokamaheśvaram  || 2,24.14 ||
na somayāgādadhiko maheśārādhane kratuḥ  |
samo vā vidyate tasmātsomenābhyarcayetparam  || 2,24.15 ||
pitāmahena viprāṇāmādāvabhihitaḥ śubhaḥ  |
dharmo vimuktaye sākṣācchrautaḥ smārto dvidhā punaḥ  || 2,24.16 ||
śrautastretāgnisaṃbandhātsmārtaḥ pūrvaṃ mayoditaḥ  |
śreyaskaratamaḥ śrautastasmācchrautaṃ samācaret || 2,24.17 ||
ubhāvabhihitau dharmau vedādeva viniḥ sṛtau  |
śiṣṭācārastṛtīyaḥ syācchratismṛtyoralābhataḥ  || 2,24.18 ||
dharmeṇābhigato yaistu vedaḥ saparibṛṃhaṇaḥ  |
te śiṣṭā brāhmaṇāḥ proktā nityamātmaguṇānvitāḥ  || 2,24.19 ||
teṣāmabhimato yaḥ syāccetasā nityameva hi  |
sa dharmaḥ kathitaḥ sadbhirnānyeṣāmiti dhāraṇā  || 2,24.20 ||
purāṇaṃ dharmaśāstraṃ ca vedānāmupabṛṃhaṇam  |
ekasmādbrahmavijñānaṃ dharmajñānaṃ tathaikataḥ  || 2,24.21 ||
dharmaṃ jijñāsamānānāṃ tatpramāṇataraṃ smṛtam  |
dharmaśāstraṃ purāṇaṃ tadbrahmajñāne parā pramā  || 2,24.22 ||
nānyato jāyate dharmo brahmavidyā ca vaidikī  |
tasmāddharmaṃ purāṇaṃ ca śraddhātavyaṃ dvijātibhiḥ  || 2,24.23 ||

iti śrīkūrmapurāṇe ṣaṭsāhastryāṃ saṃhitāyāmuparivibhāge caturviśo'dhyāyaḥ

īṇṛEE ṇīCḥṭZūḻṣṣīṅE ZEīCḥEṇ̱

vyāsa uvāca
eṣa vo'bhihitaḥ kṛtsno gṛhasthāśramavāsinaḥ  |
dvijāteḥ paramo dharmo vartanāni nibodhata  || 2,25.1 ||
dvividhastu gṛhī jñeyaḥ sādhakaścāpyasādhakaḥ  |
adhyāpanaṃ yājanaṃ ca pūrvasyāhuḥ pratigraham  |
kusīdakṛṣivāṇijyaṃ prakurvotāsvayaṅkṛtam  || 2,25.2 ||
kṛṣerabhāvādvāṇijyaṃ tadabhāvātkusīdakam  |
āpatkalpo hyaṃ jñeyaḥ pūrvokto mukhya iṣyate  || 2,25.3 ||
svayaṃ vā karṣaṇaṃ kuryādvāṇijyaṃ vā kusīdakam  |
kaṣṭā pāpīyasī vṛttiḥ kusīdaṃ tadvivarjayet || 2,25.4 ||
kṣātravṛttiṃ parāṃ prahurna svayaṃ karṣaṇaṃ dvijaiḥ  |
tasmātkṣātreṇa varteta vartanenāpadi dvijaḥ  || 2,25.5 ||
tena cāvāpyajīvaṃstu vaiśyavṛttiṃ kṛṣiṃ vrajet |
na kathañcana kurvota brāhmaṇaḥ karma karṣaṇam  || 2,25.6 ||
labdhalābhaḥ pitṝn devān brāhmaṇāṃścāpi pūjayet |
te tṛptāstasya taṃ doṣaṃ śamayanti na saṃśayaḥ  || 2,25.7 ||
devebhyaśca pitṛbhyaśca dadyādbhāgaṃ tu viṃśakam  |
triṃśadbhāgaṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ kṛṣiṃ kurvanna duṣyati  || 2,25.8 ||
vaṇikpradadyāddviguṇaṃ kusīdī triguṇaṃ punaḥ  |
kṛṣīvalo na doṣeṇa yujyate nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 2,25.9 ||
śiloñchaṃ vāpyādadīta gṛhasthaḥ sādhakaḥ punaḥ  |
vidyāśilpādayastvanye bahavo vṛttihetavaḥ  || 2,25.10 ||
asādhakastu yaḥ prokto gṛhasthāśramasaṃsthitaḥ  |
śiloñche tasya kathite dve vṛttī paramarṣibhiḥ  || 2,25.11 ||
amṛtenāthavā jīvenmṛtenāpyathavā yadi  |
ayācitaṃ syādamṛtaṃ mṛtaṃ bhekṣaṃ tu yācitam  || 2,25.12 ||
kuśūladhānyako vā syātkumbhīdhānyaka eva vā  |
tryahaihiko vāpi bhavedaśvastanika eva ca  || 2,25.13 ||
caturṇāmapi caiteṣāṃ dvijānāṃ gṛhamedhinām  |
śreyān paraḥ paro jñeyo dharmato lokajittamaḥ  || 2,25.14 ||
ṣaṭkarmaiko bhavatyeṣāṃ tribhiranyaḥ pravartate  |
dvābhyāmekaścaturthastu brahmasatreṇa jīvati  || 2,25.15 ||
vartayaṃstu śiloñchābhyāmagnihotraparāyaṇaḥ  |
iṣṭīḥ pārvāyaṇāntīyāḥ kevalā nirvapetsadā  || 2,25.16 ||
na lokavṛtiṃ varteta vṛttihetoḥ kathañcana  |
ajihmāmaśaṭhāṃ śuddhāṃ jīvedbrāhmaṇajīvikām  || 2,25.17 ||
yācitvā vāpi sadbhyo'nnaṃ pitṝndevāṃstu toṣayet |
yācayedvā śuciṃ dāntaṃ na tṛpyeta svayaṃ tataḥ  || 2,25.18 ||
yastu dravyārjanaṃ kṛtvā gṛhasthastoṣayenna tu  |
devān pitṛṃśca vidhinā śunāṃ yoniṃ vrajatyasau  || 2,25.19 ||
dharmaścārthaśca kāmaśca śreyo mokṣaścatuṣṭayam  |
dharmāviruddhaḥ kāmaḥ syādbrāhmaṇānāṃ tu netaraḥ  || 2,25.20 ||
yor'tho dharmāya nātmārthaḥ sor'tho'narthastathetaraḥ  |
tasmādarthaṃ samāsādya dadyādvai juhuyādyajet || 2,25.21 ||

iti śrīkūrmapurāṇe ṣaṭsāhastryāṃ saṃhitāyāmuparivibhāge pañcaviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ


_____________________________________________________________

īṇṛEE ṇīCḥṭZūḻṣṣīṅE ZEīCḥEṇ̱

vyāsa uvāca
athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi dānadharmamanuttamam  |
brahmaṇābhihitaṃ pūrvamṛṣīṇāṃ brahmavādinām  || 2,26.1 ||
arthānāmudite pātre śraddhayā pratipādanam  |
dānamityabhinirdiṣṭaṃ bhuktimuktiphalapradam  || 2,26.2 ||
yaddadāti viśiṣṭebhyaḥ śraddhayā parayā yutaḥ  |
tadvai vittamahaṃ manye śeṣaṃ kasyāpi rakṣati  || 2,26.3 ||
nityaṃ naimittikaṃ kāmyaṃ trividhaṃ dānamucyate  |
caturthaṃ vimalaṃ proktaṃ sarvadānottamottamam  || 2,26.4 ||
ahanyahani yatkiñciddīyate'nupakāriṇe  |
anuddiśya phalaṃ tasmādbrāhmaṇāya tu nityakam  || 2,26.5 ||
yattu pāpopaśāntyarthaṃ dīyate viduṣāṃ kare  |
naimittikaṃ taduddiṣṭaṃ dānaṃ sadbhiranuṣṭhitam  || 2,26.6 ||
apatyavijayaiśvaryasvargārthaṃ yatpradīyate  |
dānaṃ tatkāmyamākhyātamṛṣibhirdharmacintakaiḥ  || 2,26.7 ||
yadīśvaraprīṇanārthaṃ brahmavitsu pradīyate  |
cetasā dharmayuktena dānaṃ tadvimalaṃ śivam  || 2,26.8 ||
dānadharmaṃ niṣeveta pātramāsādya śaktitaḥ  |
utpatsyate hi tatpātraṃ yattārayati sarvataḥ  || 2,26.9 ||
kuṭumbabhaktavasanāddeyaṃ yadatiricyate  |
anyathā dīyate yaddhi na taddānaṃ phalapradam  || 2,26.10 ||
śrotriyāya kulīnāya vinītāya tapasvine  |
vṛttasthāya daridrāya pradeyaṃ bhaktipūrvakam  || 2,26.11 ||
yastu dadyānmahīṃ bhaktyā brāhmaṇāyāhitāgnaye  |
sa yāti paramaṃ sthānaṃ yatra gatvā na śocati  || 2,26.12 ||
ikṣubhiḥ saṃtatāṃ bhumiṃ yavagodhūmaśalinīm  |
dadāti vedaviduṣe yaḥ sa bhūyo na jāyate  || 2,26.13 ||
gocarmamātrāmapi vā yo bhūmiṃ saṃprayacchati  |
brāhmaṇāya daridrāya sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate  || 2,26.14 ||
bhūmidānātparaṃ dānaṃ vidyate neha kiñcana  |
annadānaṃ tena tulyaṃ vidyādānaṃ tato'dhikam  || 2,26.15 ||
yo brāhmaṇāya śāntāya śucaye dharmaśāline  |
dadāti vidyāṃ vidhinā brahmaloke mahīyate  || 2,26.16 ||
dadyādaharahastvannaṃ śraddhayā brahmacāriṇe  |
sarvapāpavinirmukto brahmaṇaḥ sthānamāpnuyāt || 2,26.17 ||
gṛhasthāyānnadānena phalaṃ prāpnoti mānavaḥ  |
āmamevāsya dātavyaṃ dattvāpnoti parāṃ gatim  || 2,26.18 ||
vaiśākhyāṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ tu brāhmaṇān sapta pañca vā  |
upoṣya vidhinā śāntaḥ śuciḥ prayatamānasaḥ  || 2,26.19 ||
pūjayitvā tilaiḥ kṛṣṇairmadhunā na viśeṣataḥ  |
gandhādibhiḥ samabhyarcya vācayedvā svyaṃ vadet || 2,26.20 ||
prīyatāṃ dharmarājeti yadvā manasi vartate  |
yāvajjīvakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ tatkṣaṇādeva naśyati  || 2,26.21 ||
kṛṣṇājine tilān kṛttvā hiraṇyaṃ madhusarpiṣī  |
dadāti yastu viprāya sarvaṃ tarati duṣkṛtam  || 2,26.22 ||
kṛtānnamudakumbhaṃ ca vaiśākhyāṃ ca viśeṣataḥ  |
nirdiśya dharmarājāya viprebhyo mucyate bhayāt || 2,26.23 ||
suvarṇatilayuktaistu brāhmaṇān sapta pañca vā  |
tarpayedudapātraistu brahmahatyāṃ vyapohati  || 2,26.24 ||
māghamāse tu viprastu dvādaśyāṃ samupoṣitaḥ  |
śuklāmvaradharaḥ kṛṣṇaistilairhutvā hutāśanam  || 2,26.25 ||
pradadyādbrāhmaṇebhyastu tilāneva samāhitaḥ  |
janmaprabhṛti yatpāpaṃ sarvaṃ tarati vai dvijaḥ  || 2,26.26 ||
amāvasyāmanuprāpya brāhmaṇāya tapasvine  |
yatkiciddevadeveśaṃ dadyāccoddiśya śaṅkaram  || 2,26.27 ||
prīyatāmīśvaraḥ somo mahādevaḥ sanātanaḥ  |
saptajanmakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ tatkṣaṇādeva naśyati  || 2,26.28 ||
yastu kṛṣṇacaturdaśyāṃ snātvā devaṃ pinākinam  |
ārādhayeddvijamukhe na tasyāsti punarbhavaḥ  || 2,26.29 ||
kṛṣṇāṣṭamyāṃ viśeṣeṇa dhārmikāya dvijātaye  |
snātvābhyarcya yathānyāyaṃ pādaprakṣālanādibhiḥ  || 2,26.30 ||
prīyatāṃ me mahādevo dadyāddravyaṃ svakīyakam  |
sarvapāpavinirmuktaḥ prāpnoti paramāṃ gatim  || 2,26.31 ||
dvijaiḥ kṛṣṇacaturdaśyāṃ kṛṣṇāṣṭamyāṃ viśeṣataḥ  |
amāvāsyāyāṃ bhaktaistu pūjanīyastrilocanaḥ  || 2,26.32 ||
ekādaśyāṃ nirāhāro dvādaśyāṃ puruṣottamam  |
arcayedbāhmaṇamukhe sa gacchetparamaṃ padam  || 2,26.33 ||
eṣā tithirvaiṣṇavīṃ syāddvādaśī śuklapakṣake  |
tasyāmārādhayeddevaṃ prayatnena janārdanam  || 2,26.34 ||
yatkiñciddevamīśānamuddiśya brāhmaṇe śucau  |
dīyate viṣṇave vāpi tadanantaphalapradam  || 2,26.35 ||
yo hi yāṃ devatāmicchetsamārādhayituṃ naraḥ  |
brāhmaṇān pūjayedyatnātsatasyāṃ toṣayettataḥ  || 2,26.36 ||
dvijānāṃ vapurāsthāya nityaṃ tiṣṭhanti devatāḥ  |
pūjyante brāhmaṇālābhe pratimādiṣvapi kvacit || 2,26.37 ||
tasmātsarvaprayatnena tattatphalamabhīpsatā  |
dvijeṣu devatā nityaṃ pūjanīyā viśeṣataḥ  || 2,26.38 ||
vibhūtikāmaḥ satataṃ pūjayedvai purandaram  |
brahmavarcasakāmastu brahmāṇaṃ brahmakāmukaḥ  || 2,26.39 ||
ārogyakāmo'tha raviṃ dhanakāmo hutāśanam  |
karmaṇāṃ siddhikāmastu pūjayedvai vināyakam  || 2,26.40 ||
bhogakāmastu śaśinaṃ balakāmaḥ samīraṇam  |
mumukṣuḥ sarvasaṃsārātprayatnenārcayeddharim  || 2,26.41 ||
yastu yogaṃ tathā mokṣamanvicchejjñānamaiśvaram  |
sor'cayedvai virūpākṣaṃ prayatneneśvareśvaram  || 2,26.42 ||
ye vāñchanti mahāyogān jñānāni ca maheśvaram  |
te pūjayanti bhūteśaṃ keśavaṃ cāpi bhoginaḥ  || 2,26.43 ||
vāridastṛptimāpnoti sukhamakṣayyamannadaḥ  |
tilapradaḥ prajāmiṣṭāṃ dīpadaścakṣuruttamam  || 2,26.44 ||
bhūmidaḥ sarvamāpnoti dīrghamāyurhiraṇyadaḥ  |
gṛhado'gryāṇi veśmāni rūpyado rūpamuttamam  || 2,26.45 ||
vāsodaścandrasālokyamaśvisālokyamaśvadaḥ  |
anaḍudaḥ śriyaṃ puṣṭāṃ godo vradhnasya viṣṭapam  || 2,26.46 ||
yānaśayyāprado bhāryāmaiśvaryamabhayapradaḥ  |
dhānyadaḥ śāśvataṃ saukhyaṃ brahmado brahmasātmyatām  || 2,26.47 ||
dhānyānyapi yathāśakti vipreṣu pratipādayet |
vedavitsu viśiṣṭeṣu pretya svargaṃ samaśnute  || 2,26.48 ||
gavāṃ ghāsapradānena sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate  |
indhanānāṃ pradānena dīptāgnirjāyate naraḥ  || 2,26.49 ||
phalamūlāni śākāni bhojyāni vividhāni ca  |
pradadyādbrāhmaṇebhyastu mudā yuktaḥ sadā bhavet || 2,26.50 ||
auṣadhaṃ snehamāhāraṃ rogiṇe rogaśāntaye  |
dadāno rogarahitaḥ sukhī dīrghāyureva ca  || 2,26.51 ||
asipatravanaṃ mārgaṃ kṣuradhārāsamanvitam  |
tīvritāpaṃ ca tarati chatropānatprado naraḥ  || 2,26.52 ||
yadyadiṣṭatamaṃ loke yaccāpi dayitaṃ gṛhe  |
tattadguṇavate deyaṃ tadevākṣyamicchatā  || 2,26.53 ||
apane viṣuve caiva grahaṇe candrasūryayoḥ  |
saṃkrāntyādiṣu kāleṣu dattaṃ bhavati cākṣayam  || 2,26.54 ||
prayāgādiṣu tīrtheṣu puṇyeṣvāyataneṣu ca  |
dattvā cākṣayamāpnoti nadīṣu ca vaneṣu ca  || 2,26.55 ||
dānadharmātparo dharmo bhūtānāṃ neha vidyate  |
tasmādviprāya dātavyaṃ śrotriyāya dvijātibhiḥ  || 2,26.56 ||
svagāyurbhūtikāmena tathā pāpopaśāntaye  |
mumukṣuṇā ca dātavyaṃ brāhmaṇebhyastathānvaham  || 2,26.57 ||
dīyamānaṃ tu yo mohādgoviprāgnisureṣu ca  |
nivārayati pāpātmā tiryagyoniṃ vrajettu saḥ  || 2,26.58 ||
yastu dravyārjanaṃ kṛtvā nārcayedbrāhmaṇān surān  |
sarvasvamapahṛtyainaṃ rājā rāṣṭrātpravāsayet || 2,26.59 ||
yastu durbhikṣavelāyāmannādyaṃ na prayacchati  |
mriyamāṇeṣu vipreṣu brāhmaṇaḥ sa tu garhitaḥ  || 2,26.60 ||
na tasmātpratigṛhṇīyurna viśeyuśca tena hi  |
aṅkayitvā svakādrāṣṭrāttaṃ rājā vipravāsayet || 2,26.61 ||
yastvasadbhyo dadātīha svadravyaṃ dharmasādhanam  |
sa pūrvābhyadhikaḥ pāpī narake pacyate naraḥ  || 2,26.62 ||
svādhyāyavanto ye viprā vidyāvanto jitendriyāḥ  |
satyasaṃyamasaṃyuktāstebhyo dadyāddvijottamāḥ  || 2,26.63 ||
subhuktamapi vidvāṃsaṃ dhārmikaṃ bhojayeddvijam  |
na tu mūrkhamavṛttasthaṃ daśarātramupoṣitam  || 2,26.64 ||
sannikṛṣṭamatikramya śrotriyaṃ yaḥ prayacchati  |
sa tena karmaṇā pāpī dahatyāsaptamaṃ kulam  || 2,26.65 ||
yadisyādadhiko vipraḥ śīlavidyādibhiḥ svayam  |
tasmai yatnena dātavyaṃ atikramyāpi sannidhim  || 2,26.66 ||
yo'rcitaṃ pratigṛhṇīyāddadyādarcitameva ca  |
tāvubhau gacchataḥ svargaṃ narakaṃ tu viparyaye  || 2,26.67 ||
na vāryapi prayaccheta nāstike haituke'pi ca  |
pāṣaṇḍeṣu ca sarveṣu nāvedavidi dharmavit || 2,26.68 ||
apūpaṃ ca hiraṇyaṃ ca gāmaśvaṃ pṛthivīṃ tilān  |
avidvān pratigṛhṇāno bhasmī bhavati kāṣṭhavat || 2,26.69 ||
dvijātibhyo dhanaṃ lipsetpraśastebhyo dvijottamaḥ  |
api vā jātimātrebhyo na tu śūdrātkathañcana  || 2,26.70 ||
vṛttisaṅkocamanvicchenneheta dhanavistaram  |
dhanalobhe prasaktastu brāhmaṇyādeva hīyate  || 2,26.71 ||
vedānadhītya sakalān yajñāṃścāvāpya sarvaśaḥ  |
na tāṃ gatimavāpnoti saṅkocādyāmavāpnuyāt || 2,26.72 ||
pratigraharucirna syātyātrārthaṃ tu samāharet |
sthityarthādadhikaṃ gṛhṇan brāhmaṇo yātyadhogatim  || 2,26.73 ||
yastu yācanako nityaṃ na sa svargasya bhājanam  |
udvejayati bhūtāni yathā caurastathaiva saḥ  || 2,26.74 ||
gurūn bhṛtyāṃścojjihīrṣurarciṣyan devatātithīn  |
sarvataḥ pratigṛhṇīyānna tu tṛpyetsvayaṃ tataḥ  || 2,26.75 ||
evaṃ gṛhastho yuktātmā devatātithipūjakaḥ  |
vartamānaḥ saṃyātātmā yāti tatparamaṃ padam  || 2,26.76 ||
putre nidhāya vā sarvaṃ gatvāraṇyaṃ tu tattvavit |
ekākī vicarennityamudāsīnaḥ samāhitaḥ  || 2,26.77 ||
eṣa vaḥ kathito dharmo gṛhasthānāṃ dvijottamāḥ  |
jñātvānutiṣṭhenniyataṃ tathānuṣṭhāpayeddvijān  || 2,26.78 ||
iti devamanādimekamīśaṃ
gṛhadharmeṇa samarcayedajastram  |
samatītya sa sarvabhūtayoniṃ
prakṛtiṃ yāti paraṃ na yāti janma  || 2,26.79 ||

iti śrīkūrmapurāṇe ṣaṭsāhastryāṃ saṃhitāyāmuparivibhāge ṣaḍviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ


_____________________________________________________________


vyāsa uvāca
evaṃ gṛhāśrame sthitvā dvitīyaṃ bhāgamāyuṣaḥ  |
vānaprasthāśramaṃ gacchetsadāraḥ sāgnireva ca  || 2,27.2 ||
nikṣipya bhāryāṃ putreṣu gacchedvanamathāpi vā  |
dṛṣṭvāpatyasya cāpatyaṃ jarjarīkṛtavigrahaḥ  || 2,27.2 ||
śuklapakṣasya pūrvāhne praśaste cottarāyaṇe  |
gatvāraṇyaṃ niyamavāṃstapaḥ kuryātsamāhitaḥ  || 2,27.3 ||
phalamūlāni pūtāni nityamāhāramāharet |
yatāhāro bhavettena pūjayetpitṛdevatāḥ  || 2,27.4 ||
pūjayitvātithiṃ nityaṃ snātvā cābhyarcayetsurān  |
gṛhādāhṛtya cāśnīyādaṣṭau grāsān samāhitaḥ  || 2,27.5 ||
jaṭāśca bibhṛyānnityaṃ nakharomāṇi notsṛjet |
svādhyāyaṃ sarvadā kuryānniyacchedvācamanyataḥ  || 2,27.6 ||
agnihotraṃ ca juhuyātpañcayajñān samācaret |
munyannaiṃrvividhairmedhyaiḥ śākamūlaphalena vā  || 2,27.7 ||
cīravāsā bhavennityaṃ snāyāttriṣavaṇaṃ śuciḥ  |
sarvabhūtānukampī syātpratigrahavivarjitaḥ  || 2,27.8 ||
darśena paurṇamāsena yajetniyataṃ dvijaḥ  |
ṛkṣeṣvāgrayaṇe caiva cāturmāsyāni cāharet |
uttarāyaṇaṃ ca kramaśo dakṣasyāyanameva ca  || 2,27.9 ||
vāsantaiḥ śāradairmedhyairmunyannaiḥ svayamāhṛtaiḥ  |
puroḍāśāṃścarūṃścaiva vidhivannirvapetpṛthak || 2,27.10 ||
devatābhyaśca tadhutvā vanyaṃ medhyataraṃ haviḥ  |
śeṣaṃ samupabhuñjīta lavaṇaṃ ca svayaṃ kṛtam  || 2,27.11 ||
varjayenmadhumāṃsāni bhaumāni kavakāni ca  |
bhūstṛṇaṃ śigrukaṃ caiva śleṣmātakaphalāni ca  || 2,27.12 ||
na phālakṛṣṭamaśnīyādutsṛṣṭamapi kenacit |
na grāmajātānyārto'pi puṣpāṇi ca phalāni ca  || 2,27.13 ||
śrāvaṇenaiva vidhinā vahniṃ paricaretsadā  |
na druhyetsarvabhūtāni nirdvandvo nirbhayo bhavet || 2,27.14 ||
na naktaṃ kiñcidaśnīyādrātrau dhyānaparo bhavet |
jitendriyo jitakrodhastattvajñānavicintakaḥ  |
brahmacārī bhavennityaṃ na patnīmapi saṃśrayet || 2,27.15 ||
yastu patnyā vanaṃ gatvā maithunaṃ kāmataścaret |
tadvrataṃ tasya lupyeta prāyaścittīyate dvijaḥ  || 2,27.16 ||
tatra yo jāyate garbho na saṃspṛśyo dvijātibhiḥ  |
na hi vede'dhikāro'sya tadvaṃśepyevameva hi  || 2,27.17 ||
adhaḥ śayīta satataṃ sāvitrījāpyatatparaḥ  |
śaraṇyaḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ saṃvibhāgaparaḥ sadā  || 2,27.18 ||
parivādaṃ mṛṣāvādaṃ nidrālasyaṃ vivarjayet |
ekāgniraniketaḥ syātprokṣitāṃ bhūmimāśrayet || 2,27.19 ||
mṛgaiḥ saha caredvāsaṃ taiḥ sahaiva ca saṃvaset |
śilāyāṃ śarkarāyāṃ vā śayīta susamāhitaḥ  || 2,27.20 ||
sadyaḥ prakṣālako vā syānmāsasaṃcayiko'pi vā  |
ṣaṇmāsanicayo vā syātsamānicaya eva vā  || 2,27.21 ||
tyajedāśvayuje māsi saṃpannaṃ pūrvasaṃcitam  |
jīrṇāni caiva vāsāṃsi śākamūlaphalāni ca  || 2,27.22 ||
dantolūkhaliko vāsyātkāpotīṃ vṛttimāśrayet |
aśmakuṭṭo bhavedvāpi kālapakvabhugeva vā  || 2,27.23 ||
naktaṃ cānna samaśnīyāddivā cāhṛtya śaktitaḥ  |
caturthakāliko vā syātsyādvāpyaṣṭamakālikaḥ  || 2,27.24 ||
cāndrāyaṇavidhānairvā śukle kṛṣṇe ca vartayet |
pakṣe pakṣe samaśnīyādyavāgūṃ kvathitāṃ sakṛt || 2,27.25 ||
puṣpamūlaphalairvāpi kevalairvartayetsadā  |
svābhāvikaiḥ svayaṃ śīrṇairvaikhānasamate sthitaḥ  || 2,27.26 ||
bhūmau vā parivarteta tiṣṭhedvā prapadairdinam  |
sthānāsanābhyāṃ viharenna kvaciddhairyamutsṛjet || 2,27.27 ||
grīṣme pañcatapāśca syādvarṣāsvabhrāvakāśakaḥ  |
ārdravāsāstu hemante kramaśo vardhayaṃstapaḥ  || 2,27.28 ||
upaspṛśya triṣavaṇaṃ pitṛdevāṃśca tarpayet |
ekapādena tiṣṭheta marīcīn vā pibettadā  || 2,27.29 ||
pañcāgnirdhūmapo vā syāduṣmapaḥ somapo'pi vā  |
payaḥ pibecchuklapakṣe kṛṣṇāpakṣe tu gomayam  |
śīrṇaparṇāśano vā syātkṛcchrairvā vartayetsadā  || 2,27.30 ||
yogābhyāsarataśca syādrudrādhyāyī bhavetsadā  |
atharvaśiraso'dhyetā vedāntābhyāsatatparaḥ  || 2,27.31 ||
yamān seveta satataṃ niyamāṃścāpyatandritaḥ  |
kṛṣṇājinī sottarīyaḥ śuklayajñopavītavān  || 2,27.32 ||
atha cāgnīn samāropya svātmani dhyānatatparaḥ  |
anagniraniketaḥ syānmunirmokṣaparo bhavet || 2,27.33 ||
tāpaseṣveva vipreṣu yātrikaṃ bhaikṣamāharet |
gṛhamedhiṣu cānyeṣu dvijeṣu vanavāsiṣu  || 2,27.34 ||
grāmādāhṛtya vāśnīyādaṣṭau grāsān vane vasan  |
pratigṛhya puṭenaiva pāṇinā śakalena vā  || 2,27.35 ||
vividhāścopaniṣada ātmasaṃsiddhaye japet |
vidyāviśeṣān sāvitrīṃ rudrādhyāyaṃ tathaiva ca  || 2,27.36 ||
mahāprāsthānikaṃ cāsau kuryādanaśanaṃ tu vā  |
agnipraveśamanyadvā brarhmārpaṇavidhau sthitaḥ  || 2,27.37 ||
yastu samyagimamāśramaṃ śivaṃ
saṃśrayedaśivapuñjanāśanam  |
tāpasaḥ sa paramaiśvaraṃ padaṃ
yāti yatra jagato'sya saṃsthitiḥ  || 2,27.38 ||

iti śrīkūrmapurāṇe ṣaṭsāhastryāṃ saṃhitāyāmuparivibhāge saptaviśo'dhyāya


_____________________________________________________________


vyāsa uvāca
evaṃ vanāśrame sthitvā tṛtīyaṃ bhāgamāyuṣaḥ  |
caturthamāyuṣo bhāgaṃ saṃnyāsena nayetkramāt || 2,28.1 ||
agnīnātmanī saṃsthāpya dvijaḥ pravrajito bhavet |
yogābhyāsarataḥ śānto brahmavidyāparāyaṇaḥ  || 2,28.2 ||
yadā manasi saṃjātaṃ vaitṛṣṇyaṃ sarvavastuṣu  |
tadā saṃnyāsamicchecca patitaḥ syādviparyaye  || 2,28.3 ||
prājāpatyāṃ nirūpyeṣṭimāgneyīmathavā punaḥ  |
dāntaḥ pakvakaṣāyo'sau brahmāśramamupāśrayet || 2,28.4 ||
jñānasaṃnyāsinaḥ kecidvedasaṃnyāsinaḥ pare  |
karmasaṃnyāsinastvanye trividhāḥ parikīrtitāḥ  || 2,28.5 ||
yaḥ sarvasaṅganirmukto nirdvandvaścaiva nirbhayaḥ  |
procyate jñānasaṃnyāsī svātmanyeva vyavasthitaḥ  || 2,28.6 ||
vedamevābhyasennityaṃ nirāśī niṣparigrahaḥ  |
procyate vedasaṃnyāsī mumukṣurvijitendriyaḥ  || 2,28.7 ||
yastvagnīnātmasātkṛtvā brahmārpaṇaparo dvijaḥ  |
jñeyaḥ sa karmasaṃnyāsī mahāyajñaparāyaṇaḥ  || 2,28.8 ||
trayāṇāmapi caiteṣāṃ jñānī tvabhyadhiko mataḥ  |
na tasya vidyate kāryaṃ na liṅgaṃ vā vipaścitaḥ  || 2,28.9 ||
nirmamo nirbhayaḥ śānto nirdvandvaḥ parṇabhojanaḥ  |
jīrṇakaupīnavāsāḥ syānnagno vā dhyānatatparaḥ  || 2,28.10 ||
brahmacārī mitāhāro grāmādannaṃ samāharet |
adhyātmamatirāsīta nirapekṣo nirāmiṣaḥ  || 2,28.11 ||
ātmanaiva sahāyena sukhārthaṃ vicarediha  |
nābhinandeta maraṇaṃ nābhinandeta jīvitam  || 2,28.12 ||
kālameva pratīkṣeta nideśaṃ bhṛtako yathā  |
nādhyetavyaṃ na vaktavyaṃ śrotavyaṃ na kadācana  |
evaṃ jñātvā paro yogī brahmabhūyāya kalpate  || 2,28.13 ||
ekavāsāthavā vidvān kaupīnācchādanastathā  |
muṇḍī śikhī vātha bhavettridaṇḍī niṣparigrahaḥ  |
kāṣāyavāsāḥ satataṃ dhyānayogaparāyaṇaḥ  || 2,28.14 ||
grāmānte vṛkṣamūle vā vaseddevālaye'pi vā  |
samaḥ śatrau ca mitre ca tathā mānāpamānayoḥ  |
bhaikṣyeṇa vartayennityaṃ naikānnādī bhavetkvacit || 2,28.15 ||
yastu mohena vālasyādekānnādī bhavedyatiḥ  |
na tasya niṣkṛtiḥ kāciddharmaśāstreṣu kathyate  || 2,28.16 ||
rāgadveṣavimuktātmā samaloṣṭāśmakāñcanaḥ  |
prāṇihaṃsānivṛttaśca maunī syātsarvanispṛhaḥ  || 2,28.17 ||
dṛṣṭipūtaṃ nyasetpādaṃ vastrapūtaṃ jalaṃ pibet |
satyapūtāṃ vadedvāṇīṃ manaḥ pūtaṃ samācaret || 2,28.18 ||
naikatra nivaseddeśe varṣābhyo'nyatra bhikṣukaḥ  |
snānaśaucarato nityaṃ kamaṇḍalukaraḥ śuciḥ  || 2,28.19 ||
brahmacaryarato nityaṃ vanavāsarato bhavet |
mokṣaśāstreṣu nirato brahmasūtrī jitendriyaḥ  || 2,28.20 ||
dambhāhaṅkāranirmukto nindāpaiśunyavarjitaḥ  |
ātmajñānaguṇopeto yatirmokṣamavāpnuyāt || 2,28.21 ||
abhyasetsatataṃ vedaṃ praṇavākhyaṃ sanātanam  |
snātvācamya vidhānena śucirdevālayādiṣu  || 2,28.22 ||
yajñopavītī śāntātmā kuśapāṇiḥ samāhitaḥ  |
dhautakāṣāyavasano bhasmacchannatanūrahaḥ  || 2,28.23 ||
adhiyajñaṃ brahma japedādhidaivikameva ca  |
ādhyātmikaṃ ca satataṃ vedāntābhihitaṃ ca yat || 2,28.24 ||
putreṣu vātha nivasan brahmacārī yatirmuniḥ  |
vedamevābhyasennityaṃ sa yāti paramāṃ gatim  || 2,28.25 ||
ahiṃsā satyamasteyaṃ brahmacaryaṃ tapaḥ param  |
kṣamā dayā ca satoṣo vratānyasya viśeṣataḥ  || 2,28.26 ||
vedāntajñānaniṣṭho vā pañca yajñān samāhitaḥ  |
kuryādaharahaḥ snātvā bhikṣānnenaiva tena hi  || 2,28.27 ||
homamantrāñjapennityaṃ kāle kāle samāhitaḥ  |
svādhyāyaṃ cānvahaṃ kuryātsāvitrīṃ saṃdhyayorjapet || 2,28.28 ||
dhyāyīta satataṃ devamekānte parameśvaram  |
ekānnaṃ varjayennityaṃ kāmaṃ krodhaṃ parigraham  || 2,28.29 ||
ekavāsā dvivāsā vā śikhī yajñopavītavān  |
kamaṇḍalukaro vidvān tridaṇḍī yāti tatparam  || 2,28.30 ||

iti śrīkūrmapurāṇe ṣaṭsāhastryāṃ saṃhitāyāmuparivibhāge'ṣṭāviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ


_____________________________________________________________


vyāsa uvāca
evaṃ svāśramaniṣṭhānāṃ yatīnāṃ niyatātmanām  |
bhaikṣeṇa vartanaṃ proktaṃ phalamūlairathāpi vā  || 2,29.1 ||
ekakālaṃ caredbhaikṣaṃ na prasajyeta vistare  |
bhaikṣe prasakto hi yatirviṣayeṣvapi sajjati  || 2,29.2 ||
saptāgāraṃ caredbhaikṣamalābhāttu punaścaret |
prakṣālya pātre bhuñjīyādadbhiḥ prakṣālayettu tat || 2,29.3 ||
athavānyadupādāya pātre bhuñjīta nityaśaḥ  |
bhuktvā tatsaṃtyajetpātraṃ yātrāmātramalolupaḥ  || 2,29.4 ||
vidhūme sannamusale vyaṅgāre bhuktavajjane  |
vṛtte śarāvasaṃpāte bhikṣāṃ nityaṃ yatiścaret || 2,29.5 ||
godohamātraṃ tiṣṭheta kālaṃ bhikṣuradhomukhaḥ  |
bhikṣetyuktvā sakṛttūṣṇīmaśnīyādvāgyataḥ śuciḥ  || 2,29.6 ||
prakṣālya pāṇipādau ca samācamya yathāvidhi  |
āditye darśayitvānnaṃ bhuñjīta prāṅmukhottaraḥ  || 2,29.7 ||
hutvā prāṇāhutīḥ pañca grāsānaṣṭau samāhitaḥ  |
ācamya devaṃ brahmāṇaṃ dhyāyīta parameśvaram  || 2,29.8 ||
alābuṃ dārupātraṃ ca mṛṇmayaṃ vaiṇavaṃ tataḥ  |
catvāri yatipātrāṇi manurāha prajāpatiḥ  || 2,29.9 ||
prāgrātre pararātre ca madhyarātre tathaiva ca  |
saṃdhyāsvahni viśeṣeṇa cintayennityamīśvaram  || 2,29.10 ||
kṛtvā hṛtpadmanilaye viśvākhyaṃ viśvasaṃbhavam  |
ātmānaṃ sarvabhūtānāṃ parastāttamasaḥ sthitam  || 2,29.11 ||
sarvasyādhārabhūtānāmānandaṃ jyotiravyayam  |
pradhānapuruṣātītamākāśaṃ dahanaṃ śivam  || 2,29.12 ||
tadantaḥ sarvabhāvānāmīśvaraṃ brahmarūpiṇam  |
dhyāyedanādimadvaitamānandādiguṇālayam  || 2,29.13 ||
mahāntaṃ paramaṃ brahma puruṣaṃ satyamavyayam  |
sitetarāruṇākāraṃ maheśaṃ viśvarūpiṇam  || 2,29.14 ||
oṅkārānte'tha cātmānaṃ saṃsthāpya paramātmani  |
ākāśe devamīśānaṃ dhyāyītākāśamadhyagam  || 2,29.15 ||
kāraṇaṃ sarvabhāvānāmānandaikasamāśrayam  |
purāṇaṃ puruṣaṃ śaṃbhuṃ dhyāyanmucyeta bandhanāt || 2,29.16 ||
yadvā guhāyāṃ prakṛtau jagatsaṃmohanālaye  |
vicintya paramaṃ vyoma sarvabhūtaikakāraṇam  || 2,29.17 ||
jīvanaṃ sarvabhūtānāṃ yatra lokaḥ pralīyate  |
ānandaṃ brahmaṇaḥ sūkṣmaṃ yatpaśyanti mumukṣavaḥ  || 2,29.18 ||
tanmadhye nihitaṃ brahma kevalaṃ jñānalakṣaṇam  |
anantaṃ satyamīśānaṃ vicintyāsīta saṃyataḥ  || 2,29.19 ||
guhyādguhyatamaṃ jñānaṃ yatīnāmetadīritam  |
yo'nutiṣṭhenmaheśena so'śnute yogamaiśvaram  || 2,29.20 ||
tasmāddhyānarato nityamātmavidyāparāyaṇaḥ  |
jñānaṃ samabhyasedbrāhmaṃ yena mucyeta bandhanāt || 2,29.21 ||
matvā pṛthaksvamātmānaṃ sarvasmādeva kevalam  |
ānandamajaraṃ jñānaṃ dhyāyīta ca punaḥ param  || 2,29.22 ||
yasmātbhavanti bhūtāni yadgatvā neha jāyate  |
sa tasmādīśvaro devaḥ parasmādyo'dhitiṣṭhati  || 2,29.23 ||
yadantare tadgaganaṃ śāśvataṃ śivamavyayam  |
yadaṃśastatparo yastu sa devaḥ syānmaheśvaraḥ  || 2,29.24 ||
vratāni yāni bhikṣūṇāṃ tathaivopavratāni ca  |
ekaikātikrame teṣāṃ prāyaścittaṃ vidhīyate  || 2,29.25 ||
upetya ca striyaṃ kāmātprāyaścittaṃ samāhitaḥ  |
prāṇāyāmasamāyuktaṃ kuryātsāṃtapanaṃ śuciḥ  || 2,29.26 ||
tataścareta niyamātkṛcchraṃ saṃyatamānasaḥ  |
punarāśramamāgamya caredbhiśruratandritaḥ  || 2,29.27 ||
na dharmayuktamanṛtaṃ hinastīti manīṣiṇaḥ  |
tathāpi ca na kartavyaṃ prasaṅgo hyeṣa dāruṇaḥ  || 2,29.28 ||
ekarātropavāsaśca prāṇāyāmaśataṃ tathā  |
uktvānṛtaṃ prakartavyaṃ yatinā dharmalipsunā  || 2,29.29 ||
paramāpadgatenāpi na kāryaṃ steyamanyataḥ  |
steyādabhyadhikaḥ kaścinnāstyadharma iti smṛtiḥ  |
hiṃsā caiṣāparā diṣṭā yā cātmajñānanāśikā  || 2,29.30 ||
yadetaddraviṇaṃ nāma prāṇa hyete bahiśvarāḥ  |
sa tasya harati prāṇān yo yasya harate dhanam  || 2,29.31 ||
evaṃ kṛtvā sa duṣṭātmā bhinnavṛtto vratāccyutaḥ  |
bhūyo nirvedamāpannaścareccāndrāyaṇavratam  || 2,29.32 ||
vidhinā śāstradṛṣṭena saṃvatsaramiti śrutiḥ  |
bhūyo nirvedamāpannaścaredbhikṣuratandritaḥ  || 2,29.33 ||
akasmādeva hiṃsāṃ tu yadi bhikṣuḥ samācaret |
kuryātkṛchrātikṛcchraṃ tu cāndrāyaṇamathāpi vā  || 2,29.34 ||
skandedindriyadaurbalyātstriyaṃ dṛṣṭvā yatiryadi  |
tena dhārayitavyā vai prāṇāyāmāstu ṣoḍaśa  |
divāskande trirātraṃ syātprāṇāyāmaśataṃ tathā  || 2,29.35 ||
ekānne madhumāṃse ca navaśrāddhe tathaiva ca  |
pratyakṣalavaṇe coktaṃ prājāpatyaṃ viśodhanam  || 2,29.36 ||
dhyānaniṣṭhasya satataṃ naśyate sarvapātakam  |
tasmānmaheśvaraṃ jñātvā tasya dhyānaparo bhavet || 2,29.37 ||
yadbrahma paramaṃ jyotiḥ pratiṣṭhākṣaramadvayam  |
yo'ntarātra paraṃ brahma sa vijñeyo maheśvaraḥ  || 2,29.38 ||
eṣa devo mahādevaḥ kevalaḥ paramaḥ śivaḥ  |
tadevākṣaramadvaitaṃ tadādityāntaraṃ param  || 2,29.39 ||
yasmānmahīyate devaḥ svadhāmni jñānasaṃjñite  |
ātmayogāhvaye tattve mahādevastataḥ smṛtaḥ  || 2,29.40 ||
nānyaddevānmahādevādvyatiriktaṃ prapaśyati  |
tamevātmānamanveti yaḥ sa yāti paraṃ padam  || 2,29.41 ||
manyate ye svamātmānaṃ vibhinnaṃ parameśvarāt |
na te paśyanti taṃ devaṃ vṛthā teṣāṃ pariśramaḥ  || 2,29.42 ||
ekameva paraṃ brahma vijñeyaṃ tattvamavyayam  |
sa devastu mahādevo naitadvijñāya badhyate  || 2,29.43 ||
tasmādyateta niyataṃ yatiḥ saṃyatamānasaḥ  |
jñānayogarataḥ śānto mahādevaparāyaṇaḥ  || 2,29.44 ||
eṣa vaḥ kathito vipro yatīnāmāśramaḥ śubhaḥ  |
pitāmahena vibhunā munīnāṃ pūrvamīritam  || 2,29.45 ||
nāputraśiṣyayogibhyo dadyādidamanuttamam  |
jñānaṃ svayaṃbhuvā proktaṃ yatidharmāśrayaṃ śivam  || 2,29.46 ||
iti yatiniyamānāmetaduktaṃ vidhānaṃ
paśupatiparitoṣe yadbhavedekahetuḥ  |
na bhavati punareṣāmudbhavo vā vināśaḥ
praṇihitamanaso ye nityamevācaranti  || 2,29.47 ||

iti śrīkūrmapurāṇe ṣaṭsāhastryāṃ saṃhitāyāmuparivibhāge ekonatriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ


_____________________________________________________________


vyāsa uvāca
ataḥ paraṃ pravalakṣyāmi prāyaścittavidhiṃ śubham  |
hitāya sarvaviprāṇāṃ doṣāṇāmapanuttaye  || 2,30.1 ||
akṛtvā vihitaṃ karma kṛtvā ninditameva ca  |
doṣamāpnoti puruṣaḥ prāyaścittaṃ viśodhanam  || 2,30.2 ||
prāyaścittamakṛtvā tu na tiṣṭhedbrāhmaṇaḥ kvacit |
yadbrūyurbrāhmaṇāḥ śāntā vidvāṃsastatsamācaret || 2,30.3 ||
vedārthavittamaḥ śānto dharmakāmo'gnimān dvijaḥ  |
sa eva syātparo dharmo yameko'pi vyavasyati  || 2,30.4 ||
anāhitāgnayo viprāstrayo vedārthapāragāḥ  |
yadbrūyurdharmakāmāste tajjñeyaṃ dharmasādhanam  || 2,30.5 ||
anekadharmaśāstrajñā ūhāpohaviśāradāḥ  |
vedādhyayanasaṃpannāḥ saptaite parikīrtitāḥ  || 2,30.6 ||
mīmāṃsājñānatattvajñā vedāntakuśalā dvijāḥ  |
ekaviṃśatisaṃkhyātāḥ prayāścittaṃ vadanti vai  || 2,30.7 ||
brahmahā madyapaḥ steno gurutalpaga eva ca  |
mahāpātakinastvete yaścaitaiḥ saha saṃvaset || 2,30.8 ||
saṃvatsaraṃ tu patitaiḥ saṃsargaṃ kurute tu yaḥ  |
yānaśayyāsanairnityaṃ jānan vai patito bhavet || 2,30.9 ||
yājanaṃ yonisaṃbandhaṃ tathaivādhyāpanaṃ dvijaḥ  |
kṛtvā sadyaḥ patejjñānātsaha bhojanameva ca  || 2,30.10 ||
avijñāyātha yo mohātkuryādadhyāpanaṃ dvijaḥ  |
saṃvatsareṇa patati sahādhyayanameva ca  || 2,30.11 ||
brahmāhā dvādaśābdāni kuṭiṃ kṛtvā vane vaset |
bhaikṣamātmaviśuddhyarthaṃ kṛtvā śavaśirodhvajam  || 2,30.12 ||
brāhmaṇāvasathān sarvān devāgārāṇi varjayet |
vinindan svayamātmānaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ taṃ ca saṃsmaran  || 2,30.13 ||
asaṃkalpitayogyāni saptāgārāṇi saṃviśet |
vidhūme śanakairnityaṃ vyaṅgāre bhuktavajjane  || 2,30.14 ||
ekakālaṃ caredbhaikṣaṃ doṣaṃ vikhyāpayannṛṇām  |
vanyamūlaphalairvāpi vartayeddhairyamākṣitaḥ  || 2,30.15 ||
kapālapāṇiḥ khaṭvāṅgī brahmacaryaparāyaṇaḥ  |
pūrṇe tu dvādaśe varṣe brahmahatyāṃ vyapohati  || 2,30.16 ||
akāmataḥ kṛte pāpe prāyaścittamidaṃ śubham  |
kāmato maraṇācchuddhirjñeyā nānyena kenacit || 2,30.17 ||
kuryādanaśanaṃ vātha bhṛgoḥ patanameva vā  |
jvalantaṃ vā viśedagniṃ jalaṃ vā praviśetsvayam  || 2,30.18 ||
brāhmaṇārthe gavārthe vā samyakprāṇān parityajet |
brahmahatyāpanodārthamantarā vā mṛtasya tu  || 2,30.19 ||
dīrghāmayānvitaṃ vipraṃ kṛtvānāmayameva tu  |
dattvā cānnaṃ sa durbhikṣe brahmahatyāṃ vyapohati  || 2,30.20 ||
aśvamedhāvabhṛthake snātvā vā śudhyate dvijaḥ  |
sarvasvaṃ vā vedavide brāhmaṇāya pradāya tu  || 2,30.21 ||
sarasvatyāstvaruṇayā saṃgame lokaviśrute  |
śudhyettriṣavaṇasnānāttrirātropoṣito dvijaḥ  || 2,30.22 ||
gatvā rāmeśvaraṃ puṇyaṃ snātvā caiva mahodadhau  |
brahmacaryādibhiryukto dṛṣṭvā rudraṃ vimucyate  || 2,30.23 ||
kapālamocanaṃ nāma tīrthaṃ devasya śūlinaḥ  |
snātvābhyarcya pitṝn bhaktyā brahmahatyāṃ vyapohati  || 2,30.24 ||
yatra devādidevena bharaveṇāmitaujasā  |
kapālaṃ sthāpitaṃ pūrvaṃ brahmaṇaḥ parameṣṭhinaḥ  || 2,30.25 ||
samabhyarcya mahādevaṃ tatra bhairavarūpiṇam  |
tarpapitvā pitṝn snātvā mucyate brahmahatyayā  || 2,30.26 ||

iti śrīkūrmapurāṇe ṣaṭsāhastryāṃ saṃhitāyāmuparivibhāge triśo'dhyāyaḥ


_____________________________________________________________


ṛṣaya ūcuḥ
kathaṃ devena rudreṇa śaṅkareṇāmitaujasā  |
kapālaṃ brahmaṇaḥ pūrvaṃ sthāpitaṃ dehajaṃ bhuvi  || 2,31.1 ||
sūta uvāca
śṛṇudhvamṛṣayaḥ puṇyāṃ kathāṃ pāpapraṇāśanīm  |
māhātmyaṃ devadevasya mahādevasya dhīmataḥ  || 2,31.2 ||
purā pitāmahaṃ devaṃ meruśṛṅge maharṣayaḥ  |
procuḥ praṇamya lokādiṃ kimekaṃ tattvamavyayam  || 2,31.3 ||
sa māyayā maheśasya mohito lokasaṃbhavaḥ  |
avijñāya paraṃ bhāvaṃ svātmānaṃ prāha dharṣiṇam  || 2,31.4 ||
ahaṃ dhātā jagadyoniḥ svayaṃbhūreka īśvaraḥ  |
anādimatparaṃ brahma māmabhyarcya vimucyate  || 2,31.5 ||
ahaṃ hi sarvadevānāṃ pravartakanivartakaḥ  |
na vidyate cābhyadhiko matto lokeṣu kaścana  || 2,31.6 ||
tasyaivaṃ manyamānasya jajñe nārāyaṇāṃśajaḥ  |
provāca prahasan vākyaṃ roṣatāmravilocanaḥ  || 2,31.7 ||
kiṃ kāraṇamidaṃ brahman vartate tava sāṃpratam  |
ajñānayogayuktasya na tvetaducitaṃ tava  || 2,31.8 ||
ahaṃ dhātā hi lokānāṃ yajño nārāyaṇaḥ prabhuḥ  |
na māmṛte'sya jagato jīvanaṃ sarvadā kvacit || 2,31.9 ||
ahameva paraṃ jyotirahameva parā gatiḥ  |
matpreritena bhavatā sṛṣṭaṃ bhuvanamaṇḍalam  || 2,31.10 ||
evaṃ vivadatormohātparasparajayaiṣiṇoḥ  |
ājagmuryatra tau devau vedāścatvāra eva hi  || 2,31.11 ||
anvīkṣya devaṃ brahmāṇaṃ yajñātmānaṃ ca saṃsthitam  |
procuḥ saṃvignahṛdayā yāthātmyaṃ parameṣṭhinaḥ  || 2,31.12 ||
ṛgveda uvāca
yasyāntaḥ sthāni bhūtāni yasmātsarvaṃ pravartate  |
yadāhustatparaṃ tattvaṃ sa devaḥ syānmaheśvaraḥ  || 2,31.13 ||
yajurveda uvāca
yo yajñairakhilairīśo yogena ca samarcyate  |
yamāhurīśvaraṃ devaṃ sa devaḥ syātpinākadhṛk || 2,31.14 ||
sāmaveda uvāca
yenedaṃ bhrāmyate cakraṃ yadākāśāntaraṃ śivam  |
yogibhirvidyate tattvaṃ mahādevaḥ sa śaṅkaraḥ  || 2,31.15 ||
atharvaveda uvāca
yaṃ prapaśyanti yogeśaṃ yatanto yatayaḥ param  |
maheśaṃ puruṣaṃ rudraṃ sa devo bhagavān bhavaḥ  || 2,31.16 ||
evaṃ sa bhagavān brahmā vedānāmīritaṃ śubham  |
śrutvāha prahasan vākyaṃ viśvātmāpi vimohitaḥ  || 2,31.17 ||
kathaṃ tatparamaṃ brahma sarvasaṅgavivarjitam  |
ramate bhāryayā sārdhaṃ pramathaiścātigarvitaiḥ  || 2,31.18 ||
itirite'tha bhagavān praṇavātmā sanātanaḥ  |
amūrto mūrtimān bhūtvā vacaḥ prāha pitāmaham  || 2,31.19 ||
praṇava uvāca
na hyeṣa bhagavān patnyā svātmano vyatiriktayā  |
kadācidramate rudrastādṛśo hi maheśvaraḥ  || 2,31.20 ||
ayaṃ sa bhagavānīśaḥ svayañjyotiḥ sanātanaḥ  |
svānandabhūtā kathitā devī nāgantukā śivā  || 2,31.21 ||
ityevamukte'pi tadā yajñamūrterajasya ca  |
nājñānamagamannāśamīśvarasyaiva māyayā  || 2,31.22 ||
tadantare mahājyotirviriñco viśvabhāvanaḥ  |
prāpaśyadadbhutaṃ divyaṃ pūrayan gaganāntaram  || 2,31.23 ||
tanmadhyasaṃsthaṃ vimalaṃ maṇḍalaṃ tejasojjvalam  |
vyomamadhyagataṃ divyaṃ prādurāsīddvijottamāḥ  || 2,31.24 ||
sa dṛṣṭvā vadanaṃ divyaṃ mūrdhni lokapitāmahaḥ  |
tena tanmaṇjalaṃ ghoramālokayadaninditam  || 2,31.25 ||
prajajvālātikopena brahmaṇaḥ pañcamaṃ śiraḥ  |
kṣaṇādadṛśyata mahān puruṣo nīlalohitaḥ  || 2,31.26 ||
triśūlapiṅgalo devo nāgayajñopavītavān  |
taṃ prāha bhagavān brahmā śaṅkaraṃ nīlalohitam  || 2,31.27 ||
jānāmi bhavataḥ pūrvaṃ lalāṭādeva śaṅkara  |
prādurbhāvaṃ maheśānmāmeva śaraṇaṃ vraja  || 2,31.28 ||
śrutvā sagarvavacanaṃ padmayoneratheśvaraḥ  |
prāhiṇotpuruṣaṃ kālaṃ bhairavaṃ lokadāhakam  || 2,31.29 ||
sa kṛtvā sumahadyuddhaṃ brahmaṇā kālabhairavaḥ  |
cakarta tasya vadanaṃ viriñcasyātha pañcamam  || 2,31.30 ||
nikṛttavadano devo brahmā devena śaṃbhunā  |
mamāra ceśayogena jīvitaṃ prāpa viśvasṛk || 2,31.31 ||
athānupaśyadgiriśaṃ maṇḍalāntarasaṃsthitam  |
samāsīnaṃ mahādevyā mahādevaṃ sanātanam  || 2,31.32 ||
bhujaṅgarājavalayaṃ candrāvayavabhūṣaṇam  |
koṭisūryapratīkāśaṃ jaṭājūṭavirājitam  || 2,31.33 ||
śārdūlacarmavasanaṃ divyamālāsamanvitam  |
triśūlapāṇiṃ duṣprekṣyaṃ yoginaṃ bhūtibhūṣaṇam  || 2,31.34 ||
yamantarā yoganiṣṭhāḥ prapaśyanti hṛdīśvaram  |
tamādidevaṃ brahmāṇaṃ mahādevaṃ dadarśa ha  || 2,31.35 ||
yasya sā paramā devī śaktirākāśasaṃsthitā  |
so'nantaiśvaryayogātmā maheśo dṛśyate kila  || 2,31.36 ||
yasyāśeṣajagadbījaṃ vilayaṃ yāti mohanam  |
sakṛtpraṇāmamātreṇa sa rudraḥ khalu dṛśyate  || 2,31.37 ||
yo'tha nācāraniratān svabhaktāneva kevalam  |
vimocayati lokānāṃ nāyako dṛśyate kila  || 2,31.38 ||
yasya vedavidaḥ śāntā nirdvandvā brahmacāriṇaḥ  |
vidanti vimalaṃ rūpaṃ sa śaṃbhurdṛśyate kila  || 2,31.39 ||
yasya brahmādayo devā ṛṣayo brahmavādinaḥ  |
arcayanti sadā liṅgaṃ viśveśaḥ khalu dṛśyate  || 2,31.40 ||
yasyāśeṣajagadbījaṃ vilayaṃ yāti mohanam  |
sakṛtpraṇāmamātreṇa sa rudraḥ khalu dṛśyate  || 2,31.41 ||
vidyāsahāyo bhagavān yasyāsau maṇḍalāntaram  |
hiraṇyagarbhaputro'sāvīśvaro dṛśyate kila  || 2,31.42 ||
yasyāśeṣajagatsūtirvijñānatanurīśvarī  |
na muñcati sadā pārśvaṃ śaṅkaro'sāvadṛśyata  || 2,31.43 ||
puṣpaṃ vā yadi vā patraṃ yatpādayugale jalam  |
dattvā tarati saṃsāraṃ rudro'sau dṛśyate kila  || 2,31.44 ||
tatsannidhāne sakalaṃ niyacchati sanātanaḥ  |
kālaḥ kila sa yogātmā kālakālo hi dṛśyate  || 2,31.45 ||
jīvanaṃ sarvalokānāṃ trilokasyaiva bhūṣaṇam  |
somaḥ sa dṛśyate devaḥ somo yasya vibhūṣaṇam  || 2,31.46 ||
devyā saha sadā sākṣādyasya yogaḥ svabhāvataḥ  |
gīyate paramā muktiḥ sa yogī dṛśyate kila  || 2,31.47 ||
yogino yogatattvajñā viyogābhimukhāniśam  |
yogaṃ dhyāyanti devyāsau sa yogī dṛśyate kila  || 2,31.48 ||
so'nuvīkṣya mahādevaṃ mahādevyā sanātanam  |
varāsane samāsīnamavāpa paramāṃ smṛtim  || 2,31.49 ||
labdhvā māheśvarīṃ divyāṃ saṃsmṛtiṃ bhagavānajaḥ  |
toṣayāmāsa varadaṃ somaṃ somavibhūṣaṇam  || 2,31.50 ||
brahmovāca
namo devāya mahate mahādevyai namo namaḥ  |
namaḥ śivāya śāntāya śivāyai śāntaye namaḥ  || 2,31.51 ||
oṃ namo brahmaṇe tubhyaṃ vidyāyai te namo namaḥ  |
namo mūlaprakṛtaye maheśāya namo namaḥ  || 2,31.52 ||
namo vijñānadehāya cintāyai te namo namaḥ  |
namaste kālakālāya īśvarāyai namo namaḥ  || 2,31.53 ||
namo namo'stu rudrāya rudrāṇyai te namo namaḥ  |
namo namaste kāmāya māyāyai ca namo namaḥ  || 2,31.54 ||
niyantre sarvakāryāṇāṃ kṣobhikāyai namo namaḥ  |
namo'stu te prakṛtaye namo nārāyaṇāya ca  || 2,31.55 ||
yogādāyai namastubhyaṃ yogināṃ gurave namaḥ  |
namaḥ saṃsāranāśāya saṃsārotpattaye namaḥ  || 2,31.56 ||
nityānandāya vibhave namo'stvānandamūrtaye  |
namaḥ kāryavihīnāya viśvaprakṛtaye namaḥ  || 2,31.57 ||
oṅkāramūrtaye tubhyaṃ tadantaḥ saṃsthitāya ca  |
namaste vyomasaṃsthāya vyomaśaktyai namo namaḥ  || 2,31.58 ||
iti somāṣṭakeneśaṃ praṇanāma pitāmahaḥ  |
papāta daṇḍavadbhūmau gṛṇan vai śatarudriyam  || 2,31.59 ||
atha devo mahādevaḥ praṇatārtiharo haraḥ  |
provācotthāpya hastābhyāṃ prato'smi tava sāṃpratam  || 2,31.60 ||
dattvāsau paramaṃ yogamaiśvaryamatulaṃ mahat |
provācāgre sthitaṃ devaṃ nīlalohitamīśvaram  || 2,31.61 ||
eṣa brahmāsya jagataḥ saṃpūjyaḥ prathamaḥ sutaḥ  |
ātmano rakṣaṇīyaste gururjyeṣṭhaḥ pitā tava  || 2,31.62 ||
ayaṃ purāṇapuruṣo na hantavyastvayānagha  |
svayogaiśvaryamāhātmyānmāmeva śaraṇaṃ gataḥ  || 2,31.63 ||
ayaṃ ca yajño bhagavān sagarvo bhavatānagha  |
śāsitavyo viriñcasya dhāraṇīyaṃ śirastvayā  || 2,31.64 ||
brahmahatyāpanodārthaṃ vrataṃ lokāya darśayan  |
carasva satataṃ bhikṣāṃ saṃsthāpaya suradvijān  || 2,31.65 ||
ityetaduktvā vacanaṃ bhagavān parameśvaraḥ  |
sthānaṃ svābhāvikaṃ divyaṃ yayau tatparamaṃ padam  || 2,31.66 ||
tataḥ sa bhagavānīśaḥ kaparde nīlalohitaḥ  |
grāhayāmāsa vadanaṃ brahmaṇaḥ kālabhairavam  || 2,31.67 ||
cara tvaṃ pāpanāśārthaṃ vrataṃ lokahitāvaham  |
kapālahasto bhagavān bhikṣāṃ gṛhṇātu sarvataḥ  || 2,31.68 ||
uktvaivaṃ prāhiṇotkanyāṃ brahmahatyāmiti śrutām  |
daṃṣṭrākarālavadanāṃ jvālāmālāvibhūṣaṇām  || 2,31.69 ||
yāvadvārāṇasīṃ divyāṃ purīmeṣa gamiṣyati  |
tāvattvaṃ bhīṣaṇe kālamanugaccha trilocanam  || 2,31.70 ||
evamābhāṣya kālāgniṃ prāha devo maheśvaraḥ  |
aṭasva nikhilaṃ lokaṃ bhikṣārtho manniyogataḥ  || 2,31.71 ||
yadā drakṣyasi deveśaṃ nārāyaṇamanāmayam  |
tadāsau vakṣyati spaṣṭamupāyaṃ pāpaśodhanam  || 2,31.72 ||
sa devadevatāvākyamākarṇya bhagavān haraḥ  |
kapālapāṇirviśvātmā cacāra bhuvanatrayam  || 2,31.73 ||
āsthāya vikṛtaṃ veṣaṃ dīpyamānaṃ svatejasā  |
śrīmatpavitramatulaṃ jaṭājūṭavirājitam  || 2,31.74 ||
koṭisūryapratīkāśaiḥ pramathaiścātigarvitaiḥ  |
bhāti kālāgninayano mahādevaḥ samāvṛtaḥ  || 2,31.75 ||
pītvā kadamṛtaṃ divyamānandaṃ parameṣṭhinaḥ  |
līlāvilāsūbahulo lokānāgacchatīśvaraḥ  || 2,31.76 ||
taṃ dṛṣṭvā kālavadanaṃ śaṅkaraṃ kālabhairavam  |
rūpalāvaṇyasaṃpannaṃ nārīkulamagādanu  || 2,31.77 ||
gāyanti vividhaṃ gītaṃ nṛtyanti purataḥ prabhoḥ  |
sasmitaṃ prekṣya vadanaṃ cakrurbhrūbhaṅgameva ca  || 2,31.78 ||
sa devadānavādīnāṃ deśānabhyetya śūladhṛk |
jagāma viṣṇorbhavanaṃ yatrāste madhusūdanaḥ  || 2,31.79 ||
nirīkṣya divyabhavanaṃ śaṅkaro lokaśaṅkaraḥ  |
sahaiva bhūtapravaraiḥ praveṣṭumupacakrame  || 2,31.80 ||
avijñāya paraṃ bhāvaṃ divyaṃ tatpārameśvaram  |
nyavārayattriśūlāṅkaṃ dvārapālo mahābalaḥ  || 2,31.81 ||
śaṅkhacakragadāpāṇiḥ pītavāsā mahābhujaḥ  |
viṣvaksena iti khyāto viṣṇoraṃśasamudbhavaḥ  || 2,31.82 ||
athainaṃ śaṅkaragaṇo yuyudhe viṣṇusaṃbhavam  |
bhīṣaṇo bhairavādeśātkālavega iti śrutaḥ  || 2,31.83 ||
vijitya taṃ kālavegaṃ krodhasaṃraktalocanaḥ  |
rudrāyābhimukhaṃ raudraṃ cikṣepa ca sudarśanam  || 2,31.84 ||
atha devo mahādevastripurāristriśūlabhṛt |
tamāpatantaṃ sāvajñamālokayadamitrajit || 2,31.85 ||
tadantare mahadbhūtaṃ yugāntadahanopamam  |
śūlenorasi nirbhidya pātayāmāsa taṃ bhuvi  || 2,31.86 ||
sa śūlābhihato'tyarthaṃ tyaktvā svaṃ paramaṃ balam  |
tatyāja jīvitaṃ dṛṣṭvā mṛtyuṃ vyādhihatā iva  || 2,31.87 ||
nihatya viṣṇupuruṣaṃ sārdhaṃ pramathapuṅgavaiḥ  |
viveśa cāntaragṛhaṃ samādāya kalevaram  || 2,31.88 ||
nirīkṣya jagato hetumīśvaraṃ bhagavān hariḥ  |
śiro lalāṭātsaṃbhidya raktadhārāmapātayat || 2,31.89 ||
gṛhāṇa bhagavan bhikṣāṃ madīyāmamitadyute  |
na vidyate'nābhyuditā tava tripuramardana  || 2,31.90 ||
na saṃpūrṇaṃ kapālaṃ tadbrahmaṇaḥ parameṣṭhinaḥ  |
divyaṃ varṣasahasraṃ tu sā ca dhārā pravāhitā  || 2,31.91 ||
athābravītkālarudraṃ harirnārāyaṇaḥ prabhuḥ  |
saṃstūya vaidikairmantrairbahumānapuraḥ saram  || 2,31.92 ||
kimarthametadvadanaṃ brahmaṇo bhavatā dhṛtam  |
provāca vṛttamakhilaṃ bhagavān parameśvaraḥ  || 2,31.93 ||
samāhūya hṛṣīkeśo brahmahatyāmathācyutaḥ  |
prārthayāmāsa deveśo vimuñceti triśūlinam  || 2,31.94 ||
na tatyājātha sā pārśvaṃ vyāhṛtāpi murāriṇā  |
ciraṃ dhyātvā jagadyoniḥ śaṅkaraṃ prāha sarvavit || 2,31.95 ||
vrajasva bhagavan divyāṃ purīṃ vārāṇasīṃ śubhām  |
yatrākhilajagaddoṣaṃ kṣipraṃ nāśayatāśvaraḥ  || 2,31.96 ||
tataḥ śarvāṇi guhyāni tīrthānyāyatanāni ca  |
jagāma līlayā devo lokānāṃ hitakāmyayā  || 2,31.97 ||
saṃstūyamānaḥ pramathairmahāyogairitastataḥ  |
nṛtyamāno mahāyogī hastanyastakalevaraḥ  || 2,31.98 ||
tamabhyadhāvadbhagavān harirnārāyaṇaḥ svayam  |
athāsthāyāparaṃ rūpaṃ nṛtyadarśanalālasaḥ  || 2,31.99 ||
nirīkṣamāṇo novindaṃ vṛṣendrāṅkitaśāsanaḥ  |
sasmito'nantayogātmā nṛtyati sma punaḥ punaḥ  || 2,31.100 ||
atha sānucaro rudraḥ saharirdharmavāhanaḥ  |
bheje mahādevapurīṃ vārāṇasīmiti śrutām  || 2,31.101 ||
praviṣṭamātre deveśe brahmahatyā kapardini  |
hā hetyuktvā sanādaṃ sā pātālaṃ prāpa duḥ khitā  || 2,31.102 ||
praviśya paramaṃ sthānaṃ kapālaṃ brahmaṇo haraḥ  |
gaṇānāmagrato devaḥ sthāpayāmāsa śaṅkaraḥ  || 2,31.103 ||
sthāpayitvā mahādevo dadau tacca kalevaram  |
uktvā sajīvamastvīśo viṣṇave sa ghṛṇānidhiḥ  || 2,31.104 ||
ye smaranti mamājastraṃ kāpālaṃ veṣamuttamam  |
teṣāṃ vinaśyati kṣipramihāmutra ca pātakam  || 2,31.105 ||
āgamya tīrthapravare snānaṃ kṛtvā vidhānataḥ  |
tarpayitvā pitṝn devānmucyate brahmahatyayā  || 2,31.106 ||
aśāśvataṃ jagajjñātvā ye'smin sthāne vasanti vai  |
dehānte tatparaṃ jñānaṃ dadāmi paramaṃ padam  || 2,31.107 ||
itīdamuktvā bhagavān samāliṅgya janārdanam  |
sahaiva pramatheśānaiḥ kṣaṇādantaradhīyata  || 2,31.108 ||
sa labdhvā bhagavān kṛṣṇo viṣvaksenaṃ triśūlinaḥ  |
svaṃ deśamagattūrṇaṃ gṛhītvāṃ paramaṃ vapuḥ  || 2,31.109 ||
etadvaḥ kathitaṃ puṇyaṃ mahāpātakanāśanam  |
kapālamocanaṃ tīrthaṃ sthāṇoḥ priyakaraṃ śubham  || 2,31.110 ||
ya imaṃ paṭhate'dhyāyaṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ samīpataḥ  |
vācikairmānasaiḥ pāpaiḥ kāyikaiśca vimucyate  || 2,31.111 ||

iti śrīkūrmapārāṇe ṣaṭsāhastryāṃ saṃhitāyāmuparivibhāge ekatriśo'dhyāyaḥ

_____________________________________________________________


vyāsa uvāca
surāpastu surāṃ taptāmagnivarṇāṃ svayaṃ pibet |
tayā sa kāye nirdagdhe mucyate tu dvijottamaḥ  || 2,32.1 ||
gomūtramagnivarṇaṃ vā gośakṛdrasameva vā  |
payo ghṛtaṃ jalaṃ vātha mucyate pātakāttataḥ  || 2,32.2 ||
jalārdravāsāḥ prayato dhyātvā nārāyaṇaṃ harim  |
brahmahatyāvrataṃ cātha carettatpāpaśāntaye  || 2,32.3 ||
suvarṇasteyakṛdvipro rājānamabhigamya tu  |
svakarma khyāpayan brūyānmāṃ bhavānanuśāstviti  || 2,32.4 ||
gṛhītvā musalaṃ rājā sakṛdhanyāttataḥ svayam  |
vadhe tu śuddhyate steno brāhmaṇastapasaiva vā  || 2,32.5 ||
skandhenādāya musalaṃ lakuṭaṃ vāpi khādiram  |
śaktiṃ cobhayatastīkṣṇāmāyasaṃ daṇḍameva vā  || 2,32.6 ||
rājā tena ca gantavyo muktakeśena dhāvatā  |
ācakṣāṇena tatpāpamevaṅkarmāsmi śādhi mām  || 2,32.7 ||
śāsanādvā vimokṣādvā stenaḥ steyādvimucyate  |
aśāsitvā tu taṃ rājāstenasyāpnoti kilbiṣam  || 2,32.8 ||
tapasāpanunutsustu suvarṇasteyajaṃ malam  |
cīravāsā dvijo'raṇye caredbrahmahaṇo vratam  || 2,32.9 ||
snātvāśvamedhāvabhṛthe pūtaḥ syādathavā dvijaḥ  |
pradadyādvātha viprebhyaḥ svātmatulyaṃ hiraṇyakam  || 2,32.10 ||
caredvā vatsaraṃ kṛcchraṃ brahmacaryaparāyaṇaḥ  |
brāhmaṇaḥ svarṇahārī tu tatpāpasyāpanuttaye  || 2,32.11 ||
gurorbhāryāṃ samāruhya brāhmaṇaḥ kāmamohitaḥ  |
avagūhetstriyaṃ taptāṃ dīptāṃ kārṣṇāyasīṃ kṛtām  || 2,32.12 ||
svayaṃ vā śiśnavṛṣaṇāvutkṛtyādhāya cāñcalau  |
ātiṣṭheddakṣiṇāmāśāmānipātādajihmagaḥ  || 2,32.13 ||
gurvarthaṃ vā hataḥ śuddhyeccaredvā brahmahā vratam  |
śākhāṃ vā kaṇṭakopetāṃ pariṣvajyātha vatsaram  |
adhaḥ śayīta niyato mucyate gurutalpagaḥ  || 2,32.14 ||
kṛcchraṃ vābdaṃ caredvipraścīravāsāḥ samāhitaḥ  |
aśvamedhāvabhṛthake snātvā vā śuddhyate naraḥ  || 2,32.15 ||
kāle'ṣṭame vā bhuñjāno brahmacārī sadāvratī  |
sthānāsanābhyāṃ viharaṃstrirahno'bhyupayannapaḥ  || 2,32.16 ||
adhaḥ śāyī tribhirvarṣaistadvyapohati pātakam  |
cāndrāyaṇāni vā kuryātpañca catvāri vā punaḥ  || 2,32.17 ||
patitaiḥ saṃprayuktānāmatha vakṣyāmi niṣkṛtim  |
patitena tu saṃsargaṃ yo yena kurute dvijaḥ  |
sa tatpāpāpanodārthaṃ tasyaiva vratamācaret || 2,32.18 ||
taptakṛcchraṃ caredvātha saṃvatsaramatandritaḥ  |
ṣāṇmāsike tu saṃsarge prāyaścittārdhamarhati  || 2,32.19 ||
ebhirvratairapohanti mahāpātakino malam  |
puṇyatīrthābhigamanātpṛthivyāṃ vātha niṣkṛtiḥ  || 2,32.20 ||
brahmahatyā surāpānaṃ steyaṃ gurvaṅganāgamaḥ  |
kṛtvā taiścāpi saṃsargaṃ brāhmaṇaḥ kāmakārataḥ  || 2,32.21 ||
kuryādanaśanaṃ vipraḥ puṇyatīrthe samāhitaḥ  |
jvalantaṃ vā viśedagniṃ dhyātvā devaṃ kapardinam  || 2,32.22 ||
na hyanyā niṣkṛtirdṛṣṭā munibhirdharmavādibhiḥ  |
tasmātpuṇyeṣu tīrtheṣu dahedvāpi svadehakam  || 2,32.23 ||
gatvā duhitaraṃ vipraḥ svasāraṃ vā snuṣāmapi  |
praviśejjvalanaṃ dīptaṃ matipūrvamiti sthitiḥ  || 2,32.24 ||
mātṛṣvasāṃ mātulānīṃ tathaiva ca pitṛṣvasām  |
bhāgineyīṃ samāruhya kuryātkṛcchrātikṛcchrakau  || 2,32.25 ||
cāndrāyaṇaṃ ca kurvota tasya pāpasya śāntaye  |
dhyāyan devaṃ jagadyonimanādinidhanaṃ param  || 2,32.26 ||
bhrātṛbhāryāṃ samāruhya kuryāttatpāpaśāntaye  |
cāndrāyaṇāni catvāri pañca vā susamāhitaḥ  || 2,32.27 ||
paitṛṣvastreyīṃ gatvā tu svastreyāṃ mātureva ca  |
mātulasya sutāṃ vāpi gatvā cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret || 2,32.28 ||
sakhibhāryāṃ samāruhya gatvā śyālīṃ tathaiva ca  |
ahorātroṣito bhūtvā taptakṛcchraṃ samācaret || 2,32.29 ||
udakyāgamane viprastrirātreṇa viśudhyati  |
cāṇḍālīgamane caiva taptakṛcchratrayaṃ viduḥ  |
saha sāṃtapanenāsya nānyathā niṣkṛtiḥ smṛtā  || 2,32.30 ||
mātṛgotrāṃ samāsādya samānapravarāṃ tathā  |
cādrāyaṇena śudhyeta prayatātmā samāhitaḥ  || 2,32.31 ||
brāhmaṇo brāhmaṇīṃ gatvā gṛcchramekaṃ samācaret |
kanyakāṃ dūṣayitvā tu careccāndrāyaṇavratam  || 2,32.32 ||
amānuṣīṣu puruṣa udakyāyāmayoniṣu  |
retaḥ siktvā jale caiva kṛcchraṃ sāntapanaṃ caret || 2,32.33 ||
bandhakīgamane viprastrirātreṇa viśuddhyati  |
gavi bhathunamāsevya careccāndrāyaṇavratam  || 2,32.34 ||
ajāvī maithunaṃ kṛtvā prājāpatyaṃ careddvijaḥ  |
patitāṃ ca striyaṃ gatvā tribhiḥ kṛcchrai rviśuddhyati  || 2,32.35 ||
pulkasīgamane caiva kracchraṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret |
naṭīṃ śailūṣakīṃ caiva rajakīṃ veṇujīvinīm  |
gatvā cāndrāyaṇaṃ kuryāttathā carmopajīvinīm  || 2,32.36 ||
brahāmacārī striyaṃ gacchetkathañcitkāmamohitaḥ  |
saptagāraṃ caredbhaikṣaṃ vasitvā gardabhājinam  || 2,32.37 ||
upaspṛśettriṣavaṇaṃ svapāpaṃ parikīrtayan  |
saṃvatsareṇa caikena tasmātpāpātpramucyate  || 2,32.38 ||
brahmahatyāvrataṃ vāpi ṣaṇmāsānācaredyamī  |
mucyate hyavakīrṇo tu brāhmaṇānumate sthitaḥ  || 2,32.39 ||
saptarātramakṛtvā tu bhaikṣacaryāgnipūjanam  |
retasaśca samutsarge prāyaścittaṃ samācaret || 2,32.40 ||
oṅkārapūrvikābhistu mahāvyāhṛtibhiḥ sadā  |
saṃvatsaraṃ tu bhuñjāno naktaṃ bhikṣāśanaḥ śuciḥ  || 2,32.41 ||
sāvitrīṃ ca japeccaiva nityaṃ krodhavivarjitaḥ  |
nadītīreṣu tīrtheṣu tasmātpāpādvimucyate  || 2,32.42 ||
hatvā tu kṣatriyaṃ vipraḥ kuryādbrahmahaṇo vratam  |
akāmato vai ṣaṇmāsān dadyān pañcaśataṃ gavām  || 2,32.43 ||
abdaṃ careta niyato vanavāsī samāhitaḥ  |
prājāpatyaṃ sāntapanaṃ taptakṛcchraṃ tu vā svayam  || 2,32.44 ||
pramāpyākāmato vaiśyaṃ kuryātsaṃvatsaradvayam  |
gosahasraṃ sapādaṃ ca dadyādbrahmahaṇo vratam  |
kṛcchrātikṛcchrau vā kuryāccāndrāyaṇamathāvi vā  || 2,32.45 ||
saṃvatsaraṃ vrataṃ kuryācchūdraṃ hatvā pramādataḥ  |
gosahasrārdhapādaṃ ca dadyāttatpāpaśāntaye  || 2,32.46 ||
aṣṭau varṣāṇi ṣaṭtrīṇi kuryādbrahmahaṇo vratam  |
hatvā tu kṣatriyaṃ vaiśyaṃ śūdraṃ caiva yathākramam  || 2,32.47 ||
nihatya brāhmaṇīṃ viprastvaṣṭavarṣaṃ vrataṃ caret |
rājanyāṃ varṣaṣaṭkaṃ tu vaiśyāṃ saṃvatsaratrayam  |
vatsareṇa viśuddhyeta śūdrāṃ hatvā dvijottamaḥ  || 2,32.48 ||
vaiśyāṃ hatvā pramādena kiñciddadyāddvijātaye  |
antyajānāṃ vadhe caiva kuryāccāndrāyaṇaṃ vratam  |
parākeṇāthavā śuddhirityāha bhagavānajaḥ  || 2,32.49 ||
maṇḍūkaṃ nakulaṃ kākaṃ dandaśūkaṃ ca mūṣikam  |
śvānaṃ hatvā dvijaḥ kuryātṣoḍaśāṃśaṃ vrataṃ tataḥ  || 2,32.50 ||
payaḥ pibettrirātraṃ tu śvānaṃ hatvā suyantritaḥ  |
mārjāraṃ vātha nakulaṃ yojanaṃ vādhvano vrajet |
kṛcchraṃ dvādaśarātraṃ tu kuryādaśvavadhe dvijaḥ  || 2,32.51 ||
abhrīṃ kārṣṇāyasīṃ dadyātsarpaṃ hatvā dvijottamaḥ  |
palālabhāraṃ ṣaṇḍaṃ ca saisakaṃ caikamāṣakam  || 2,32.52 ||
dhṛtakumbhaṃ varāhaṃ ca tiladroṇaṃ ca tittirim  |
śukaṃ dvihāyanaṃ vatsaṃ krauñcaṃ hatvā trihāyanam  || 2,32.53 ||
hatvā haṃsaṃ balākāṃ ca bakaṃ barhiṇameva ca  |
vānaraṃ śyenabhāsau ca sparśayedbrāhmaṇāya gām  || 2,32.54 ||
kravyādāṃstu mṛgān hatvā dhenuṃ dadyātpayasvinīm  |
akravyādān vatsatarīmuṣṭraṃ hatvā tu kṛṣṇalam  || 2,32.55 ||
kiñcideva tu viprāya dadyādasthimatāṃ vadhe  |
anasthnāṃ caiva hiṃsāyāṃ prāṇāyāmena śudhyati  || 2,32.56 ||
phaladānāṃ tu vṛkṣāṇāṃ chedane japyamṛkśatam  |
gulmavallīlatānāṃ tu puṣpitānāṃ ca vīrudhām  || 2,32.57 ||
anyeṣāṃ caiva vṛkṣāṇāṃ sarasānāṃ ca sarvaśaḥ  |
phalapuṣpodbhavānāṃ ca ghṛtaprāśo viśodhanam  || 2,32.58 ||
hastināṃ ca vadhe dṛṣṭaṃ taptakṛcchraṃ viśodhanam  |
cāndrāyaṇaṃ parākaṃ vā gāṃ hatvā tu pramādataḥ  |
matipūrvaṃ vadhe cāsyāḥ prāyaścittaṃ na vidyate  || 2,32.59 ||

iti śrīkūrmapurāṇe ṣaṭsāhastryāṃ saṃhitāyāmuparivibhāge dvātriśo'dhyāyaḥ


_____________________________________________________________


vyāsa uvāca
manuṣyāṇāṃ tu haraṇaṃ kṛtvā strīṇāṃ gṛhasya ca  |
vāpīkūpajalānāṃ ca śudhyeccāndrāyaṇena tu  || 2,33.1 ||
dravyāṇāmalpasārāṇāṃ steyaṃ kṛtvānyaveśmataḥ  |
caretsāṃtapanaṃ kṛcchraṃ tanniryātyātmaśuddhaye  || 2,33.2 ||
dhānyānnadhanacauryaṃ tu kṛtvā kāmāddvijottamaḥ  |
svajātīyagṛhādeva kṛcchrārdhena viśuddhyati  || 2,33.3 ||
bhakṣabhojyāpaharaṇe yānaśayyāsanasya ca  |
puṣpamūlaphalānāṃ ca pañcagavyaṃ viśodhanam  || 2,33.4 ||
tṛṇakāṣṭhadrumāṇāṃ ca śuṣkānnasya guḍasya ca  |
cailacarmāmiṣāṇāṃ ca trirātraṃ syādabhojanam  || 2,33.5 ||
maṇimuktāpravālānāṃ tāmrasya rajatasya ca  |
ayaḥ kāṃsyopalānāṃ ca dvādaśāhaṃ kaṇāśanam  || 2,33.6 ||
kārpāsakīṭajorṇānāṃ dviśaphaikaśaphasya ca  |
pakṣigandhauṣadhīnāṃ ca rajvāścaiva tryahaṃ payaḥ  || 2,33.7 ||
naramāṃsāśanaṃ kṛtvā cāndrāyaṇamathācaret |
kākaṃ caiva tathā śvānaṃ jagdhvā hastinameva ca  |
varāhaṃ kukkuṭaṃ cātha taptakṛcchreṇa śudhyati  || 2,33.8 ||
kravyādānāṃ ca māṃsāni purīṣaṃ mūtrameva ca  |
gogomāyukapīnāṃ ca tadeva vratamācaret |
upoṣya dvādaśāhaṃ tu kūṣmāṇḍairjuhuyādghṛtam  || 2,33.9 ||
nakulolūkamārjāraṃ jagdhvā sāṃtapanaṃ caret |
śvāpadoṣṭrakharāñjagdhvā taptakṛcchreṇa śuddhyati  |
vratavaccaiva saṃskāraṃ pūrveṇa vidhinaiva tu  || 2,33.10 ||
bakaṃ caiva balākaṃ ca haṃsaṃ kāraṇḍavaṃ tathā  |
cakravākaṃ plavaṃ jagghvā dvādaśāhamabhojanam  || 2,33.11 ||
kapotaṃ ṭiṭṭibhaṃ caiva śukaṃ sārasameva ca  |
ulūkaṃ jālapādaṃ ca jagdhvāpyetadvrataṃ caret || 2,33.12 ||
śiśumāraṃ tathā cāṣaṃ matsyamāṃsaṃ tathaiva ca  |
jagdhvā caiva kaṭāhārametadeva caredvratam  || 2,33.13 ||
kokilaṃ caiva matsyāṃśca maṇḍukaṃ bhujagaṃ tathā  |
gomūtrayāvakāhāro māsenaikena śuddhyati  || 2,33.14 ||
jalecarāṃśca jalajān prattudānnakhaviṣkirān  |
raktapādāṃstathā jagdhvā saptāhaṃ caitadācaret || 2,33.15 ||
śuno māṃsaṃ śuṣkamāṃsamātmārthaṃ ca tathā kṛtam  |
bhuktvā māsaṃ caredetattatpāpasyāpanuttaye  || 2,33.16 ||
vārtākaṃ bhustṛṇaṃ śigruṃ khukhuṇḍaṃ karakaṃ tathā  |
prājāpatyaṃ carejjagdhvā śaṅkhaṃ kumbhīkameva ca  || 2,33.17 ||
palāṇḍuṃ laśunaṃ caiva bhuktvā cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret |
nālikāṃ taṇḍulīyaṃ ca prājāpatyena śuddhyati  || 2,33.18 ||
aśmāntakaṃ tathā potaṃ taptakṛcchreṇa śuddhyati  |
prājāpatyena śuddhiḥ syātkakkubhāṇḍasya bhakṣaṇe  || 2,33.19 ||
alābuṃ kiṃśukaṃ caiva bhuktvā caitadvrataṃ caret |
udumbaraṃ ca kāmena taptakṛcchreṇa śuddhyati  || 2,33.20 ||
vṛthā kṛsarasaṃyāvaṃ pāyasāpūpasaṃkulam  |
bhuktvā caivaṃ vidhaṃ tvannaṃ trirātreṇa viśuddhyati  || 2,33.21 ||
pītvā kṣīrāṇyapeyāni brahmacārī samāhitaḥ  |
gomūtrayāvakāhāro māsenaikena śuddhyati  || 2,33.22 ||
anirdaśāhaṃ gokṣīraṃ māhiṣaṃ cājameva ca  |
saṃdhinyāśca vivatsāyāḥ piban kṣīramidaṃ caret || 2,33.23 ||
eteṣāṃ ca vikārāṇi pītvā mohena mānavaḥ  |
gomūtrayāvakāhāraḥ saptarātreṇa śuddhyati  || 2,33.24 ||
bhuktvā caiva navaśrāddhe mṛtake sūtake tathā  |
cāndrāyaṇena śuddhyeta brāhmaṇastu samāhitaḥ  || 2,33.25 ||
yasyāgnau hūyate nityaṃ na yasyāgraṃ na dīyate  |
cāndrāyaṇaṃ caretsamyaktasyānnaprāśane dvijaḥ  || 2,33.26 ||
abhojyānāṃ tu sarveṣāṃ bhuktvā cānnamupaskṛtam  |
antāvasāyināṃ caiva taptakṛcchreṇa śuddhyati  || 2,33.27 ||
cāṇḍālānnaṃ dvijo bhuktvā samyakcāndrāyaṇaṃ caret |
buddhipūrvaṃ tu kṛcchrābdaṃ punaḥ saṃskārameva ca  || 2,33.28 ||
asurāmadyapānena kuryāccāndrāyaṇavratam  |
abhojyānnaṃ tu bhuktvā ca prājāpatyena śuddhyati  || 2,33.29 ||
viṇmūtrapāśanaṃ kṛtvā retasaścaitadācaret |
anādiṣṭeṣu caikāhaṃ sarvatra tu yathārthataḥ  || 2,33.30 ||
viḍvarāhakharoṣṭrāṇāṃ gomāyoḥ kapikākayoḥ  |
prāśya mūtrapurīṣāṇi dvijaścāndrāyaṇaṃ caret || 2,33.31 ||
ajñānātprāśya viṇmūtraṃ surāsaṃspṛṣṭameva ca  |
punaḥ saṃskāramarhanti trayo varṇā dvijātayaḥ  || 2,33.32 ||
kravyādāṃ pakṣiṇāṃ caiva prāśya mūtrapurīṣakam  |
mahāsāṃtapanaṃ mohāttathā kuryāddvijottamaḥ  |
bhāsamaṇḍūkakurare viṣkire kṛcchramācaret || 2,33.33 ||
prājāpatyena śuddhyeta brāhāmaṇocchiṣṭabhojane  |
kṣatriye taptakṛcchraṃ syādvaiśye caivātikṛcchrakam  |
śūdrocchiṣṭaṃ dvijo bhuktvā kuryāccāndrāyaṇavratam  || 2,33.34 ||
surābhāṇḍodare vāri pītvā cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret |
śunocchiṣṭaṃ dvijo bhuktvā trirātreṇa viśuddhyati  |
gomūtrayāvakāhāraḥ pītaśeṣaṃ ca rāgavān  || 2,33.35 ||
apo mūtrapurīṣādyairdūṣitāḥ prāśayedyadā  |
tadā sāṃtapanaṃ proktaṃ vrataṃ pāpaviśodhanam  || 2,33.36 ||
cāṇḍālakūpabhāṇḍeṣu yadi jñānātpibejjalam  |
caretsāṃtapanaṃ kṛcchraṃ brāhmaṇaḥ pāpaśodhanam  || 2,33.37 ||
cāṇḍālena tu saṃspṛṣṭaṃ pītvā vāri dvijottamaḥ  |
trirātreṇa viśuddhyeta pañcagavyena caiva hi  || 2,33.38 ||
mahāpātakisaṃsparśe bhuṅkte'snātvā dvijo yadi  |
buddhipūrvaṃ tu mūḍhātmā taptakṛcchraṃ samācaret || 2,33.39 ||
spṛṣṭvā mahāpātakinaṃ cāṇḍālaṃ vā rajasvalām  |
pramādādbhojanaṃ kṛtvā trirātreṇa viśuddhyati  || 2,33.40 ||
snānārhe yadi bhuñjīta ahorātreṇa śuddhyati  |
buddhipūrvaṃ tu kṛcchreṇa bhagavānāha padmajaḥ  || 2,33.41 ||
śuṣkaparyuṣitādīni gavādipratidūṣitam  |
bhuktvopavāsaṃ kurvota kṛcchrapādamathāpi vā  || 2,33.42 ||
saṃvatsarānte kṛcchraṃ tu caredvipraḥ punaḥ punaḥ  |
ajñātabhuktaśuddhyarthaṃ jñātasya tu viśeṣataḥ  || 2,33.43 ||
vrātyānāṃ yajanaṃ kṛtvā pareṣāmantyakarma ca  |
abhicāramahīnaṃ ca tribhiḥ kṛcchrairviśuddhyati  || 2,33.44 ||
brāhmaṇādihatānāṃ tu kṛtvā dāhādikāḥ kriyāḥ  |
gomūtrayāvakāhāraḥ prājāpatyena śuddhyati  || 2,33.45 ||
tailābhyakto'thavā kuryādyadi mūtrapurīṣake  |
ahorātreṇa śuddhyeta śmaśrukarma ca maithunam  || 2,33.46 ||
ekāhena vivāhāgniṃ parihārya dvijottamaḥ  |
trirātreṇa viśaddhyeta trirātrātṣaḍahaṃ punaḥ  || 2,33.47 ||
daśāhaṃ dvādaśāhaṃ vā parihārya pramādataḥ  |
kṛcchraṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ kuryāttatpāpasyāpanuttaye  || 2,33.48 ||
patitāddravyamādāya tadutsargeṇa śuddhyati  |
caretsāṃtapanaṃ kṛcchramityāha bhagavān prabhuḥ  || 2,33.49 ||
anāśakanivṛttāstu pravrajyāvasitāstathā  |
careyustrīṇi kṛcchrāṇi trīṇi cāndrāyaṇāni ca  || 2,33.50 ||
punaśca jātakarmādisaṃkāraiḥ saṃskṛtā dvijāḥ  |
śuddhyeyustadvrataṃ samyakcareyurdharmavardhanāḥ  || 2,33.51 ||
anupāsitasaṃdhyastu tadaharyāpako vaset |
anaśnan saṃyatamanā rātrau cedrātrimeva hi  || 2,33.52 ||
akṛtvā samidādhānaṃ śuciḥ snātvā samāhitaḥ  |
gāyatryaṣṭasahasrasya japyaṃ kuryādviśuddhaye  || 2,33.53 ||
upāsīta na cetsaṃdhyāṃ gṛhastho'pi pramādataḥ  |
snātvā viśuddhyate sadyaḥ pariśrāntastu saṃyamāt || 2,33.54 ||
vedoditāni nityāni karmāṇi ca vilopya tu  |
snātakavratalopaṃ tu kṛtvā copavaseddinam  || 2,33.55 ||
saṃvatsaraṃ caretkṛcchramagnyutsādī dvijottamaḥ  |
cāndrāyaṇaṃ caredvrātyo gopradānena śuddhyati  || 2,33.56 ||
nāstikyaṃ yadi kurvota prājāpatyaṃ careddvijaḥ  |
devadrohaṃ gurudrohaṃ taptakṛcchreṇa śuddhyati  || 2,33.57 ||
uṣṭrayānaṃ samāruhya kharayānaṃ ca kāmataḥ  |
trirātreṇa viśuddhyettu nagno vā praviśejjalam  || 2,33.58 ||
ṣaṣṭhānnakālatāmāsaṃ saṃhitājapa eva ca  |
homāśca śākalā nityamapāṅktānāṃ viśodhanam  || 2,33.59 ||
nīlaṃ raktaṃ vasitvā ca brāhmaṇo vastrameva hi  |
ahorātroṣitaḥ snātaḥ pañcagavyena śuddhyati  || 2,33.60 ||
vedadharmapurāṇānāṃ caṇḍālasya tu bhāṣaṇe  |
cāndrāyaṇena śuddhiḥ syānna hyanyā tasya niṣkṛtiḥ  || 2,33.61 ||
udbandhanādinihataṃ saṃspṛśya brāhmaṇaḥ kvacit |
cāndrāyaṇena śuddhiḥ syātprājāpatyena vā punaḥ  || 2,33.62 ||
ucchiṣṭo yadyanācāntaścāṇḍālādīn spṛśeddvijaḥ  |
pramādādvai japetsnātvā gāyatryaṣṭasahasrakam  || 2,33.63 ||
drupadānāṃ śataṃ vāpi brahmacārī samāhitaḥ  |
trirātropoṣitaḥ samyakpañcagavyena śuddhyati  || 2,33.64 ||
caṇḍālapatitādīṃstu kāmādyaḥ saṃspṛśeddvijaḥ  |
ucchiṣṭastatra kurvota prājāpatyaṃ viśuddhaye  || 2,33.65 ||
cāṇḍālasūtakaśavāṃstathā nārīṃ rajasvalām  |
spṛṣṭvā snāyādviśuddhyarthaṃ tatspṛṣṭaṃ patitiṃ tathā  || 2,33.66 ||
cāṇḍālasūtakaśavaiḥ saṃspṛṣṭaṃ saṃspṛśedyadi  |
pramādāttata ācamya japaṃ kuryātsamāhitaḥ  || 2,33.67 ||
tatspṛṣṭasparśinaṃ spṛṣṭvā buddhipūrvaṃ dvijottamaḥ  |
ācamettadviśuddhyarthaṃ prāha devaḥ pitāmahaḥ  || 2,33.68 ||
bhuñjānasya tu viprasya kadācitsaṃstravedgudam  |
kṛtvā śaucaṃ tataḥ snāyādupoṣya juhuyādghṛtam  || 2,33.69 ||
cāṇḍālāntyaśavaṃ spṛṣṭvā kṛcchraṃ kuryādviśuddhaye  |
spṛṣṭvābhyaktastvasaṃspṛśyamahorātreṇa śuddhyati  || 2,33.70 ||
surāṃ spṛṣṭvā dvijaḥ kuryātprāṇāyāmatrayaṃ śuciḥ  |
palāṇḍuṃ laśunaṃ caiva ghṛtaṃ prāśya tataḥ śuciḥ  || 2,33.71 ||
brāhmaṇastu śunā daṣṭastryahaṃ sāyaṃ payaḥ pibet |
nābherūrdhvaṃ tu daṣṭasya tadeva dviguṇaṃ bhavet || 2,33.72 ||
syādetattriguṇaṃ bāhvormūrdhni ca syāccaturguṇam  |
snātvā japedvā sāvitrīṃ śvabhirdaṣṭo dvijottamaḥ  || 2,33.73 ||
anirvartya mahāyajñān yo bhuṅkte tu dvijottamaḥ  |
anāturaḥ sati dhane kṛcchrārdhena sa śuddhyati  || 2,33.74 ||
āhitāgnirupasthānaṃ na kuryādyastu parvaṇi  |
ṛtau na gacchedbhāryāṃ vā so'pi kṛcchrārdhamācaret || 2,33.75 ||
vinādbhirapsu nāpyārtaḥ śarīraṃ sanniveśya ca  |
sacailo jalamāplutya gāmālabhya viśuddhyati  || 2,33.76 ||
buddhipūrvaṃ tvabhyudito japedantarjale dvijaḥ  |
gāyatryaṣṭasahasraṃ tu tryahaṃ copavasedvratī  || 2,33.77 ||
anugamyecchayā śūdraṃ pretībhūtaṃ dvijottamaḥ  |
gāyatryaṣṭasahasraṃ ca japyaṃ kuryānnadīṣu ca  || 2,33.78 ||
kṛtvā tu śapathaṃ vipro viprasya vadhasaṃyutam  |
mṛṣaiva yāvakānnena kuryāccāndrāyaṇaṃ vratam  || 2,33.79 ||
paṅktyāṃ viṣamadānaṃ tu kṛtvā kṛcchreṇa śuddhyati  |
chāyāṃ śvapākasyāruhya snātvā saṃprāśayedghṛtam  || 2,33.80 ||
īkṣedādityamaśucirdṛṣṭvāgniṃ candrameva vā  |
mānuṣaṃ cāsthi saṃspṛśya snānaṃ kṛtvā viśuddhyati  || 2,33.81 ||
kṛtvā tu mithyādhyayanaṃ caredbhaikṣaṃ tu vatsaram  |
kṛtaghno brāhmaṇagṛhe pañca saṃvatsaraṃ vratī  || 2,33.82 ||
huṅkāraṃ brāhmaṇasyoktvā tvaṅkāraṃ ca garīyasaḥ  |
snātvānaśnannahaḥ śeṣaṃ praṇipatya prasādayet || 2,33.83 ||
tāḍayitvā tṛṇenāpi kaṇṭhaṃ baddhvāpi vāsasā  |
vivāde vāpi nirjitya praṇipatya prasādayet || 2,33.84 ||
avagūrya caretkṛcchramatikṛcchraṃ nipātane  |
kṛcchrātikṛcchrau kurvota viprasyotpādya śoṇitam  || 2,33.85 ||
gurorākrośamanṛtaṃ kṛtvā kuryādviśodhanam  |
